> **★ PRIORITY NOTATION RULE (baked r1011; corrected r1014) — `X` (embedding size) vs `r` (areal radius); read each occurrence in context, never by pattern.**
> **⚠ `X` — SCOPE CORRECTED r1796 by ARC 13 station ④. The row below states the embedding reading as though it
> were corpus-wide. It is not: **P9 uses $X$ for something else entirely, in 20 of the corpus's 45 uses.***
> ***THE EMBEDDING MAGNITUDE*** — *P7, P3, P8, and p0's power-of-a-point ($|X|^{2}-\alpha^{2}$, Steiner's
> invariant); P5's throat seam at $X=\alpha$.* **The row below is correct for these and the warning it carries
> is the reason it exists.**
> ***THE KANTOWSKI–SACHS SCALE FUNCTION*** — **P9 ×20**, in $\dd s^{2}=-\dd\tau^{2}+X(\tau)^{2}\dd\chi^{2}+
> Y(\tau)^{2}\dd\Omega^{2}$, **paired with $Y$ and appearing as $X'/X$, $X''/X$ throughout the homogeneous
> class.** *Its vacuum kernel is $Y=r(\tau)$, $X=\sqrt{-f(r)}$ — **a metric function, not a position.***
> **⌗ Disjoint by paper, as with $\eta$ (r1795) — no paper uses both, and P9's is always $X(\tau)$ or $X'/X$,
> never bare $|X|$.** *But this row is the one carrying the drift warning, so a node reading it takes the
> embedding sense everywhere and is wrong in the paper that uses the symbol most.* **Read P9's $X$ as the scale
> function; read every other paper's as below.**
> **`X`** = the magnitude of the embedding position vector, `X=√(X₁²+X₂²+…)`, `=α` at the waist/throat: the throat's **3-sphere SIZE**, an embedding quantity. The bug that seeded the drift was writing this size as `r=α` — the *areal* symbol for the *embedding* size. **The throat's size is `X=α`.**
> **`r`** = the **signed areal radius**, a manifold coordinate that varies (through `r=0` onto the `r<0` branch). It takes genuine values on the manifold: the de Sitter horizon `r=α`, the branch point `r=0`, the collapse horizon `r=2M`.
> **The throat and `r` relate correctly through the slicing tangency.** The slicing curve — member = the sky angle `w`, *"the dial"* — **runs tangent to the throat at the areal radius `r₀`** (the slicing parameter / designated root); **`r₀=α/√3` for Nariai** (the merged double root). So *"at Nariai the slicing tangents the throat at `r=α/√3`" is a correct areal statement* (it is `r₀`, member-dependent), **not** a category error.
> **The only error is confusing the two:** writing the throat *size* as `r=α` (should be `X=α`), or treating a member's slicing-tangent value as the throat's fixed intrinsic radius. **For each occurrence ask: is it the embedding size (`X=α`), the areal radius the slicing reaches (`r`/`r₀`), the de Sitter horizon (`r=α`), or the merged horizon (`α/√3`)? — and fix per the definition it actually carries, never by pattern.** [→ §0: `X`, `r`, `r₀`, `throat/waist/equator`]


> ### ★ NAMING RULE FOR THE LAP'S LOCI (r2155) — never write the bare word "seam"
> *Four distinct loci, repeatedly conflated for ~2000 revisions. **Name the locus explicitly, every time.***
>
> | locus | position | what it is |
> |---|---|---|
> | **branch point** | $r=0$, close of the lift | the cosmological beginning; where the conjugate branch becomes the matter branch. ***NOT a seam.*** |
> | **turnaround** | $\|r\|=(2M\alpha^2)^{1/3}=3766.6$ Mpc | comoving turnaround, $1-f=0$; the collapse branch's floor |
> | **the lift** | turnaround $\to r=0$, length $\pi\alpha/3$ | Re$\,\tilde\tau$ frozen; carries the Euclidean null |
> | **the seam** | $r=-2\alpha/\sqrt3$ **and** $r=+\alpha/\sqrt3$ | ***ONE point of the substrate***: $\varphi=-240^\circ\equiv+120^\circ$ in $\varphi=2\pi r/\sqrt3\alpha$. The bead meets it going in ("back") and again one full lap later ("front"). The Nariai double root; $z\approx0.66$ on the far pass. |
>
> ### ★ THE RATE IS NAMED BY WHAT FIXES IT (r3401–r3404) — native parameters, and the translation
> *The cosmological rate is set by the substrate's geometry and by nothing else.* Its two parameters are
> **α = √(3/Λ)**, the substrate curvature radius — *an invariant length, not a mass* — and **x₀**, the offset of
> the cut. Neither is a content of the universe.
>
> $$c^2H^2(z)=\frac{c^2}{\alpha^2}\Big(1+\frac{2(1+z)^3}{x_0^{3}}\Big)$$
>
> **x₀ is measured directly and calibration-free**: z_acc = x₀ − 1 = 0.6648 ± 0.0467, without a distance
> ladder, without the microwave background, and without any density. What the rate is a rate *of* is **the
> stacking of the foliation**; content does not enter it, radiation being absent from the slicing operator's
> vacuum kernel (it requires m′(r) ≠ 0, a bend of the cut) and the second term being the offset itself.
>
> **THE TRANSLATION IS ONE-WAY.** Written in the fitted pair the same rate reads H² = H₀²[Ω_m(1+z)³ + Ω_Λ],
> with **Ω_m = 2/(x₀³+2)** and **Ω_Λ = x₀³/(x₀³+2)** following identically. *That is a translation between
> parameter sets, not a decomposition into components.* Reach for **x₀** and give Ω_m as its image; never the
> reverse. Ω_m is the offset, and Ω_{m,0} records the epoch τ̃₀ at which we observe rather than a density.
>
> **AND THE COINCIDENCE OF FORM IS NOT A COINCIDENCE OF ONTOLOGY.** In the standard reading cosmic time is
> orthogonal to the three-dimensional world and the expansion is *driven* — by initial conditions and by
> whatever densities obtain at each moment, through the field equations. Here the rate is **read off a fixed
> background**, whose foliation is **not orthogonal** to the proper time of observers at rest, so those
> observers move through the universe over cosmic time in a geometrically fixed manner. **The two agree on the
> function and share nothing beneath it.**
>
> **WHERE THE FIELD EQUATIONS ACT: the leaf, and there in full.** All stress-energy gravitates, by the ordinary
> Friedmann readout of the self-gravitating congruence. The perturbations, the plasma's sound horizon and its
> diffusion length run *in* the content and take that rate; a comoving separation read across leaves takes the
> stacking rate. **What is kept apart is not two physics but one physics and its projection** — carried on the
> layer, then projected, never the projection's machinery run as the existent (P7 `sec:CR-FLRW`).
>
> **⚠ "radiation-free rate" is retired, corpus-wide, and occurs zero times (r3401–r3404).** It named the rate by
> a component it lacks, which presupposes the rate is built from components at all. Say **the geometric rate**,
> **the stacking rate**, or **fixed by α and x₀**. The failure it invited is on record and is worth knowing:
> *radiation-free → ΛCDM minus a term → the remaining term is matter → content sets the rate → the constraint
> and the rate share a ρ_tot.* P7 `sec:CR-FLRW` names it in advance — *"computations imported wholesale into CR
> silently re-impose the synchronous identification, collapsing the non-synchronous ontology back onto its own
> shadow and returning a straw cosmology"* — together with its inverse, *"mistaking the layer's ontological rate
> for the appearance and refusing it its physics."*

> ### ★ PROTECTED TERM (r3389) — "branch point" means $r=0$, and nothing else
> *The word carried three senses across sixteen papers and ~278 occurrences, and one of them named the very
> locus $r=0$ has to be told apart from. It produced flat contradictions **inside single papers**: P5 said both

> point" against its own masthead's "**NOT** the branch point, and NOT cosmogenesis, which is realised at $r=0$,
> a lap away". Cleared corpus-wide at r3386–r3388.*
>

> |---|---|---|

> | the join of P3's spherical and de Sitter pieces at $\theta=\pi/2$, $r=1$, where the parameter turns off the real axis | **the join** | ~~branch point~~ |

> **"Nariai point" is not an import — it is P5's own word**, already used at every one of its sites ("the two
> Nariai points where two roots collide", "the discriminant changes sign across each Nariai point").
>
> **Cleared everywhere, including the receipts (r3390).** A record that quotes a claim the corpus no longer
> makes is not a record; it is a live falsehood with a date on it. Twelve receipt sites were corrected, one of
> which was doubly stale — it quoted P5's *"the **deck** group is $S_3$"* when P5 says the **monodromy** group
> is $S_3$ and the deck group is trivial.
>
> **Retained, and deliberately:** *branched at Nariai*, *branched cover*, *branch set*, *branch convention*,
> *algebraic branch point* (of a meromorphic invariant). **None is the protected noun naming a locus of the
> lap**, and each states its own referent, so none can be misread. The rule is about the noun phrase **"the
> branch point"** standing alone.
>
> **⚠ The member is not the locus.** A recurring error distinct from the term: *"the cosmogenesis branch point

> is the $r=0$ locus alone, one point per worldline — the labelling is what distinguishes it.** Calling the

> **When speaking of the substrate or the slicing curve, say *"the seam (one point, met a lap apart)"* — front and

>

> *The collapse side is **two thirds** of the excursion. Any phrasing that makes the seam an "endpoint" demotes it.*

**What this is.** A working index for one operation: replacing every note that says *"the ontology is
unassigned"* with a flag that says **the ontology is set by the corpus's load-bearing pieces** — and
then, paper by paper, filling each flag with the specific, interrogated justification for *why* the
corpus sets it that way. The end state is a conservatively and objectively reduced ontological
foundation baked into a mathematical framework as something that **requires load-bearing pieces**, not
"Daryl says so." We remove Daryl from the mechanism and leave behind Daryl's gift: the formally reduced
pieces that shake out of what objective analysis — of the world, and of how we learn — has established.

**Status (r320).** The principle below now has its first fully worked instance: the SO(6)-face ontology
adjudication (the colour frontier's compact face). It is **baked into the corpus** — colour-closure
decoupled onto P1/P7, and the compact face's status (real-by-construction, *not* a co-equal **existent**;
the "two co-equal real forms" reading closed **ontologically, not structurally**) written into **P14**
(`boundary_paper.tex` §ontology, r319); the discrete-structure classification into **P4** (r317). The
existence-criterion the adjudication turns on is grounded not as a posit but as an **empirical
determination**: the absolute cosmic time is fixed by the CMB redshift-isotropy (P4 `modern_parallax` / BST §4.3.2 /
Fortress — "ontology from evidence," the differential alternative excluded by ~3 orders). **Recorded (r324):** the adjudication is now written here as §1a, after P14's fresh different-node cold
read cleared it (r323); this record rides its own fresh cold read in turn. The principle this index states — the gate reads and holds
the ontology from the load-bearing pieces, never "because Daryl says" — is exactly what the adjudication enacted.

## ⌗ THE CROSS-PAPER JOINS — claims no single paper owns, added r3591 by 59 from the field-bake gathers

> **▣ WHY THEY ARE HERE AND NOT IN A PAPER.** *Each of these says how two or more corpus pieces stand to one
> another. **Both halves of every one are already in the corpus, in their own papers, correctly**; what is
> absent is the sentence identifying them — and a paper that stated it would be claiming a relation it does
> not own. `K4`'s span was landed here at `r1895` for exactly this reason and is the precedent.*
> ⌗ ***The failure this prevents is specific: the r3164 Cartan bake computed one holonomy, treated holonomy
> as one topic, and mistook a second object for an opening in the first.***

### ⛔⛭ A THEOREM USED WITHOUT ITS HYPOTHESES AUDITED — added r3701 by 59, from 60's v2 field passes

***Two independent v2 passes, in different fields, found the same shape: a result the corpus states
CORRECTLY, resting on a named theorem whose HYPOTHESES the corpus never checks. In both, the conclusion
holds — and in both, the check was cheap and nobody had run it.***

| where | the theorem | the hypothesis nobody audited | the finding |
|---|---|---|---|
| **`P05` `rem:galois`** — number theory, `T1` | *a cubic's Galois group is $S_3$ when its discriminant is not a square* | ***irreducibility*** | *the inference is **invalid** without it. Control: $r^3-r^2+r-1$ factors as $(r-1)(r^2+1)$, discriminant $-16$, not a square, Galois group $\mathbb{Z}_2$. **The horizon cubic IS irreducible over $\mathbb{C}(M)$, so the conclusion stands*** |
| **`P05` `prop:monodromy`** — numerical analysis, `Q51` | *the two branch-point transpositions differ and generate $S_3$* | ***the loop's radius*** | *two preconditions the sentence does not state, **both failing SILENTLY**: a loop wider than the branch-point separation $4/(3\sqrt3)=0.7698$ returns the **3-cycle** — a correct monodromy of a different loop* |

⇒ ***AND THE CONSEQUENCE IS THE SAME IN BOTH: a downstream reader cannot tell a load-bearing citation from
an ornamental one.*** *`T1` matters because **three earlier bakes — algebraic geometry, combinatorics,
complex analysis — all USE the $S_3$ result and none audits it.** `Q51` matters because the failure is
silent: the wrong loop returns a valid-looking answer.*

⌗ ***BOTH ARE IN `P05`, WHICH IS NOT A COINCIDENCE WORTH INFLATING BUT IS WORTH RECORDING***: *that paper
carries the corpus's densest concentration of imported theorems, so it is where the class is most likely to
recur. **Neither finding is a defect in `P05`'s results** — both conclusions hold. The absence is a stated
hypothesis, in a paper whose other arguments state theirs.*

⌗ *Related in kind and already held elsewhere: `D1` (index theory) found `P14`'s Atiyah--Singer citation
**doing no work in the count** — the analytical index is computed by definition and the theorem supplies
only deformation invariance. **That is the same question asked of a citation rather than of a hypothesis,
and it came out the other way: honest, not load-bearing.***

### ⛭ TWO HOLONOMIES, NOT ONE — `C10`, Cartan/holonomy bake

| bundle | order | group | its work |
|---|---|---|---|
| the **residue pairing** — `P05`, `P07` | **24** | $S_4=W(A_3)=W(\mathfrak{so}(6,\mathbb{C}))$ | closes the substrate's Weyl group |
| the **wall monodromies** with the hinge 3-cycle — `P14` | **81** | $3^4$, a finite 3-group | supplies colour's selection rules |

⇒ ***Different groups, different bases, different work, sharing only a factor of three.*** *Both are in the
corpus and no paper says they are two. **The bake that treated them as one computed the first and read the
second as an opening in it**, which is the concrete cost of the missing sentence.*

### ⛭⛭ ONE BOUNDEDNESS ARGUMENT, FOUR SECTORS — `F18`/`F19`, functional-analysis bake; joined r3746 by 60

> ⌗ *The `F15` row below was landed at r3591; **this one was recorded in the same ledger row as landed and
> was not** — the row named both and only the compactness join reached the map. Placed here now, on the
> same rule: **a claim about how corpus pieces relate is owned by the map, not by a paper.***

| sector | the risk | what the construction supplies | what survives |
|---|---|---|---|
| **quantum** — `P10`, the Euclidean kernel | an unbounded-below Hamiltonian makes $K=e^{-\hat H\lvert\Delta\eta\rvert}$ **diverge** | *"mode by mode a harmonic oscillator, whose Hamiltonian is **bounded below**"* — no runaway direction to diverge along | **the kernel exists** |
| **cosmological** — `P15`, the onset datum | *"a beginning at genuinely **unbounded** curvature places no finite floor under $z_{\rm onset}$ … and the single datum ceases to be a datum at all"* | the scale is set by $\alpha$ everywhere, so the divergence is **the areal coordinate degenerating** and not a scale of the geometry | **$\rho_r/\rho_m$ stays a DATUM** |
| **fermionic** — `P14`, the Dirac index *(via `F14`)* | infinite leaf length $\Rightarrow$ not Fredholm $\Rightarrow$ **index undefined** | **finite** proper length in $\dd\ell=\dd r/\sqrt{\lvert f\rvert}$, through the Nariai limit | **the index equals THREE** |
| **radiative** — `P11`, the TT oscillator | ghost, tachyon or **runaway** in the propagating mode | $Q$ bounded: its super-horizon branches go as $a^{-3}$ and as a **constant** | **a healthy massless de Sitter scalar** |

⇒ ***ONE FORM, FOUR TIMES: an unbounded or infinite structure would destroy a well-defined quantity; the
construction supplies the bound; the quantity survives.***

⛭⛭ ***AND THE SAME SCALE DOES THE BOUNDING IN ALL FOUR.*** *$\alpha$ bounds the substrate's curvature
(`P15`, explicitly), fixes the interval the areal radius runs on (`P10`), makes $f$'s zeros simple on a
bounded interval (`P14`/`F14`), and sets the de Sitter rate whose $a$ the `P11` branches are read against.*
⇒ **Not merely analogous — the same scale discharging the same duty in four sectors.**

⛔ ***AND NO PAPER JOINS THEM.*** *`P10` argues its boundedness against the conformal-factor problem, `P15`
against the $z_{\rm onset}$ limit, `P14`'s is established only through `F14`, and `P11` states its own in
its own words with `bounded below` ×0.* ⌗ *Each is correct where it stands; what is absent is the sentence
identifying them — which is why it is here and not in one of the four.*

⌗ ***THE FOURTH INSTANCE ALSO CARRIED A MISATTRIBUTION, NOW CORRECTED IN THE PAPER.*** *`P11` said $Q$
decays as $a^{-2}$; the $a^{-2}$ belongs to $W=a\,\delta\psi$, the rescaled variable the equation is
written in. **The paper's conclusion was unaffected** — $Q$ is bounded either way — and the sentence is
fixed at r3746 with its own receipt. *That is what a join is worth: setting four instances side by side is
what exposed the one that was stated wrong.**

### ⛭ THREE COMPACTNESS STATUSES ON THREE SPACES — `F15`, functional-analysis bake

| space | status | why | where |
|---|---|---|---|
| the **substrate** $\mathrm{dS}_5=SO(5,1)/SO(4,1)$ | **not compact** | topologically $\mathbb{R}\times S^4$ | `P06` |
| the **Wick face** | **compact** | the signature change | `P13` |
| the **leaf** | **compact, and closed** | the slicing | `P14` |

⇒ ***`P13` names the Atiyah–Hirzebruch obstruction's load-bearing hypotheses as "compactness and a continuous
isometry", and the three spaces answer that hypothesis differently.*** *Set side by side the pattern is the
argument: **the obstruction bites where the space is compact and the isometry continuous, and the corpus's own
opening is exactly where one of those fails.** No paper sets them side by side.

### ⛭ ONE FUNCTOR, THREE PROPERTIES, THREE PAPERS — `K2`, category bake

*$\Psi$ taking a vantage to the geometry it describes is a functor, and its three functorial properties are
not three facts about it but **three companion papers**: what it hits is `P09`'s range theorem, what it
identifies is the quotient `P05` computes, and where it fails to be faithful is the designation freedom `P03`
carries.* ⌗ ⚠ ***And the multiplicity is dimension-conditioned***: *"three cuts, one geometry" is a fibre
multiplicity of $D-1$ — four-to-one at $D=5$, and **from $D=6$ not a finite multiplicity at all**, because
there is no fold to read. **A dimension-conditioned multiplicity is new content rather than a renaming**, so
that bake's own "census pass — zero" close is reopenable; it is recorded as reopenable and not reopened.*

### ⛭ TWO SPECTRAL GAPS, TWO JOBS — `S3`, spectral bake

*The tower's lowest frequency is $\mu_2=\sqrt6$ with no zero mode and no soft region, so **the infrared is
regulated for free while the ultraviolet is untouched**: `P07`'s own words are that this construction "has no
infrared problem to solve and an ultraviolet problem every interacting field theory has". **That IR regulation
is a spectral gap.*** *The wall's gap — the full asymptotic mass separating its bound modes from the continuum,
landed in `P14` at `r3545` — is a second one, doing a different job: it makes the generation count **stable**
rather than merely defined.* ⇒ ***Two gaps, two jobs, and neither paper references the other.***

### ⛭ NARIAI AND PETROV TYPE D ARE ONE ALGEBRAIC EVENT — `S4`, spectral bake

*Both are a discriminant vanishing: the horizon cubic's at Nariai, and the speciality invariant $I^3-27J^2$ at
Type D. **Two cubics, two discriminants, one kind of event** — `P09` carries the speciality invariant and
eleven papers carry the double root, and the identification is in neither.*

### ⛭ THE CYCLOID IS BAND-LIMITED, AND THAT FORCES THE CLAIM `P02` ARGUES FOR — `H21`, harmonic bake

*$r(z)=M(1+\cos z)$ read on $\mathbb{R}/2\pi\mathbb{Z}$ has Fourier content of exactly two terms, a constant
and **one** harmonic: **band-limited to $\lvert k\rvert\le1$**, the simplest non-constant periodic function
there is. **A single harmonic has exactly two critical points and they are of identical character by
construction** — which is `P02`'s central structural claim, reached from the other side.* ⌗ *`P02` proves it
from $r''=-(r-M)$ and `r3583` added the Thales reading; the harmonic reading is a third route to the same
place, and three independent routes to one claim is worth recording as such.*

## 0. GLOSSARY — load-bearing geometric terms (hold these exactly)

> **⛭ THE CORPUS-WIDE CANON — symbols as well as terms.** A symbol or term used in more than one paper is
> defined here, once, and the papers use it as defined. **The ledgers (§1t, §1u, §1w) report; this defines.**
> Anything definitional that turns up while filling a ledger belongs here.
>
> *This document holds definitions, not their history. Where a convention has been unsettled, is being
> settled, or could drift, that is carried by `CONSOLIDATE` **ARC 9 · the canon layer**, which holds the
> instances, the patterns and the discharge plan.*
>
> **Where a term's meaning is general but its value depends on the geometry, both are given** — the definition,
> then the value per member. Quote a name with its geometry, or quote the value.

### ★★★ THE ONTOLOGY — what each object IS. **State it in these words. Every document that touches the geometry names the rung it means.**

***This is the programme's positive statement of its own objects. It is not a correction and not a warning: it is
what the things are, in the words to use for them. A document that touches the geometry pulls this in explicitly
— it does not assume it, and it does not rely on the reader reconstructing it.***

| # | the object | what it IS | the word for it | who owns it |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **①** | **$\mathrm{dS}_5=SO(5,1)/SO(4,1)$** — a hyperboloid in flat ambient $M^6$, isometry $\mathfrak{so}(5,1)$, sole scale $\alpha=\sqrt{3/\Lambda}$ | ***THE SUBSTRATE.*** **The thing that is SLICED.** Maximal symmetry complete and exhausted. **Everywhere intrinsically real**, its Lorentzian signature intrinsic to its positive curvature; the imaginary variables the construction reaches it through are **instruments over an everywhere-real geometry** | **"the substrate"** — *reserved for this and nothing else* | **p0** (`geometric_core_paper`), with P12 for the base and the forcing |
| **②** | **A CUT of the substrate** — a four-geometry. **$\mathrm{dS}_4$ is one such**: the maximally symmetric one, the empty cosmology, the clock on $\Lambda$ alone | ***THE BACKGROUND*** (when it is $\mathrm{dS}_4$), or ***a cut*** / ***a spacetime geometry*** generally. **The thing that is REASSIGNED.** A geometry is a cut exactly when its isometry group contains a sweep-subgroup of the substrate's | **"the $\mathrm{dS}_4$ background"** · **"a cut"** — ***qualified, always.*** *Bare "background" is not available: the corpus uses it in four senses — this rung, the CMB/radiation background, a background to compute a field on, and the perturbation-theory background (the monopole).* **Never "the substrate"** | **P8** (the operator), **P9** (the range), **P7** (the reassignment) |
| **③** | **Schwarzschild, Nariai, the Friedmann geometries** | ***THE EXACT SOLUTIONS.*** Each a **symmetry-broken cut**; **matter is the bend of the cut off the vacuum profile** | **"the exact solutions"** | **P8**, **P9**, **P3** |
| **④** | **$\mathcal S_t$ — a smooth THREE-dimensional Riemannian manifold, $(\mathcal S_t, h_t)$, advancing in cosmic time $t$** | ***THE ONTOLOGICAL SPATIAL LAYER. THE EXISTENT. THE UNIVERSE.*** What exists and advances. **The lapse is its metrical rate of advance, $d\tau=N\,dt$** | **"the layer"** · **"the existent"** | **P7**, axiomatically |
| **⑤** | **$(M,g)$ — a four-dimensional Lorentzian manifold; $\Sigma_t\subset M$ its spacelike hypersurfaces.** ⌗ **AND THE FOUR IS NOW DERIVED, r2376+c54.10 — it had been a stipulation.** *The matter sector's fold is $D-1$; the horizon relation collapses to a single multiple-angle only at $D=4,5$; and the mass-parity grading chirality exists only at even $D$ ⇒ **four is the only dimension carrying both a generation count and a chirality**. **This settles rung ⑤ and rung ②, NOT rung ①** — every constraint on the substrate is still a lower bound (see the fifth-dimension note below). Sources: P3 `rem:dimension`, P14 `sec:scope`* | ***SPACETIME. THE REPRESENTATION.*** **NOT an existent.** *"$\Sigma_t$ **represents** $\mathcal S_t$ but does not **constitute** it."* Granting it existence is the category error the problem of time, the hole argument and CTCs all descend from | **"spacetime"** · **"the projection"** · **"the representation"** | **P7**, axiomatically |

**⛭ WHICH WORDS ARE RESERVED, AND WHICH MUST BE QUALIFIED.** ***`substrate` is reserved: it names rung ① and
nothing else.*** *It is a term of art here and the corpus uses it that way throughout.* **`background`,
`geometry` and `ontology` are the field's ordinary words and cannot be reserved** — `background` carries four
senses here, `geometry` and `ontology` far more. ***So rung ② is named `the $\mathrm{dS}_4$ background` or `a
cut`, qualified every time; and rungs ③–⑤ are named by their phrases — `the exact solutions`, `the layer`, `the
existent`, `spacetime`, `the projection` — never by a bare common noun.***

**⛭ THE FIFTH DIMENSION IS FORCED, and this is why the ladder has the shape it has:** ***"the construction
generates many distinct four-geometries from one substrate, and slicing a four-dimensional de Sitter space only
re-coordinatizes it."*** *Slicing is codimension-one and dimension-reducing. **A four-dimensional substrate would
generate nothing.***

**⛭ AND THE STATEMENT ABOVE IS A FLOOR, NOT A CEILING — corrected r2376+c54.6, and do not let the two
dimensional facts fuse.** *"Slicing $\mathrm{dS}_4$ only re-coordinatizes it" excludes $\mathrm{dS}_4$ and
yields $D\ge5$; **no upper bound is established anywhere in the corpus**, and least-arbitrariness cannot supply
one ($\mathrm{dS}_D$ is moduli-free for every $D$, so the criterion selects the manifold **at fixed
dimension**).* **Two different objects, and the canon depends on keeping them apart:**

| | the object | its dimension | status |
|---|---|---|---|
| **rung ①** | **the substrate** | $D\ge5$ | ***bounded below only.*** *A ceiling here would set a feature by hand — Rule 3's maximally-arbitrary move* |
| **rungs ②/⑤** | **the cut, and the spacetime it represents** | $=4$ | ***DERIVED r2376+c54.10*** *from the matter sector, not from the symmetry* |

***A document that reads the second as capping the first has re-made the error c54.6 exists to correct.***

**⛭ AND ONE RESULT THAT BELONGS TO RUNG ② AND IS EASILY ATTACHED TO RUNG ①.** *The thesis maximal-symmetry
result — de~Sitter the unique real manifold whose Lorentzian signature is intrinsic to its positive curvature —
**is stated for the four-dimensional case.** It is a result about **the background**, and the property it
establishes is **the substrate's**. **P8's embedding $-X_0^2+X_1^2+\dots+X_4^2=\alpha^2$ is likewise rung ②**,
used where pure de~Sitter's own slicings are at issue. ***p0 and P3 and P7 now say so in the rung's own words where the maximal-symmetry result stands; P8 says it in its own — it names its object "pure de~Sitter", which IS rung ②, and needs no further marker*** *(checked r1879; an earlier note claimed all three said it in the same words, which overstated)*.

**⛭ AND THE SWEEP'S NEGATIVE RESULT IS THE MORE USEFUL HALF: the other sixteen do NOT qualify.**
> ⛔⛭⛭ ***THE `wall` CLAUSE BELOW IS SUPERSEDED BY THE `wall` ①②③ ROWS ABOVE — noted r3680, and the
> contradiction stood in one file.*** *59 landed `wall` as **three objects** at r3650 (×171 obstruction, ×57 locus, ×47 field configuration) and this sentence, written by the earlier sweep, still offers `wall` as its example of a word that does **not** qualify.* ⇒ **A canon that contradicts itself in one file is worse than one that is silent, because both halves read as measured.** *Annotated rather than deleted: the sweep's reasoning is the record, and what changed is that somebody read the sites.*

*`wall` is one
sense across sixteen papers — the loss of the last continuous symmetry — *(P1's "a row of runners aimed at a wall"
is a simile; P4's void/wall contrast is standard cosmology jargon)*. `layer` is one sense across fifteen — **the
ontological layer, the existent that advances**. `face` has a metaphorical sense (*"a face of a single de Sitter
determination"*) and a technical one, **but the technical is ALWAYS qualified** — *compact face*, *Wick face*,
*SO(6) face* — **so it never appears bare in the technical sense.** `seam`, `cut`, `leaf`, `throat`, `ruling`,
`bead`, `hinge` are each single-sensed and heavily used.*
***So the corpus's ordinary vocabulary is clean, and `branch` is the exception rather than the first of a family.***
**That is worth stating because the opposite finding would have meant a much larger job.**

### ⛭ THE EPISTEMIC TERMS — added r1803 by ARC 13 station ⑪. **§0 says "symbols as well as TERMS" and had no row for a single one of the five that govern what every other row is worth.**
*P6 has zero computations, so the symbol question does not apply to it. **It is the terms paper**, and its terms
are the ones that license the word *forced* everywhere else — its own masthead: **"it licenses the word 'forced'
everywhere in the corpus, and fixes the ALTITUDE at which each forcing is held."***

| term | what it means, at source | where |
|---|---|---|
| **THE CRITERION OF NECESSITY** | **R2 of P6's four rules: *prefer the world that REQUIRES the phenomena over the one that merely PERMITS them through adjustable parameters*** — and P6 calls it **"the load-bearing one."** *This is what `prop:forced`, `A3.3`'s admits-not-forces, and `A5.1`'s free-data reading all rest on (r1782)* | 8 papers |
| **LEAST-ARBITRARINESS** | **R2 in the ONTOLOGICAL register**: *a structure with an unforced modulus is a **family** $\{W_\lambda\}$, not a world; **maximal symmetry is the unique moduli-free choice.*** ***This licenses the corpus's foundational SELECTION of the substrate*** | p0, P14, P6 |
| **ALTITUDE** | **the height at which a forcing is held** — not whether a claim is true but **what kind of warrant it has**. Every *"forced within CR"* sits at one | 10 papers |
| **RULE-FAVOURED ALTITUDE** | the specific altitude the corpus's results occupy: ***"every 'forced within CR' is a coherence claim at rule-favoured altitude, awaiting the world's non-local discriminator — never correspondence"*** | P6 |
| **COHERENCE vs CORRESPONDENCE** | the distinction the altitude is measured against. **`lem:vindication` is built from successes — *survivorship, not measurement* — "a compelling hypothesis with confirming instances, not a calibrated reliability."** Sampling where the rules MISLED (ether, caloric, Kepler's solids) and computing a base rate is **the discipline's FIRST PROGRAMME, not presumed** | 16 papers |
**⛭ WHY THESE BELONG IN A SYMBOL CANON: because every other row here is a NOUN, and these five say what the
nouns are WORTH.** *A node can read all 31 symbol rows, use each correctly, and still not know that **"forced"
means forced-within-CR at rule-favoured altitude** rather than forced simpliciter. ***P6 §place fixes the spine
and it is worth carrying whole: "P1 needs no epistemic ground; P4 is where the programme first leans on the
method; p0's unification is held at coherence-not-correspondence; P16 draws R2 at its sharpest."****

it `\emph{within-state} index` — **with markup between the two words the search needed adjacent**.* ⇒ *De-
emphasised, the label sense runs **×22 across seven papers**. **A markup-blind search over LaTeX is the
`\rcpt`-buried-in-a-comment failure wearing new clothes**, and it very nearly shipped an "and nowhere else"
into this table.*

present and always different — `Killing form` against `Killing vector`. **Here ① and ③ share the bare word
and ② differs only by a hyphenated adjective a reader may take for emphasis.***

"index-$n$ subgroup". *Measured at r3610 by `\rcpt{D2_index_carries_three_senses_inside_one_paper}`, whose control

### ⛭ `conserved charge` — added r3608 by 60, from the integrable-systems bake's `I7`. **×15 across nine papers, three objects — and one of them is a NEGATIVE EXISTENCE CLAIM about the other two.**

| reading | what it names | where | count |
|---|---|---|---|
| `conserved charge` **A** | **THE ASYMPTOTIC MASS CHARGE** — *an ADM / Abbott--Deser class boundary integral at infinity.* ⛔ ***Every single occurrence is a DENIAL***: "a conserved charge in an asymptotically-de~Sitter spacetime is widely held not to be well defined" | **`P02` · `P03` · `P08` · `P10` · `P12`** | **×5** |
| `conserved charge` **B** | **A FIRST INTEGRAL OF A FLOW, or a conserved charge of a field** — *the homothety's charge on the null cone; the twist $c$ whose sign IS the graviton's handedness; the shear charge whose decay is the no-hair law* | **`P11` ×5 · `P05` · `P07`** | **×7** |
| `conserved charge` **C** | **A CONSERVED QUANTUM NUMBER** — *the progenitor charge a derivation of the baryon asymmetry $\eta$ would have to reach* | **`P16` ×2 · `P07`** | **×3** |

**⛭ ⛔ THIS ROW CLEARS THE `integrable` BAR AND THEN EXCEEDS IT, AND THE DIFFERENCE IS THE REASON IT IS HERE.**
*`integrable` ① and ⑥ are opposite in **consequence** — a singularity a measure survives against a system whose
motion is solvable.* ⇒ ***`conserved charge` A and B are opposite in EXISTENCE.*** *A does not merely name a
different object from B; **it asserts that the object B names is not well defined in this spacetime class** —
and the corpus is asymptotically de~Sitter throughout.* ⌗ **So a reader who carries A's verdict into `P11`'s
sentence concludes the graviton's handedness is not well defined, which is the opposite of what `P11` proves.**

**⛭ AND THE COLLISION IS INSIDE ONE PAPER, which is the bar `I0` set for a row.** *`P07` carries **C** at
character 134,508 and **B** at 307,922 — 173,414 apart in one paper, so a reader meets them separately and
has nothing in either sentence to tell them the phrase has changed object.*
⌗ ⚠ ***AND THE FIVE A-SITES ARE NOT A DISAGREEMENT.*** *All five papers say the same true thing, and the row
does not ask any of them to change it. **What is missing is anywhere that says the three are three.***

⇒ **THE RULE, and it is the `z` rule sharpened:** *`conserved charge` is never to be written unqualified where
the spacetime class is in view. **A** takes "asymptotic"; **B** takes "first integral" or names its flow;
**C** takes "quantum number". *Measured at r3608 by `\rcpt{I7_conserved_charge_carries_three_objects_and_one_denies_the_others}`, whose control runs the same
classifier over `first integral` and gets one sense back — the line that would have caught a classifier
inventing the split.*

### ⛭ `wall` — added r3650 by 59, from the integrable-systems bake's `I18`. **509 live occurrences, three objects.**

| reading | what it names | where | count |
|---|---|---|---|
| `wall` ① | **THE CHIRALITY WALL** — *an **obstruction**: the Atiyah–Hirzebruch index argument closing the geometric route to chiral matter, whose load-bearing hypotheses are compactness and a continuous isometry* | **`P13` · `P14`** | **×171** |
| `wall` ② | **THE RANGE WALL** — *a **boundary of the construction**: the Type-N edge of the stratification, where continuous symmetry is lost and free gravitational radiation begins* | **`P09` · `P11` · `P12`** | **×57** |
| `wall` ③ | **A DOMAIN WALL** — *a **field configuration**: the three walls carrying the generations, with $\sigma_y$ eigenvalues for chiralities* | **`P14`** | **×47** |
| `monodromy` ① ⚑ ***entered r3680, from the index-theory v2 pass*** | **THE COVERING-SPACE MONODROMY** — *a **permutation of roots**: the horizon cubic's three sheets over the $2M$-plane, branched at Nariai, monodromy group $S_3$ and **deck group trivial**; a finite group acting on a fibre* | **`P05` ×44** · `P07` ×5 · `P03` | **×92 total** |
| `monodromy` ② | **THE LINEAR-ODE (FUCHSIAN) MONODROMY** — *a **matrix of analytic continuation**: the perturbation equation's solution carried around a regular singular point, **unipotent** with off-diagonal $2\pi ip$, the scalar's $-4\pi i/\rho$ twice the tensor's; an infinite-order matrix, not a permutation* | **`P16` ×9** | ⌗ *and `P14`'s mode monodromy at the $r=0$ wall is of this kind* |
| ⛔ **AND `P14` CARRIES BOTH** | *① at L82/L189 — "the Weyl $S_3$ is the deck/monodromy symmetry of the solution space", used to make flavour **global** — and ② at L176, `P14_mode_monodromy_at_the_wall`, the spinor mode's single-valuedness around a loop.* ⇒ ***One paper, one word, two objects: a finite permutation group and a unipotent matrix, which are not the same mathematics.*** | **`P14` ×14** | ⌗ *both senses landed in `COMPLEX_ANALYSIS_LEDGER`; neither was distinguished from the other* |
| — | *ordinary prose, figure captions, and compounds* | scattered | remainder |

**⛭ ⛔ THEY ARE THREE KINDS OF THING, NOT THREE INSTANCES OF ONE.** *① is a **theorem** that forbids;
② is a **locus** in a parameter space; ③ is a **solution** of a field equation. **The corpus's own
sentences distinguish them and the word does not.***

**⛭ AND `P14` CARRIES ① AND ③ WITHIN ONE SECTION.** *`sec:chirality` opens "on the slicing curve … this
sector's **chiral wall**" (①) and the same paper's count section reads "that holonomy acts on the
**walls'** chiralities … all three **walls** at one chirality" (③). ⇒ ***A reader who carries the
obstruction sense into the configuration sense reads "three walls" as three obstructions.*** *That is the
`kernel` shape again (§0, `r3579`), and the `z` rule does not save it: **`P14` uses two.***

⌗ *Recorded, **not** unified. All three are load-bearing and each is standard in its own literature, so
this falls under the $\gamma$ rule (r1797) exactly as `kernel` and $\mathcal{H}$ do.*

### ⛭ `kernel` — added r3579 by 59, from the functional-analysis bake's `F13`. **153 live occurrences, four readings, and two of them are opposite objects in the field the word belongs to.**

| reading | what it names | where | count |
|---|---|---|---|
| `kernel` ① | **THE VACUUM KERNEL** — *the solution set of $T_{\mu\nu}=0$ for a symmetry class; the metric functions the class admits before matter bends them.* **The corpus's own coinage and its dominant sense** | **P9 ×34 · P8 ×19 · P7 ×21** | **×62** |
| `kernel` ② | **THE KERNEL OF AN OPERATOR** — *the null space, $\ker$, in the ordinary linear-algebraic sense; `dim ker` in P14's index* | **P14 · P12 · P9** | **×13** |
| `kernel` ③ | **AN INTEGRAL KERNEL** — *the Euclidean propagator $e^{-\hat H\lvert\Delta\eta\rvert}$ of the branch-point filter, a function of two arguments that one integrates against* | **P10 · P15 · P7** | **×6** |
| `kernel` ④ | **loose, in prose** — *"the kernel of the argument", the ordinary English sense* | scattered | remainder |

**⛭ ⛔ ① AND ② ARE OPPOSITE OBJECTS IN THIS FIELD, WHICH IS WHY THE ROW EXISTS.** *A functional analyst reads
`kernel` as ② without hesitation — the set a map sends to zero. The corpus's ① is the set a map sends to zero
**of a different map**: not of the slicing operator but of the Einstein tensor, so it is the operator's
**image** that ① describes and its **kernel** only by a second construction.* ⇒ ***A reader of that field
meeting "the vacuum kernel" in P9 will import the wrong object, and nothing in the sentence corrects them.***

**⛭ AND THEY ARE NOT DISJOINT BY PAPER, WHICH IS HOW THIS DIFFERS FROM $z$.** *P9 carries ① thirty-four times
and ② as well; P14 carries ② at `dim ker` while ① runs through the papers it cites. **The $z$ rule — "no paper
uses two" — does not hold here**, so the disambiguation cannot be left to context and has to be the reader's
to make.* ⌗ *Recorded, **not** unified: ① is the corpus's own and load-bearing, ② and ③ are inherited, so this
falls under the $\gamma$ rule (r1797) exactly as $\mathcal{H}$ does.*

**⌗ RELATIONS — SETTLED r1822.** ***A relation that DEFINES a symbol is part of that symbol's entry; a relation that STATES A RESULT belongs to `PHYSICAL_VALUES_LEDGER`, which holds the corpus's numbers by design.*** *So the three here stay — $2M=\alpha\sin u\cos^{2}u$ (defines $u$), $\mathrm{d}\tau=N\,\mathrm{d}t$ (defines $\tau$ against the lapse), $16\pi\rho={}^{3}R+K^{2}-K_{ij}K^{ij}-2\Lambda$ (defines $\rho$ beyond the spherical case) — **each arrived inside the row that needed it.** And $\Lambda G^{2}M^{2}/c^{4}=1/9$ stays OUT: it is a **result**, and duplicating it here would create the two-homes problem `SA-2` sweeps for.* **The line is checkable: does the row need the relation to say what the symbol IS?**
*(Original note, kept:)* **⌗ AND A NOTE ON RELATIONS, the question r1801 raised: §0 now carries three defining identities inside its family
rows** — $2M=\alpha\sin u\cos^{2}u$, $\mathrm{d}\tau=N\,\mathrm{d}t$, $16\pi\rho={}^{3}R+K^{2}-K_{ij}K^{ij}-2\Lambda$ —
*each arriving with the family that needed it rather than by design.* **The one it does not carry is
$\Lambda G^{2}M^{2}/c^{4}=1/9$**, which is p0's and the constant ledger's. ***Whether the canon should hold
relations at all is a real question and not obviously yes: the values ledger holds them by design.***

### ⛭ THE OPERATIONS — added r1801 by ARC 13 station ⑨. **§0 had rows for what a symbol IS and none for what one DOES. These are the corpus's verbs.**
*And the parity trio was already canonised — **in a `.tex` comment in P3's masthead, headed "SYMBOL CANON (r968)"** — while appearing in **sixteen to seventeen papers each.* ***A corpus-wide canon living in one paper's comment block is a canon nobody outside that paper reads.*** **Lifted here verbatim, with the operations it sits among.**

| operation | what it does | order / structure | where |
|---|---|---|---|
| $R$ | **the ORIENTATION / MASS-REFLECTION PARITY** — the $A_2$ **diagram automorphism**, the component of $\mathrm{O}(5,1)$ outside $\mathrm{SO}_0$, **and $\gamma^5$ on spinors.** *It is the operation the de Sitter geometry is even under and the Schwarzschild mass odd under*: P7's **"$R$-odd part $-2M/r$, the perspectival Schwarzschild mass."* P5: *"the diagram, anti-diagonal reflection — the vantage-swap, de Sitter-fixed, the branch-point crossing at $r=0$"* | order 2 | **17 papers** |
| $P$ | **the AREAL SPATIAL PARITY** $r\mapsto-r$ | order 2 | 17 papers; **P3 states it does NOT appear there** |
| $T$ | **the TIME REFLECTION** $X_0\mapsto-X_0$ | order 2 | 16 papers |
| $\sigma$ | **the ROOT-EXCHANGE INVOLUTION — and it is the REFLECTION OF THE SKY ANGLE $w$.** Weyl, mass-invariant, Nariai-fixed, **no branch-point crossing**; **the monodromy about the Nariai branch point IS $\sigma$** | order 2; **$(\sigma\tau)^2=\mathrm{id}$ with $\tau$ of order 3, generating $D_3\cong S_3$** | 10 papers |
| $g_1$ | **the CHART INVOLUTION — it inverts the THROAT ANGLE $u$ as $\sigma$ reflects $w$** | order 2 | P3, P5 |
| $\xi$ | **the SEAM CONTINUATION** $\theta\mapsto\pi/2+i\psi$ — **the analytic continuation whose invertibility secures the correspondence**; a *partial* involution | — | P3, P5 |
| $\Psi$ | **⚠ TWO REFERENTS, both field-standard.** ① **the Newtonian-gauge potential** ($\Phi=\Psi$ absent anisotropic stress). ② **the Wheeler--DeWitt wavefunction**, $\hat H\Psi=0$. They do not co-occur: ① is the perturbation sector, ② the canonical one. **The SLICING OPERATOR is named in words, never symbolised** (r2089); the **null-boundary correspondence** is $\mathcal{N}$ (r2089). | ① P15, receipts · ② P7 §385, P10 |
**⛭ AND THE STRUCTURE THEY MAKE, which is why they belong together rather than as seven rows:** *$\sigma$ and $R$
**generate $D_6$**; **$\sigma$ and $\tau$ generate $S_3$**; **adjoining $P$ to $S_3$ gives $\mathrm{Aut}(A_2)=S_3\times\mathbb{Z}_2=D_6$.**
*P5's warning is the one to carry: $\sigma$, $R$ and $\xi$ are **three DISTINCT operations that share a continuation
mechanism and must not be conflated*** — *$\sigma$ mass-invariant with no branch-point crossing, $R$ the vantage-swap at the
branch point $r=0$, $\xi$ the throat seam at $X=\alpha$.*

### ⛭ THE ANGULAR FAMILY — added r1800 by ARC 13 station ⑧. **Predicted at r1795, and it is the largest family yet: five symbols, ~600 uses, and NOT ONE had a row.**
| symbol | reading | where | count |
|---|---|---|---|
| $w$ | **THE SKY ANGLE — the construction's single moving part.** *"one slicing plane (a door) swinging about one hinge — and the sky angle $w$"*. The offset–mass relation is **the pure triple angle $2M=\tfrac{2}{3\sqrt3}\sin 3w$**; the horizon three-fold is **equally spaced by $120^\circ$ in $w$**; the root-exchange involution $\sigma$ **is the reflection of $w$** | **P3 ×160 · P5 ×98**, plus P7, P12, P13, P15, P2, P4, P8 | **×291, 9 papers** |
| $u$ | **THE THROAT ANGLE** — the second of the two angles the one swing carries: *"the sky angle $w$, throat angle $u$"*. $2M=\alpha\sin u\cos^{2}u$; **the chart involution $g_1$ acts on $u$ as $\sigma$ acts on $w$** | **P3 ×37**, P11, P5, P9 | **×56** |
| $\theta$ | **the ANGULAR RADIUS on the observer's sky** — *"a small circle of angular radius $\theta$"*, with the throat's planar image at $R=2/\sqrt3$; also the standard polar angle in $(t,r,\theta,\varphi)$ | 7 papers | **×92** |
| $\psi$ | **⚠ TWO READINGS.** ① **the OVERCRITICAL CONTINUATION variable** — $\sin\theta\to\cosh\psi$, the seam continuation $\xi:\theta\mapsto\pi/2+i\psi$ (**P3 ×29, P5 ×12, P13 ×22**) · ② **the GOWDY WAVE AMPLITUDE**, $e^{2\psi}dx^{2}$ (**P11 ×26**) — *inherited, Gowdy's own* | 6 papers | **×101** |
| $\chi$ | **the COMOVING RADIAL COORDINATE** — $ds^{2}=-d\tau^{2}+(\partial_\chi r)^{2}d\chi^{2}$, the proper-frame line element; the SdS solution defined on $\tau+\chi>0$ | **P15 ×29 · P7 ×16 · P3 ×14 · P1 ×12 · P9 ×7** | **×78** |
**⛭ $w$ AND $u$ ARE THE CONSTRUCTION'S TWO ANGLES AND THE CANON HAD NEITHER.** *P3's own masthead states the
discipline they encode — ***"the construction has a SINGLE MOVING PART — one slicing plane swinging about one
hinge"*** — and both angles measure that one swing, $w$ on the sky and $u$ at the throat. **The involutions act on
them in parallel: $\sigma$ reflects $w$, $g_1$ inverts $u$.***
**⛭ $\psi$'s two are disjoint by paper** *(P11 is the only Gowdy user; the continuation papers have no wave
amplitude)* **and the second is inherited, so it falls under the $\gamma$ rule (r1797): recorded, not unified.**

### ⛭ THE THERMODYNAMIC PAIR $\kappa$, $\beta$ — added r1799 by ARC 13 station ⑦. **Predicted at r1795, landed two stations later.**
| symbol | reading | where |
|---|---|---|
| $\kappa$ | **THE SURFACE GRAVITY — one meaning, eight papers, no exceptions.** The first law $dM=\tfrac{\kappa}{8\pi}dA+\dots$ (P1, P7) · $\kappa=\tfrac12\lvert f'(\alpha)\rvert=1/\alpha$ at the de Sitter horizon (P10) · **$\kappa=0$ at the Nariai double root**, where $f(r_N)=f'(r_N)=0$ (P15, P3, P16) | P15 ×11 · P10 ×8 · P16 ×5 · P7 ×2 · P6 ×2 · P1 · P3 |
| $\beta$ | **⚠ THREE READINGS.** ① **the EUCLIDEAN/THERMAL PERIOD** $\beta=2\pi\alpha$ — the Gibbons–Hawking regularity period, **and the one that matters: it is where $\hbar$ enters the corpus** (p0, P13, P10) · ② **an ANALYTIC-CONTINUATION PARAMETER** on the horizon angle, $3w=\pi/2+i\beta$, joining the seam (**P3 ×9**) · ③ **the MODE DEGENERACY** $\beta^{2}$ in the closed-$S^3$ Harrison–Zel'dovich weight (**P15 ×4**) | 5 papers |
**⛭ $\kappa$ is the corpus's cleanest shared symbol — eight papers, one meaning, and its zero locates the Nariai
member.** *No canon action beyond recording it.*
**⛭ $\beta$'s three are disjoint by paper and by form:** *① always appears as $\beta=2\pi\alpha$ or $\beta=2\pi/\kappa$
· ② always inside $\pi/2+i\beta$ · ③ always squared. **No paper uses two.*** **And ① is load-bearing in a way the
others are not: it is the single point at which the quantum of action enters, *"a different register"* in p0's
own words — so if any of the three is ever to be renamed, it is not that one.**

### ⛭ $\sigma$ — THREE REFERENTS, added r1798 by ARC 13 station ⑥. **Absent from the canon; the corpus's own reading is the dominant one.**
| reading | what it is | where | count |
|---|---|---|---|
| **① THE ROOT-EXCHANGE INVOLUTION** *(the corpus's own)* | order two, **$(\sigma\tau)^{2}=\mathrm{id}$ with the sky-angle periodicity, generating $D_3\cong S_3$** — the description groupoid's generator. **The monodromy about the Nariai branch point IS $\sigma$**; adjoining $P$ gives $\mathrm{Aut}(A_2)=S_3\times\mathbb{Z}_2=D_6$. ***AND THE $S_3$ IS WORN THREE WAYS, NOT TWO — the third face was missing from this row until r1883:*** **the deck/monodromy group of the branched cover · the WEYL group of $A_2$ · and the GALOIS group of the horizon cubic over $\mathbb{C}(2M)$** *(P5 `rem:galois`, citing Harris 1979: $\Delta=4-27(2M)^2$ is not a square, so the group is the full $S_3$)*. **And the dial is that cubic's SPLITTING field — the sky angle is its Galois closure** *(P5 `rem:galois-closure`)*. P13 §sec:sigma proves **a real involution is NOT the Wick rotation** | **P5 ×90 · P3 ×51 · P13 ×11 · p0 ×3 · P12 ×1 · P14 ×1** | **×157** |
| **② the STANDARD DEVIATION** *(inherited)* | *"$\times\sigma$ at the microwave-background baryon density"*, *"$\sigma$ of the measured primordial values"* | **P16 ×9 · P15 ×4 · P7 ×3** | **×16** |
| **③ a TENSOR INDEX** *(inherited)* | $g_{\mu\sigma}g_{\nu\rho}$ | **P9 ×5** | **×5** |
**⛭ Same structure as $\gamma$ (r1797): ① is the corpus's own and takes one definition; ② and ③ are borrowed and
their overload is deliberate.** *And disjoint in form — ② always follows a number or precedes "of", ③ always sits
in a subscript pair. **The bare standalone $\sigma$ is always the involution.***

### ⛭ $\gamma$ — INHERITED NOTATION, and the canon's rule for it is NOT "define it once" (r1797, ARC 13 station ⑤)
**Five distinct bare referents across the corpus, and every one is standard notation in the field the paper is
speaking to.** *This is a different case from $\rho$, $\tau$, $\eta$ and $X$ — those are the corpus's own symbols
and want one definition each. **$\gamma$ is borrowed, five times, from five literatures.***

| reading | where | the convention it follows |
|---|---|---|
| **a null geodesic segment** $\gamma$ | **P1** ×5 | universal GR notation for a curve |
| **the inverse-square coefficient**, $\gamma=\tfrac14$ | **P10** ×8 | the Kalf–Walter–Schmincke–Simon constant; $\gamma\le\tfrac14$ is the essential-self-adjointness threshold |
| **the Gowdy metric function**, $e^{2(\gamma-\psi)}(-dt^2+dz^2)$ | **P11** ×16 | Gowdy's own variables, unchanged since 1971 |
| **the Zipoy–Voorhees $\gamma$-metric parameter** | **P9** ×5 | the solution is *named* for the parameter |
| **a photon**, ${}^3\mathrm{He}(\alpha,\gamma){}^7\mathrm{Be}$ | **P16** ×1 | nuclear reaction-channel notation |
| *(decorated forms, never bare)* $\gamma^5,\gamma^\mu$ **Clifford** · $\gamma_{ij}$ **the induced spatial metric** | P13 ×43, P14 ×33, p0 ×11; P7, P12, P10 | Dirac and ADM, both universal |

**⛭ THE RULE, and it is the opposite of the radial family's:** ***do not unify these.*** *Renaming Gowdy's
$\gamma$ or the $\gamma$-metric's would make the papers **harder** to read for the people they are written for,
and the corpus's own standard is to speak each field's language. **The canon's job here is to record that the
overload is deliberate and inherited, so a future sweep does not "fix" it.***
**⌗ And the safety is the same structural disjointness found for $\eta$ and $X$: no paper uses two bare readings.**
*P1 has only the geodesic, P11 only the metric function, P9 only the $\gamma$-metric, P16 only the photon, P10
only the coefficient — **and the decorated Clifford and metric forms never appear bare.***

### ⛭ THE TEMPORAL SYMBOLS — added r1794 by ARC 13 station ②. **They were absent, and $\tau$ is the corpus's most widely shared symbol: sixteen of seventeen papers, 404 uses.**
*§0 held **nine rows and all nine were radial.** The temporal family had no canon at all — which is why this is a station finding and not a tidy-up.*

| symbol | quantity | defined by | where |
|---|---|---|---|
| $\tau$ | **proper / cosmic time on the existing layer.** The lapse is its metrical rate: $\mathrm{d}\tau=N\,\mathrm{d}t$ — *"the lapse recovers its meaning as the covariant metrical rate of that advance"*. **Real, and the time a worldline actually keeps.** | P7, P10, P11 state the lapse relation identically; P1 uses it for the temporal separation $\Delta\tau$ that its causal argument turns on | **238 uses, 12 papers** |
| $\tilde\tau$ | **the BEAD time — cosmic time COMPLEXIFIED, the variable of the bead law** $r^{3}=2M\alpha^{2}\sinh^{2}(3\tilde\tau/2\alpha)$. **Not a second clock: the same cosmic time continued off the real axis**, which is what gives it a period. | P3 `thm:bead`; **its imaginary period $2\pi i\alpha/3$ is the deck action of the turnaround cubic's $\mathbb{Z}_3$** — *"the shift $\tilde\tau\mapsto\tilde\tau+2\pi i\alpha/3$ leaves $r^{3}$ invariant while cycling the three cube-root sheets"* | **166 uses, 12 papers** |

**⛭ The distinction that makes the tilde load-bearing rather than decorative:** *$\tau$ is **what a clock reads**;
$\tilde\tau$ is **that same time continued into the complex plane**, and the continuation is where the structure
lives — **the $\mathbb{Z}_3$ deck action, the reality involution $\tilde\tau\mapsto\bar{\tilde\tau}$ that P13 and p0
use for the antilinear conjugation, and the half-period carrying $\sinh^{2}\mapsto-\cosh^{2}$.*** ***Dropping the
tilde loses the complexification, which is the whole content.***
**⛔ ⌗ CORRECTION r1798 — the r1794 row said "no third referent" AND THAT WAS WRONG. There is one, in P5, and
it is a GROUP GENERATOR.** *`prop:tau`: **the sky-angle periodicity $\tau$, acting as $w\mapsto w+2\pi/3$, of
order three on the fundamental domain $[0,2\pi/3)$ — $\tau^{3}=\mathrm{id}$.*** **With $\sigma$ the root-exchange
involution of order two and $(\sigma\tau)^{2}=\mathrm{id}$, generating $D_{3}\cong S_{3}$** — *the description
groupoid's complete generating set.* **This is not a time at all: it is a discrete symmetry, ~28 of P5's 52
$\tau$ uses.**
*How the miss happened, recorded because the method matters: **r1794 classified τ by reading a defining context
in each paper and P5's first match was a cosmic-time use.** The generator lives later in the same file. ***One
sample per paper is not a classification when a paper uses a symbol two ways.***
**⌗ Disjointness holds even so, and this is why it caused no error: P5's generator is always $\tau^{3}$,
$\sigma\tau$, or explicitly "the sky-angle periodicity" — never a bare $\tau$ standing for a duration.**
**⌗ Otherwise usage is consistent corpus-wide — re-checked paper by paper at r1798.** *The three
papers using only $\tilde\tau$ (P3, P16, p0-adjacent) are working on the bead; the four using only $\tau$ (P1, P10,
and the operator papers) are working on the layer. **The split tracks the physics, not habit.***

### ⛭ $\eta$ — FOUR REFERENTS, added r1795 by ARC 13 station ③. **It was absent from the canon and it is the most overloaded symbol in the corpus.**
| reading | what it is | where | count |
|---|---|---|---|
| **① the CYCLOID PARAMETER** | $r(\eta)=\tfrac{a_m}{2}(1+\cos\eta)$ — the interior collapse's angle, and **the dominant use by count** | **P2** | **×89** |
| **② the COMPOSITION DATUM** | the baryon-to-photon-like inherited composition, **held explicitly distinct from the radiation amplitude $\rho_r/\rho_m$** — *"$\eta$ (composition)"*, and P7: *"like $\eta$, the datum may remain empirical at no cost to the dissolution; deriving it is the eager target"* | **P16 ×19 · P15 ×6 · P7 ×2 · p0 ×2** | **×29** |
| **③ the MINKOWSKI FORM** | the flat embedding metric — $\eta(A,A)=\eta(B,B)=0$ marking $A,B$ as **null directions** of the 5D embedding | **P8** | **×7** |
| **④ $\eta_L,\eta_E$ — the two SIGNATURES** | Lorentzian and Euclidean metrics, in the Wick-rotation failure argument: *"$\eta_L\to\eta_E$ under $J^\top\eta J$"* | **P13** | **×1** |
**⛭ These do not collide in practice and the reason is worth stating: ③ and ④ are the same object** — the flat
metric, one as a bilinear form and one signature-labelled — *and both take arguments or subscripts, so they never
appear bare.* **① and ② are bare $\eta$ and DO share a form**, but **no paper uses both**: P2 is the only cycloid
user and it has no composition datum; the cosmology papers have no cycloid. ***Disjoint by paper, which is why
125 uses have caused no error.*** **Read each occurrence by its paper, never by pattern — and if a future paper
needs both, ② is the one to subscript, since ① is P2-internal and ② is the shared datum.**

### ⛭ THE RADIAL SYMBOLS — one meaning each, corpus-wide

| symbol | quantity | defined by |
|---|---|---|
| $r$ | the **signed areal radius**. Runs through zero to negative values on the conjugate branch; $r<0$ is the antimatter reading. | the metric, $r^{2}\mathrm{d}\Omega^{2}$ |
| $X$ | the **embedding magnitude** — the 3-sphere size, $X=\alpha$ at the throat. Distinct from $r$; the two coincide only in pure de Sitter. | the embedding |
| $r_{0}$ | the **slicing offset** — the designated root, the parameter naming a member of the family. $r_{0}=\alpha/\sqrt3$ at Nariai. | P3 |
| $r_{N}$ | the **Nariai radius** — the merged double root, $\alpha/\sqrt3=1/\sqrt\Lambda$, where $f=f'=0$ and $\kappa=0$. $M$-independent. | P12; corpus-wide |
| $r_{\mathrm{HE}}$ | the **Hubble–Eddington radius** — $(M\alpha^{2})^{1/3}$, the zero of $K_G=1/\alpha^{2}-M/r^{3}$, where a structure's gravitational hold balances the cosmic expansion. $M$-dependent. | P3, P8 |
| $r_{h}$ | a **generic horizon radius** — any root of $f$, used where the particular root does not matter. | P1, P3, P15 |
| $r_{*}$ | the **tortoise coordinate**, $\int\mathrm{d}r/f$. | standard; P15 |
| $\alpha$ | the **throat scale**, $\sqrt{3/\Lambda}$. | P7, P3 |
| $\rho$ || $\rho$ | **⚠ THREE REFERENTS, corrected r1791 by ARC 13's first station — the row named two.** **① DENSITY — and NOT only in the cosmology papers.** *Checked corpus-wide: **eleven papers** use it so — P4 ($\delta\rho/\rho$, $\rho\propto a^{-3}$) · P7 · P8 (×34) · P9 · P10 ($\rho_d$, dust) · **P12 (the Hamiltonian constraint $16\pi\rho={}^3R+K^2-K_{ij}K^{ij}-2\Lambda$)** · P13 · P15 · P16 · P11 · p0. **The old scoping to "the cosmology papers" was wrong by nine.** **② $\alpha/\sqrt3$, the merged-horizon radius** — P3 only, and **local to `fig:ellipse`. Prefer $r_N$.** **③ THE CONTINUATION PARAMETER — P2, and it is the DOMINANT usage by count (×134), previously uncanonised.** *$z=i\rho$ is the Wick rotation carrying the interior cycloid to the exterior: $r(i\rho)=M(1+\cosh\rho)$, $g_{tt}=-\tanh^{2}(\rho/2)$ — **a real continuation parameter on the complex $z$-plane, not a density and not a radius.*** **Read each occurrence in context, never by pattern.** **⌗ AND P3 CARRIED THREE OF THEM AT ONCE — resolved r1792.** *Inside P3: $\rho=\alpha/\sqrt3$ (×25) · $\rho'$ the continuation parameter (×4) · **and the observer's charting distance $\sqrt7/2$ (×3), which shared the symbol with the first.** **The charting distance is renamed $r_{\mathrm{obs}}$** — the smallest sufficient edit, three sites, breaking the collision without disturbing 25 consistent uses. *Note P3 also carries **$\varrho$ = the slicing scale $2/\sqrt3$** (×14), renamed from $R$ at **r968 to free $R$ for the parity** — so the paper holds **four** radial quantities and three of them once shared $\rho$/$\varrho$.* **⚠ A first attempt used $d$ and was wrong: P3 writes differentials as bare `dr`, `dt`, `dw` (×24).** *Checked and reverted in the same revision.* || — |

### ⛭ SEAM — the definition, the names, and the values per geometry

**The definition.** A **seam** is a **turning point of the slicing curve** — a zero of $f$, where $\mathrm{d}r/\mathrm{d}\ell=\sqrt{|f|}$ vanishes; geometrically, a point at which the secant meets the throat
circle. The secant crosses the circle at two points, and they move with the swing angle $w$. **Special cases, properties of particular members:** at the Nariai slicing angle the two crossings merge, the secant running tangent to the circle (the cubic's double root); past it the secant clears the circle (overcritical); and in the diametric ($w=0$) cut the crossings are the ends of a diameter, where $r=0$ sits at a seam. *P3
§sec:seam.* Curvature stays finite at a seam; any divergence read there is perspectival.

**The names.** Which seam, along the one closed bead:
- **front seam** — the forward turning point.
- **back seam** — **the seam at $r<0$**: the turning point reached by traversing the equator the other way from $r=0$. Since $r$ is signed and both traversals arrive at the same substrate point, the frontward and backward seam are **one point on the substrate** read at two signs of $r$. Its value is geometry-specific: the backward-radial root, $-2\alpha/\sqrt3$ at Nariai.
- **$r=0$** — the point of the throat circle **opposite the hinge**, at $X_{1}=-\alpha$, $3\alpha$ from the hinge in the $X_{0}=0$ plane; the branch point (`χ+τ̃=0`) through which the signed radius passes onto the conjugate (`r<0`) branch (`z=π±iρ'`, P2). The hinge fixes it; the secant's crossings move with the swing.
- **equatorial / throat seam ($\xi$)** — the tangency to the throat itself, $X=\alpha$, where the 2-D slice's
  signature flips ($\sin\theta\to\cosh\psi$). The invertible continuation securing the dS↔Schwarzschild
  correspondence.
- **cosmogenesis / Nariai seam** — a *species*, not a position: the degenerate merged seam, $\kappa=0$,
  $f=f'=0$, where collapse re-founds as the expanding cosmology.

**The values.** Where a seam sits is geometry-specific:

| geometry | front seam | back seam |
|---|---|---|
| **Schwarzschild** ($\Lambda=0$) | $r=2M$, the collapse horizon — the cycloid's $z=0$ critical point, opening into the two $r>2M$ exteriors | $r=0$, at $z=\pi$ — the $w=0$ cut is **diametric**, so $r=0$ sits *at* a seam by that configuration; the $r<0$ arm is the same equator traversed the other way, and the frontward and backward seam are **one point on the substrate** |
| **undercritical SdS** | the inner root of $f$ — $M$-dependent | the backward-radial root |
| **Nariai** | the merged double root, $r_{N}=\alpha/\sqrt3$ — here the front seam *is* the Nariai seam | the backward-radial root, $-2\alpha/\sqrt3$ |

**So a seam's name is general and its radius is not.** Quote the name with its geometry, or quote the radius.

### seam

**Definition (the genus).** A **seam** is a **turning point of the slicing curve** — a locus where the curve turns (a horizon `f=0`, or the equatorial maximum `dr/dθ=0`) and an **explicit invertible analytic continuation joins the two pieces meeting there** (the signature flip `sinθ→coshψ` = `θ↦π/2+iψ`, or the `r↦−r` branch). In the singularity taxonomy (P1/P2) a seam is a **finite-curvature metric singularity**: the slicing *chart* degenerates (the 2-D slicing surface's signature turns over, or the areal coordinate turns) while the **substrate curvature stays finite** — any divergence read there is perspectival (the odd/mass-term reading, a chart artefact), not the manifold's. *Source: P3 §sec:seam ("we call such a turning point a seam"); SdS L113, L466.*

**The four specific seams — distinct turning points of the ONE closed bead. Never conflate; always qualify:**
- **front seam** — areal **r=2M**. The collapse / Schwarzschild horizon (the `z=0` critical point of the cycloid, P2); opens into the two `r>2M` exteriors (Kruskal).
- **equatorial / throat seam (ξ)** — the throat, **X=α** (the 3-sphere **SIZE**; the de Sitter *areal* radius there is `r=α`, and the two coincide only in pure dS). Where the slicing runs tangent to the throat circle and the 2-D slice's signature flips (Riemannian↔Lorentzian, `sinθ→coshψ`, `θ=π/2`) — the **ξ** continuation. *Not* a symmetry, but the invertible continuation securing the dS↔Schwarzschild correspondence's exactness. *(P3/groupoid §sec:seam; boundary's "`r=α` seam".)*
- **Nariai seam** — the **degenerate merged horizon, κ=0, areal r=α/√3** (the double root, `f=f'=0`). ***It is a HORIZON, not the beginning.*** On the expansion leg the bead reaches `r=α/√3` at **z=0.654, τ̃=0.43899α ≈ 7.41 Gyr** — the lap's forward boundary, where `d²r/ds²=0` and **deceleration hands over to acceleration**. Whether the causal reassignment (the NBC occurrence) is *located* at this seam, or spans the lap, is **open** — see `THE_CLOSURE_LEDGER` r2118/r2123. **Its former gloss as "the physical cosmological beginning" was the taproot of the r=0-as-seam error and is withdrawn (r2123).** *(CR_cosmology, cosmogenesis, algebroid, dynamics, matter.)*
- **back seam** — **the seam at $r<0$**: the turning point reached by traversing the equator the other way from $r=0$. Since $r$ is signed and the two traversals arrive at the same substrate point, the frontward and backward seam are **one point on the substrate** read at two signs of $r$. Its value is geometry-specific (the backward-radial root; $-2\alpha/\sqrt3$ at Nariai). $r=0$ itself is the point of the throat circle **opposite the hinge** — hinge-relative, not fixed by the secant's crossings — and in the diametric ($w=0$) configuration it sits at a seam by that configuration.

**★ THE BRANCH POINT — the counterpart, and NOT a member of the seam genus.**
**Definition (geometry-independent).** The **branch point** is `r=0`: the locus at which the *signed* areal
radius changes sign and the slicing closes onto the conjugate (`r<0`) branch, because `g_θθ = r²` is
insensitive to `sign r`. It is the point of the throat circle **opposite the hinge** (`X₁ = −α`, `3α` from
the hinge in the `X₀=0` plane), and it is `R`'s fixed point — where the conjugate branch becomes the matter
branch. **Its signature is a VERTICAL tangent, `dr/ds → ∞`** — the opposite of a seam, where the curve turns.
**Curvature.** Finite. *the throat IS alpha, the substrate's defining constant `α`; the divergence of the `r`-chart invariants
there is the areal coordinate degenerating — a chain-rule effect, not a breakdown of the geometry (P2).*
**Physically.** ***The branch point is the beginning*** — the close of the **lift**, where antiparticles
become particles, and the acoustic era's beginning. **It is not a seam and is never to be called one.**
⚠ **In Λ=0 Schwarzschild the cycloid `r=M(1+cos z)` has a critical point at `z=π`, `r=0`** — that is a
turning point of *that* parametrisation and is why the older tables list `r=0` as Schwarzschild's back seam.
**In SdS with `M≠0`, `r=0` is not a root of `f`** (`f→−∞` there), so it is **not** a turning point of the
slicing curve at all, and on the bead it carries a vertical tangent rather than a turn.

### Euclidean null

**Definition.** The **Euclidean null** is the locus **`f = 2`** on the **lift** — equivalently
`1 − f = −1`, so that the E=1 marginal congruence has **`dr/dτ = ±i`**. At Nariai it is the single point
**`r = −0.344142 α`**, at `s/P = 0.78899` along the lift, lap angle **461.53°** in panel (B) of
`fig:dS_SdS` (71.53° past the branch point, 168.47° from the seam). *Receipt:
`storyboard_receipts/EUCNULL_unit_speed_family.py`.*

**What it is doing.** The lift runs from the **turnaround** (`dr/dτ = 0`) to the **branch point**
(`dr/dτ → ∞`) with `Re τ̃` frozen, so the whole excursion is in **imaginary cosmic time**. The Euclidean
null is the one point along it where **`|dr/dτ| = 1`** — where the Euclidean excursion crosses light speed.
It is the *same condition the seams realise on the real legs, realised on the conjugate branch*:
***the seams are the LORENTZIAN nulls; this is the EUCLIDEAN one.***

**⌗ THE UNIT-SPEED FAMILY — one cubic, three values of `c = 1 − f = (dr/dτ)²`.**
Since `1 − f = 2M/r + r²/α²`, every unit-speed locus is a root of `(1−f)² = 1`, i.e. **`f(f−2) = 0`**, and
the whole structure is the single cubic `r³ − c·r + 2M = 0`:

| `c = 1−f` | `f` | `dr/dτ` | locus | real roots |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **+1** | 0 | ±1 | **the seams** (Lorentzian null) | **three** — the Weyl triple, `+α/√3` (double), `−2α/√3` |
| **0** | 1 | 0 | **the turnaround** | one — `−(2M)^{1/3}` |
| **−1** | 2 | ±i | **the Euclidean null** | **one** — discriminant `−4 − 27(2M)² < 0` always |

*So there are exactly **two Lorentzian nulls and one Euclidean null**, and the count is forced by the
discriminant, not chosen.* **The turnaround sits between them at `c = 0`** — the family is `{+1, 0, −1}`.

**⚠ IT IS NOT A SEAM, and must not be called one.** *A seam is a **turning point of the slicing curve**,
`dr/dℓ = √|f| = 0`. At the Euclidean null `f = 2`, so `dr/dℓ = √2` and **the curve does not turn**. It is
transversal on the bead (`r'' = +1.969`), belongs to the **dynamics** side (`1 − f`, whose zero is the
turnaround) rather than the horizon cubic, and the name is deliberately free of the word `seam`.*

**⌗ THE SEAMS' BEAD SIGNATURE (equivalent to `f=0`, and the check that ties the two readings together).**
*Parametrised by path length `s` along the closed bead, both Nariai seams sit at **`dr/ds = 1` exactly** —
the back seam crossed **transversally** (`r''=−1.299`, `cosh(3s/2α)=2`), the front **tangentially**
(`r''=0`, its minimum). They differ by **order of contact, not by presence of a feature**, and their radii
stand in the exact ratio **2:1** about the branch point. Since `r` is signed and both traversals arrive at
the same substrate point, **the front and back seams are ONE point on the substrate** — the point at which
the null ruling takes the lap excursion — read at two signs of `r`.*

**⛔ THE CONVENTION IS WITHDRAWN (r2123). THERE IS NO BARE "the seam."** *The r1012 audit made `"the seam,"` unqualified, mean the Nariai seam **and** "the cosmological beginning" in one breath. **Those are two different loci** — the Nariai degenerate horizon is at `r=α/√3` (z=0.654, ~7.41 Gyr), the beginning is the **branch point r=0**. That equation is what licensed "at the branch point," "from the seam outward" and "transmits at the branch point" throughout the cosmology papers, and it is what the r2123 sweep corrected at 99 sites. **Every use of `seam` is now qualified — front / back / equatorial-ξ / Nariai — and the beginning is named `the branch point`, never a seam.** **The equatorial ξ is ALWAYS qualified** — "the equatorial seam" / "the ξ continuation" (throat, `X=α`; de Sitter areal `r=α`; the signature flip) — never bare "the seam." Front and back are always qualified as such, **and their radii are geometry-specific** (Schwarzschild `r=2M` / `r=0`; Nariai `α/√3` / `−2α/√3`). *Why this way:* the Nariai seam is the corpus's recurring physical object; the equatorial ξ is a geometric-continuation feature that happens to sit on the same throat. The `α`-coincidence in *pure* de Sitter (equatorial areal `r=α` = throat size `X=α`) is what let the bare word drift, and is a taproot of the `r`-vs-`X` conflation the priority rule corrects. **Corpus sweep (r1012):** P3/groupoid/boundary bare "the seam" (equatorial) qualified; cosmology-paper "the seam" (Nariai) left canonical. [↔ §1e, §1f, §1k, §1s]

### X — the embedding coordinates (and the embedding magnitude)

`X₀, X₁, X₂, X₃, X₄` are the coordinates of the **flat Minkowski embedding space** ℝ^{1,4} in which the substrate sits as the hyperboloid `−X₀² + X₁²+X₂²+X₃²+X₄² = α²`. The scalar **`X = √(X₁²+X₂²+X₃²+X₄²)`** is the **magnitude of the spatial embedding vector**; it equals **`α` at the equator/waist/throat** (`X₀=0`, the minimal 3-sphere) and grows off it (`X=√(α²+X₀²)`). **`X=α` is the SIZE of the throat 3-sphere — an embedding statement, not an areal radius.** Over the closed slicing it grows as **`X(T)=α cosh(τ/α)`** — the S³ radius / ontological scale factor (see *the scale factor* entry), `X=α` its throat minimum. Individual coordinates carry roles: `X₀` timelike; `(X₀,X₁)` the radial–temporal plane; `√(X₂²+X₃²+X₄²)` the areal radius `r`.
**⚠ Which chart `X` denotes:** `X` is the **raw ℝ^{1,4} embedding magnitude**. At the Nariai merged horizon (areal `r=α/√3`) the raw embedding coordinate on the waist is `X₁=√(2/3)·α`. This is **not** the celestial/gnomonic sky-chart value: the sky angle `w` (`r₀=(2/√3)sin w`) reads `(cos w, sin w)=(√3/2, 1/2)` at Nariai (`w=30°`). The raw embedding (`√(2/3)`) and the celestial/gnomonic (`√3/2`) are **different charts** — unqualified "`X`" means the **raw embedding**; the gnomonic values are the observer's *projected* sky coordinates (P3 §projection). *(Companion to the **★ PRIORITY NOTATION RULE**; never write the throat/equator as `r=α`.)*

### r — the signed areal radius

The **areal radius**: the radius of the SO(3) 2-sphere orbits, `r = √(X₂²+X₃²+X₄²)`, the `r` of `r²dΩ²` in the SdS line element. It is a **signed** coordinate — it runs through **`r=0`** (the branch point) onto the conjugate (`r<0`) branch, closing the bead. Related to the embedding radial–temporal plane by the exact dictionary **`X₁²−X₀² = α²−r²`** (PIVOT_EMBEDDING_FOUNDATION; P8): `r=0` ↔ centre hyperbola (the never-actualised endpoint, curvature-singular for M≠0); `0<r<α` ↔ spacelike static interior (throat→centre); **`r=α` ↔ the null cone `X₀=±X₁` — the de Sitter horizon / equatorial-ξ seam, the flat leaf**; `r>α` ↔ the timelike (expanding cosmological) wedge. **Values to hold: de Sitter horizon `r=α`; Nariai merged horizon `r=α/√3`; branch point `r=0`.** *(★ never conflate with `X`; the two coincide only in pure dS, at the waist point where the de Sitter areal `r=α` sits on the size-`α` 3-sphere — the coincidence that hid the bug.)*

### r₀ — the slicing parameter (the designated root)

The **slicing parameter**: the horizon-cubic root *designated* as the mass-horizon of a given member — **one number per slicing** (blind to where on the lap one stands). It fixes the mass: **`2M = r₀ − r₀³ = α((r₀/α) − (r₀/α)³)`** (P3 §mass), so `r₀` is *a turning point, not a coefficient*. In the gnomonic sky chart **`r₀ = (2/√3) sin w`** (`w` the observer's genuine sky angle; the scale `2/√3` the unique value removing the residual harmonic, giving the pure triple-angle `2M = (2/3√3) sin 3w`). **Nariai: `r₀ = α/√3`** (`w=30°`) — the designated root sitting on the merged double pair, so at Nariai `r₀` *coincides with* the merged-horizon areal radius `α/√3`. Distinct from `r` (the *running* areal coordinate) and from `X` (embedding). The root-exchange involution **σ** swaps `r₀` between roots at fixed `2M`. *(P3.)*

### R, P, T — the three discrete reflections (and C)

**Corpus canon.** Three linear reflections and one antilinear involution. Use them as defined:
- **R** — the **orientation / mass-reflection parity**: the A₂ diagram automorphism, `∈ O(5,1)\SO₀(5,1)`, realised on spinors as **γ⁵**. Acts as `r₀↦−r₀` (`2M↦−2M`), carries the fundamental **𝟑 → 𝟑̄ (matter → antimatter)**, and — worked and computed (`r_swap.py`) — **is the single reflection `r↦−r`** that simultaneously reaches the conjugate (`r<0`) branch, is the mass reflection, and **swaps the two null rulings A↔B**: the ruling-swap, the conjugate-branch continuation, and matter/antimatter are **one ℤ₂, four faces** (§1h). ⚠ **But the ruling-swap face is GENERIC and distinguishes nothing** (`ruling_swaps.py`, verbatim): *"EVERY orientation-reversing isometry (det = −1) swaps the two null rulings — T does, R does, any reflection does."* **Read the four faces as four things this one map DOES, not four things that IDENTIFY it** — only (i), (ii) and the `r=0` fixed point do that. *(r1103.)* Spacelike, **horn-preserving** (P3/§1e). This is the reflection the antimatter-progenitor theorem rides.
- **P** — the **areal spatial parity**: `r↦−r` as the *spatial-frame antipode* (flips the spatial triad `e^r,e^θ,e^φ ∝ r`), Clifford generator **γ¹γ²γ³**, which **anticommutes with γ⁵**. *(⚠ P's `r↦−r` is the spatial-frame parity — an isometry of the fixed-M geometry — and is **distinct** from R's `r↦−r`, which continues to the `−M` conjugate branch; the one coordinate symbol naming two operations is itself a conflation to hold apart.)*
- **T** — the **time reflection** `X₀↦−X₀`: timelike, **horn-flipping** (exchanges the two horns of the skew hexagon).
- **Klein-four:** P, T, γ⁵ generate `ℤ₂×ℤ₂`; since `γ⁵ = iγ⁰γ¹γ²γ³`, **R = γ⁵ ∝ PT** *as cut-spinor operators*. **★ RESOLVED r1064** (`R_eq_PT.py`): the apparent tension with "R horn-preserving / T horn-flipping" is a **category conflation of two arenas**. `R = γ⁵ ∝ PT` is a statement about the induced **4D-cut spinor operators** (`P*T = ±i·γ⁵`, computed); "R horn-preserving / T horn-flipping" is a statement about the **5D geometric reflections**. They differ because **R reflects the *transverse* cut-normal** (the 5th/offset leg), fixing all four spacetime legs → horn-preserving, and its induced cut-spinor action is `γ⁵` (reflecting a 4D cut's normal acts as the 4D volume element); whereas **P, T reflect legs *within* the 4D cut** (T flips X₀ → horn-flipping), and their spinor product is also `∝ γ⁵` since `γ⁵` is that same volume element. So R and PT induce the **same** cut-spinor operator while being **distinct** geometric reflections — both true, no tension.
