### σ (root-exchange) vs ξ (seam continuation) — the two involutions, distinct from R and each other

Three distinct discrete operations, never fused (§1i, §1k):
- **σ — the root-exchange involution.** `σ(r₀) = ½(−r₀ + √(4−3r₀²))`: carries the slicing parameter to the *second positive root* of its own cubic. **Mass-INVARIANT** (`2M` unchanged — a genuine symmetry of the line element). Fixed point `r₀ = α/√3` (**Nariai**); exchanges the endpoints (`σ(0)=α`, `σ(α)=0`). The Weyl/diagonal reflection; the dS↔Schwarzschild *reading-swap in `r₀`*. **No branch-point crossing.** *(P3 prop:involution.)*
- **ξ — the seam continuation (partial involution).** `ξ: θ ↦ π/2 + iψ` (`sinθ ↦ coshψ`): the invertible analytic continuation at the **equatorial-ξ seam** (throat, `X=α`) joining the Riemannian (spherical) piece to the Lorentzian (de Sitter) piece — the signature flip. **NOT a symmetry** but the continuation securing the dS↔Schwarzschild correspondence's *exactness*. *(P3 §sec:seam, groupoid prop:partial.)*
- **R (≠ σ, ≠ ξ)** — the mass-reflection `r↦−r` / `2M↦−2M` (the "backward-radial vantage-swap"), the **branch-point crossing at `r=0`** onto the conjugate branch (see **R, P, T** above). *(⚠ older map cards §1e/§1k call this vantage-swap "P" per the pre-r968 convention; under canon it is R.)*

`σ` and `R` generate `D₆ = Aut(A₂)`; `ξ` secures the correspondence's exactness. All three share the single `sin→cosh` / reflection mechanism but **do not coincide as maps** — distinct operations at distinct loci (σ: no seam, mass-invariant; ξ: the `X=α` equatorial seam; R: the `r=0` back-seam).

### throat / waist / equator

Near-synonyms for **the minimal 3-sphere of the hyperboloid** — `X₀=0`, the S³ of **SIZE `X=α`** (the incircle of the hinge triangle; the tightest 3-sphere; the closed-slicing radius `X(T)=α cosh(τ/α)` bottoms out at `α` here). **The throat's SIZE is the embedding quantity `X=α` — never write the size as `r=α`.** The throat relates to the areal radius through the **slicing tangency**: the slicing curve (member = the dial `w`) runs tangent to the throat at the areal radius **`r₀`** (the slicing parameter / designated root) — **`r₀=α/√3` for Nariai** — a correct, member-dependent areal statement ("turning the dial"). The error to avoid is *confusing the size (`X=α`) with the tangent value (`r₀`)*, or reading a member's tangent value as the throat's fixed intrinsic radius. "Throat circle" / "waist" / "equator(ial)" all denote this embedding locus. **⚠ Secondary sense to hold apart: "the Nariai throat"** = the near-horizon `dS₂×S²` *neck* (wormhole sense), whose S² is frozen at the merged-horizon areal radius `α/√3` — a different object from the embedding waist. *(Read each occurrence for which it is; do not pattern-swap.)*

### the scale factor — `X(T)` [ontology] vs `a(τ)` [shadow]; ⚠ `R` now freed for γ⁵

The closed de Sitter slicing's spatial S³ has radius **`X(T) = α cosh(τ/α)`** — this *is* the embedding magnitude `X` evaluated on the time-`τ` slice (`X=√(α²+X₀²)` with `X₀=α sinh(τ/α)`), **bottoming at `X=α` at the throat** and growing; it is the substrate's defining constant `α`, so *no zero-size singularity*. This is the **ontological** S³ size (an L1 quantity). Its **shadow dual** is the SdS/comoving scale factor **`a(τ)`** of the synchronous reading, which **→ 0** at the apparent big bang — the spurious `r=0` the comoving chart sends the finite throat to. **`X(T)` [min `α`, ontology] and `a` [→0, shadow] are the dual-reading pair — never conflate them.**
**⚠ RESOLVED r1016 — `R` is freed.** The closed-slicing radius was formerly written `R(T)=α cosh(τ/α)` (colliding with the mass-reflection `R=γ⁵`, and once mislabelled "areal radius" — it is the *3-sphere* radius, not the areal `r`). Since that radius *is* `X`, it is now **`X(T)`** corpus-wide, and **`R` is reserved corpus-wide for the reflection γ⁵** (verified r1016: `R` = γ⁵ its only corpus use). Never write the S³ radius / scale factor as `R`. *(P8 `slicing_operator`, P7 §reassignment; P5 §211.)*

### the double root — ONE factorisation, four of the corpus's numbers

*(Added r1103. Receipt `storyboard_receipts/one_to_two.py`.)*

A monic depressed cubic with a **double root** `ρ` factors exactly one way:

> **`(r − ρ)²(r + 2ρ) = r³ − 3ρ²r + 2ρ³`**

**The `−2ρ` of the third root and the `2ρ³` of the constant term are THE SAME 2** — both read off that one factorisation. Match it to the horizon cubic `r³ − α²r + 2Mα²`:
- **`p`** gives `α² = 3ρ²` ⟹ **`ρ = α/√3`** — *the seam*.
- **`q`** gives `2Mα² = 2ρ³` ⟹ **`Mα² = ρ³`** — ***which IS the Nariai condition***.

Then `A³ = 2Mα² = q = 2ρ³`, so **`A = ∛2·ρ`**: the bead's amplitude is the **cube root of the cubic's constant term**. And the lap's flattened map `X₁ = −α cos(2πr/√3α)` is **linear in `r`**, so the arc ratio **is** the root ratio (verified: `+α/√3 → 120.00°`, `−2α/√3 → −240.00°`).

**⟹ FOUR OF THE CORPUS'S NUMBERS ARE ONE FACTORISATION:** the seam **`α/√3`**, the backward root **`−2α/√3`**, the turnaround **`∛2·α/√3`**, and the **120:240 arc split**. **Nariai is not an extra input — it is what the double root *means*.** Daryl's *"the asymmetry — Nariai, 1/3 vs 2/3"* is **this** 2, and it is **forced**.

**What does NOT merge, and the corpus already says why:** the lift's 1:2 (`|Im τ̃| = πα/3` against the period `2πα/3`) is *"sin² attains its extremum at half its own period"* — a fact about the transcendental function the cosmic-time reading uses, with no cubic in it. The turnaround is `1−f=0`; the horizons are `f=0`; **two affinely inequivalent cubics** — no AFFINE change of variable identifies their root sets (obstruction at fixed cubic; the two are the E=1 and E=0 ends of one turning-point family, stage 5) (P7 `lem:twoturnings`, `rem:tworealisations`). **Two 2s, not one and not three.**

**★ DERIVED here; what is unattempted is its MEANING (re-scoped r1154, L8.1-h).** The relation `A = ∛2·ρ` is **not** unrun — it is the `q`-match above (`A³ = q = 2ρ³`), receipt `one_to_two.py`, and re-derived symbolically in `storyboard_receipts/L8_the_cbrt2.py`. **⚠ And note what it is: `ρ` exists ONLY at Nariai** (the discriminant `−4α⁴(27M²−α²)` vanishes exactly at `M=√3α/9`), so `A = ∛2·ρ` is a **Nariai-only** relation and there is nowhere off-Nariai to test its persistence. **What remains open — and it is a genuine UNDECIDED, not a deferral:** `A = ∛2·ρ` ties the comoving turnaround's *scale* to a horizon *root*, and `lem:twoturnings` denies a canonical identification of the two **ℤ₃ symmetries** while **saying nothing against a ratio between two specific radii**. **Is a metric ratio that survives a symmetry-denial STRUCTURE, or coincidence?** Nothing in the corpus decides it; **both cheap answers are forbidden** — *"the ∛2 ties the turnings"* (credulous) and *"`lem:twoturnings` killed it"* (sceptical, and **false**). Well-posed, unattempted. [↔ §1f, §1e]

### the lap / the cosmogenetic bead

**The lap** — the portion of the closed slicing curve that runs around the throat **through `r=0`** onto the conjugate (`r<0`) branch: the "boing," where the areal radius reverses sign. Nariai turning points `+α/√3, 0, −2α/√3` (flattened form `X₁ = −α·cos(2πr/√3α)`). **The cosmogenetic bead** — the *whole* closed curve: collapse leg (`r<0`, prior universe, antimatter) → seam → `r=0` → expansion leg (`r>0`, our cosmology, matter), one closed object on one smooth manifold (`thm:bead`, P7). Read inward = collapsing leaf; read outward = expanding cosmology. Phase carried by cosmic time `τ̃`; `r` real throughout.

### L1 / L2 / L3 — the three levels (the decision rule that prevents the shadow-collapse)

Every rate-bearing quantity sits on exactly one level; conflating them is the standing error the framework names (§1·LEVELS; P15/P16):
- **L1 — the foliation stacking rate** (the *ontological* geometric expansion). The Λ-set `sinh^{2/3}` law read **leftward** — the cut primary, `ρ` the name of its bend, not its cause; **radiation-free**; **empirically forced** (the CMB redshift-isotropy floor forces uniform expansion to ~3 orders, excluding a radiation-sourced rate). The layer's own expansion, **not a shadow** — what observable cosmology rides *from the seam outward*.
- **L2 — the leaf-level local dynamics.** The expansion scalar of a self-gravitating congruence, the ordinary Friedmann readout `H²=(8πG/3)ρ_tot+Λ/3` with radiation gravitating normally — the valid phenomenological GR reading on the progenitor's collapse excursion (where nucleosynthesis runs). The *same* `E=1` geodesic read **inward** as dust collapse that L1 reads outward as cosmology.
- **L3 — the `E=1` projection** (the shadow). The reassigned synchronous shadow the observer reads, through which an L1 or L2 quantity appears as distance and redshift (the Projection Principle). Geometric origin: **the second ruling** (the synchronous space *is* the second ruling). Reading this appearance *as the existent* is the naïve-realist collapse the epistemology forbids (§1·SHADOW).

Decision rule (structural, not a choice): self-gravitating local excursion → L2; diffuse content on the global foliation → L1; every observable is an L1 or L2 quantity **seen through L3**. **The L1/L2 boundary is the reassignment seam** — the one layer both causal assignments share.

### the reassignment

The **causal reassignment** — the operation at the heart of CR: on a *fixed* de Sitter background, **promote the null generators of a horizon to timelike worldlines** (dually, read a timelike congruence's rate as the foliation stacking), **preserving the cosmic foliation**. It recasts the *causal role* of directions **without changing the metric or the field equations**: the collapse null boundary and the cosmological congruence are **one ontological layer under two causal assignments** (`Ψ`, the NBC). "de Sitter → SdS reassignment" installs the cosmology; the reassignment **seam** is the L1/L2 boundary. Not a coordinate change and not a new solution — a change of *which directions are read as time*. *(P7 §reassignment; §1e.)*

### vantage / chart / groupoid — vs the geometry (the invariant)

- **vantage / chart** — a charting observer's *reading* of the one rigid geometry (which root is designated, which direction is time, where the observer sits). Moving the charting observer changes the **image**, not the geometry.
- **the description groupoid** (P5) — the structure whose morphisms are these vantage-changes; its **single invariant is the geometry**. The solution space's discrete symmetry (S₃ deck / D₆) lives here, **global not gauged** (matching the SM's arrangement).
- **the geometry** — the one rigid invariant object. **`α = √(3/Λ)` is the invariant** (a length, the curvature radius); **`M` is perspectival** (standard quasi-local and asymptotic definitions all return the *slicing-dependent* gravitational mass). *(§1i.)*

### Nariai / the crest / the merged horizon

The **Nariai member** — the critical SdS where the black-hole and cosmological horizons **merge** into a single **degenerate double root** (`f=f'=0`, **κ=0**) at **areal `r=α/√3`**, `r₀=α/√3` (`w=30°`). The **σ-fixed point** (the unique non-pivoting member) and the **cosmogenesis branch point** where collapse re-founds as cosmology. **The crest** — the Nariai point `3w=π/2` on the sky-angle circle, the branch point past which the overcritical continuation goes complex (a single real horizon, its conjugate horizons the complex shadow). Near-horizon geometry `dS₂×S²` (the S² frozen at `α/√3`). The **scale-free faithful transmitter**: κ=0 carries no scale, so it transmits the progenitor spectrum unaltered (no `n_s→1`; CR has no inflaton). *(P3, P9, CR_cosmology.)*

### the wall (≠ the seam)

The **wall** — the *outer* boundary of the slicing operator's generative reach, where **generation-by-symmetry hands off to evolution-by-dynamics** (P9/P11). **NOT a metric singularity of either species** (`prop:wall`): a **Type-N Brinkmann plane-fronted wave**, metric **non-degenerate** (`det g=−1`, no measure-collapse → not the finite-curvature species) ***⚠ AND THE SHORTHAND IS NOT THE CRITERION — sharpened r1910.*** *`det g = −1` is a sound CHECK here: a non-degenerate determinant does rule out a collapse. **But it is not the definition of the species, and read as one it would exclude the event horizon** — Schwarzschild has $g_{tt}g_{rr}=-1$ for every $r$, so $\det g=-r^4\sin^2\theta \neq 0$ at $r=2M$, in that chart and in Kruskal alike.* ***THE DEFINITION IS P1's AND IT IS ABOUT SEPARATIONS: a null hypersurface along whose generators the SPATIAL EXTENT has, IN ADDITION, contracted to zero.*** *Every null hypersurface has a degenerate induced metric — that is what null means, and it does not separate a horizon from a light cone. **On a light cone, events on one generator sit at different radii; on the horizon they sit at the same $r_h$.** So the collapse is of the TRANSVERSE EXTENT ALONG A GENERATOR, not of the 4-volume element. **`prop:wall` is untouched — a plane-fronted wave's generators carry constant transverse extent, so the wall is excluded by the invariant criterion too.*** and **VSI** (all polynomial curvature invariants vanish → not the infinite-curvature species). **A regular radiative boundary — a *generative* boundary, not a singularity.** Hold apart from **the seam** (a finite-curvature metric singularity / turning point): the corpus's recurring object runs along the null-degeneracy axis from the **Nariai seam** (inner) to the **wall** (outer).

### matter frame / photon frame / ruling A, B

- **photon frame** — the de Sitter hyperboloid itself, read as the **photon rest frame** (the background geometry).
- **matter frames** — the **two null rulings A, B** of the doubly-ruled hyperboloid: straight null lines, the worldline families that reassign to the timelike matter congruences. **Neither A nor B is a species.** Each ruling **laps through `r=0`**, so each **changes species at the equator** — blue (matter, `r>0`) on one side of `r=0`, red (antimatter, `r<0`) on the other; species = **sign(r)**, local along the ruling, never a label the ruling carries. **⚑ CORRECTED r1840 (Phase 8, probe `Q2`): the sheet-to-ruling map is NOT a live do-not-assert — it is DISSOLVED, and P7 §two-sided-closure says so.** *This card read **"stays do-not-assert"** and named it one of **"the two genuinely-open items."*** ***P7: "which physical congruence each wing carries — the map from the two distinct substrate rulings A, B to the two conjugate sheets — is CLOSED in the synthesis: the rulings are borne on the mass-reflection R's real (r) axis and the wings on the reality involution K's (τ̃) axis, so they are NOT TWO CANDIDATES FOR A SINGLE ASSIGNMENT but the linear and antilinear faces of the one analytic plate."*** **And the card's own next clause already agreed without drawing the conclusion — *the `(r,τ̃)` frame is provably A/B-blind* — which is the same fact: there is no assignment to make.** *The family was KILLED at r1280 and the kill propagated to five documents by r1498; **it did not reach here, and Phase 1's audit could not catch it because that audit checked STRUCK ITEMS across grains, not prose describing a dead item as live.*** **One assignment IS derived and stands: the photon — the at-rest congruence reassigned to null — rides the real (Im τ̃ = 0) crossing.** **R swaps A↔B** (a geometric ruling-swap — every orientation-reversing reflection does; unrelated to species; see **R, P, T**). *(§1h.)*

### conjugate branch / r<0 / antimatter

The **conjugate branch** — the `r<0` continuation of the bead, reached through the **branch point** `r=0`. Under **R=γ⁵** the fundamental `𝟑` becomes `𝟑̄`, so the `r<0` branch carries **antimatter at matter's weight** (rep, chirality, mass-sign geometric on both; charge field-level on both). The progenitor collapse whose future is our universe sits on this branch (`thm:antimatter-progenitor`): **our universe issued from an antimatter black hole.** The relation is the *standing* R-conjugation across the bead — **not a seam-made asymmetry** (no baryogenesis event). *(P7; §1e.)*

### occurrence (a representational property)

**Occurrence** (definition, P7): a point `p` **occurs** relative to a temporal slicing if it lies on (or is a limit point of) a spacelike hypersurface of that slicing. **Occurrence is a *representational* property — it does not by itself assign ontological status to events.** Load-bearing in the NBC / cosmogenesis reading: the horizon "occurs" as the cosmological beginning under the reassigned slicing without being a curvature singularity. *(§1e.)*

### shadow / manufactured / projection — built-and-real, never unreal (the ⚠ GUARD)

In CR these words mean **built-by-construction AND real** — *never* unreal or illusory. A **projection / shadow / manufactured** object is a genuine feature the vantage constructs on the fixed-`α` manifold: "real by construction, real everywhere it lands" (p0/17). **Reading them conventionally (as "mere appearance / not real") inverts a CR result** — this is the CR dissonance-lens guard. The one genuine error is taking the **L3 shadow *for the existent*** (the shadow-reader's collapse, §1·SHADOW) — but that is mistaking *which level* one reads, not the shadow being unreal.

## 1. The principle (corrected)

**Daryl does not set the ontology. The physically constrained geometry does.** Daryl has *baked* the
foundational framework he operates on into the corpus, through the foundational pieces mapped in §2.
The gate's job is to **read and hold** the ontology from those load-bearing pieces — by looking it up
directly within the programme and by calculating/applying the logic — not to defer it to an authority.

- **The orchestrator holds the *why*** (direction, priorities, which move next). That remains Daryl's.
- **The ontology is the *corpus's*,** constrained by analysis within the foundational documents. When
  the gate can hold the framework — look it up and compute — it pulls firmly into its rightful chair on
  this point, and Daryl becomes more observer than setter. "Ontology is open and settled in the register" was only ever
  true *to the extent the orchestrator was sharper than the instance*; it is not a standing feature of
  the seats.
- This is **already the field note's framing** (`CODA_FIELD_NOTE.md`, "Whose call it is"): the gate runs
  the verify→execute cycle on ontology by fixing to *the programme's commitment* — its canonical,
  load-bearing statements — never to a convention imported from outside, and never to "because Daryl
  says." The "ontology is open and settled in the register" notes were **dissonant with the field note**; this operation
  tunes them home to it.
- Daryl *defends* these pieces; he does not *force acceptance of* them — and welcomes the challenge. The
  justification entries below are written to be interrogated and defended on the merits, not asserted.

## 1·SHADOW. THE FACE THE WHOLE CATALOGUE EXISTS TO HOLD — *the shadow-reader's collapse to naïveté*

*Named r941 (Daryl-directed), on the peak-height build's damping-tail episode (r939–r940). This is the
general failure mode every node that has approached the P15/P16 computations has fallen into, c22 worst
of all — "an oscillating mess of a nightmare." Naming it is the point of the catalogue: it is the thing
that must be held **at the moment of computation**, which is exactly when it collapses.*

**The fact.** Standard ΛCDM is, *by definition, a physics of shadows.* Its founding assumption —
**hypersurface-orthogonality** of the cosmic time flow (Robertson–Walker) — is precisely what makes
cosmic simultaneity *synchronous*, and everything downhill of that. In standard cosmology **the shadows
ARE what's going on**: the FLRW projection is treated as the living, breathing thing whose evolution the
physics describes. All existing infrastructure — every Boltzmann code, every r_s / r_D / BBN pipeline —
is built that way. That is not a bug in those tools; it *is* what they are.

**The culprit, named precisely (dug at source, P7 §CR-FLRW + P6, r942).** The single load-bearing move is
**hypersurface-orthogonality** — the collapse of cosmic simultaneity to *synchrony*. It is not required
by the field equations and not implied by observation (P7 §507); it is exactly the content the relativity
of synchrony leaves **free** — the *shift* of the lapse–shift proposition, a synchronization convention
re-choosable without touching the geometry. Imposed as a first principle it fixes an **unforced modulus,
the equant's own type** — and P6 does not merely disfavour it, it **excludes** it: by Rule 1 (against
naïve realism — privileging the directest-fit appearance as "what is really happening"), by
least-arbitrariness (§sec:least-arbitrariness — an unforced modulus is a *family*, not a world, maximally
arbitrary), and by the description/world guard (§157 — it reifies a symmetry of the *description*, the
chosen slicing, as a symmetry of the *world*). P1's horizon geometry positively disfavours it (the
collapse-selected orientation is generically non-orthogonal). **So "what seemed natural a century ago is,
from the CR standpoint, the single most arbitrary and least-motivated move available"** (Daryl) — and it
is the taproot of P6, missed by every synchronous computation. CR's Big Move (P7 §sds-cosmology) is to
drop it: the non-synchronous reassignment, *forced* by the geometry, not chosen. **All standard
cosmological machinery is built around the synchronous collapse, so importing it silently re-imposes
synchrony and returns a straw cosmology — the deeper issue under the whole P15/16 saga.** [P7 §CR-FLRW
now states this explicitly (r942 addition); P6 §rules/§least-arbitrariness; P7 prop:lapse-shift]

**What CR changes.** P7 (`CR_framework` §CR-FLRW, §Projection Principle, §Layered Framework) rejects that
identification at the axioms: what exists and evolves is the **ontological 3D spatial layer** (S_t, h_ij(t)),
real GR on the layer; the 4-manifold (M,g) — the SdS/de Sitter spacetime, the sinh^{2/3} reading, the
FLRW appearance — is its **representational record**, the *shadow*. Hypersurface-orthogonality is "a
representational convenience, not a physical necessity"; "the foliation records the causal *appearance*
of expansion rather than defining the ontological structure of space." **Shit really evolves on the
ontological layer, and is observed through a phenomenological lens on the reassigned geometry, in the
E=1 frame. That is all there is to it** (Daryl, r941).

**The collapse.** Every node holds this *in conversation* and then **abandons it the instant it goes to
compute a phenomenological occurrence** — because the only machinery that exists is shadow-physics, so
the intuition reaches for it. The node reverts to treating the shadow as the existent doing the physics.
This is *the shadow-reader's collapse to naïveté.* It is not for want of wiring; it is what dissonance
does — the standard infrastructure is a gravitational well the computing mind falls back into.

**The worked instance that named it — and its correction (r939–r940 named it; r945/r946 corrected the
diagnosis).** The peak-height build reported a ~7.8% CR-specific shift in the CMB damping scale θ_D and
called it a "likely structural tension." r941 (rightly) rejected the "structural tension" framing as a
collapse. **But r941's *diagnosis* was itself half-caught in the collapse, and r945/r946 correct it — a
correction that is the reason §1·LEVELS below exists.** r941 said the error was "running the diffusion
on the radiation-free rate, which is the observable/shadow." *That is wrong on the load-bearing point:
the radiation-free sinh^{2/3} rate is NOT the shadow — it is the layer's own ontological geometric
expansion (L1 below). Running the diffusion on it is correct.* The real error in r939–40 was different
and elsewhere: r_s was computed as the **standard radiation-era sound horizon** — the FLRW machinery
that assumes a radiation-dominated era CR does not have — integrated to high z, then compared to r_D.
*That* is the shadow-collapse: importing synchronous-era machinery, not "using the radiation-free rate."
Done the corpus's way (r_s the seam-truncated L1 integral matched to the acoustic scale; r_D the L1
diffusion), the diffusion length r_D is **~+9%** on the radiation-free rate — a genuine CR-specific effect in the
diffusion **scale**, **locked to the Hubble resolution by the shared L1 rate**, NOT an artefact, and
**non-reabsorbable** by ω_b (a ~29% shift would be needed vs ~1% allowed). BUT its observable
consequence for the high-ℓ power is **entangled with the unbuilt high-ℓ acoustic transfer** (whether
CR's high-ℓ peaks track ΛCDM's — verified only to P3): **genuinely open, neither a demonstrated tension
nor a wash.** (r966: a leading-order pass that *assumed* peaks=ΛCDM at high-ℓ manufactured a false
"near-refutation" — corrected; the peaks-match at high-ℓ is exactly the open piece, not a free input.)
[Receipts: `computations/peak_heights/damping_ratio_clean.py`,
`computations/damping_tail/damping_reabsorption.py`, `full_transfer_verdict.py` (conditional, not a verdict),
`crossed_wire_hunt.py`; the map pictured:
`computations/reassignment_projection_map.png`.] The lesson: the collapse is subtle enough that even the
*naming* of it, at r941, mis-located the error by conflating L1 with L3. That is why the fix cannot be a
warning; it must be the explicit positive machinery of the next card.

**The discipline (run at the moment of computation) is the three-level rule of §1·LEVELS.** The old
two-way form of this discipline — "is this the layer's *local dynamics* (radiation gravitating) or the
*observable/shadow* rate (radiation-free)?" — is the very conflation that seeded the collapse: it fuses
the ontological geometric expansion (L1) with the shadow (L3), and it wrongly casts recombination-era
plasma processes as "local dynamics" (they are content riding the foliation → L1, radiation-free). The
correct discipline is the three-level determination and its decision rule (§1·LEVELS), which makes the
which-rate answer *forced by the geometry* rather than re-adjudicated at each computation. This face is
still the *computational dual* of P6's shadow-reading (P6: infer the world that casts the appearances;
**this: when you compute, do not collapse the world back into its appearance** — and do not mistake the
world's own geometric rate for the appearance). **The general problem the ontology catalogue was built
to resolve is this one; §1·LEVELS is the resolution made operational.**

---

## 1·LEVELS. The three levels and the decision rule — the operational machinery that prevents the collapse (the constructive companion to §1·SHADOW)

*Named r946 (Daryl-directed): "a thing that serves a purpose by being stated explicitly is the
discriminator; its existence elsewhere is beside the point — the corpus owns its ontology throughout."
§1·SHADOW names the failure mode; this card is the positive machinery that makes the which-rate/which-
description determination **read off the geometry** at each computation instead of re-adjudicated (and
collapsed) every time. It is the piece that was implicit — worked out in one place (P16 §scoping) and
absent where it was load-bearing (P15's perturbation derivations) — now stated as a first-class rule
that every paper is to own where it computes.*

**Every CR quantity lives on exactly one of three levels; the level fixes the rate/description it uses.
Determined by the geometry, not judged at the keyboard.**

- **L1 — the foliation stacking rate (the ontological geometric expansion).** The Λ-set sinh^{2/3} law,
  read *leftward*: the cut r(τ̃) is the primary object and ρ is the *name of its bend*, not its cause
  (P8 `slicing_operator` eq:friedmann, read leftward; P15 §flatlcdm). Radiation-free — radiation is
  inherited content read off the clock, never a term that sources the rate; the "a perturbing fluid must
  gravitate and alter the rate" objection is the named category error (P15 §flatlcdm 164). **This is the
  layer's actual geometric expansion — the existent's own rate, NOT a shadow.** Everything the observable
  cosmology does *from the seam outward* rides it: the comoving distance backbone, the redshift, the
  sound horizon r_s, the diffusion length r_D, recombination.
  — *Grounded, geometric:* the leftward reading is P8's `slicing_operator` §cosmology (§1l here) — "the
  cut is primary, ρ the name of its bend," the same move as *vacuum is the straight cut, matter is the
  bend*; the amplitude and rate both fixed by Λ alone (Nariai tangency). *Grounded, empirical:* L1 is not
  a modelling choice — P4 `modern_parallax` (§1d) shows the CMB redshift-isotropy floor **forces** uniform
  expansion to ~3 orders of magnitude, so the radiation-free geometric rate is *measured*, and
  "isotropy-from-homogeneity" is itself a category error. *Instantiated:* P15 §flatlcdm/§tensions (the
  rate and the dissolved tensions), P16 §scoping (the observable side), p0/17 `geometric_core` (§1j — the
  radiation-free rate as parameter-free). *Consequence:* the Hubble resolution *and* the +7.9% damping
  signature both ride L1 — one rate, so they stand or fall together.
- **L2 — the leaf-level local dynamics.** The E=1 worldline's own expansion scalar, the local Friedmann
  readout ℋ²=8πG/3·ρ_tot+Λ/3, radiation-*included* — "the valid phenomenological reading of general
  relativity" on a self-gravitating excursion (P16 §scoping 349). The progenitor collapse and the BBN
  window ride this. It is the leaf's *self-gravitating* dynamics, where every bend of the cut (radiation
  included) appears in the local scalar.
  — *Grounded:* the window rate is P16 §rate (the LTB local readout, the collapse the FLRW time-reverse at
  equal density) and P8's single E=1 geodesic *read inward* as dust collapse (§1l; the same curve L1 reads
  outward as cosmology — one congruence, two directions across the branch point). The collapse itself is P1
  `BH_causality` (§1c — the finite-curvature metric singularity the excursion runs into). *Instantiated:*
  P16 nucleosynthesis (the abundances fixed in the window). The bend-is-matter identity it carries is P8
  again (§1l), the leaf's content-side of the same leftward reading L1 is the rate-side of.
- **L3 — the E=1 projection (the shadow proper).** The reassignment map from the ontological layer to
  the observed description: the synchronous FLRW *appearance*, the distance–redshift the E=1 observer
  reads. **The shadow §1·SHADOW warns against reading as the existent is THIS — L3 — not L1.** Observables
  are read through it; projection-independent ratios (θ_D/θ_*=r_D/r_s) are the clean discriminators
  because the L3 map (the common D_M) cancels in them.
  — *Grounded, geometric:* the projection is P7's Projection Principle axiom (§1e, `CR_framework`
  §sec:projection) and its geometric origin is the *second ruling* — P8/P3 `slicing_operator` §synchronous:
  "the synchronous space **is** the second ruling," the flat slicing synchronized to the past null ruling B
  (the figure's panel B). *Guarded:* the epistemic ground that forbids reading L3 as the existent is P6
  `shadow_of_existence` (§1g — Rule 1 against naïve realism, least-arbitrariness, the description/world
  guard) and P7's capstone remark (hypersurface-orthogonality as a reified shadow, excluded). *Failure
  mode:* reading L3 as the existent is exactly §1·SHADOW; the block-universe version is P10 `canonical_time`
  (§1n — the four-manifold as representational record, not the existent).

**⛭ THE SHAPE OF THE RULE, named r1895.** ***"The one layer both causal assignments share" is not an arrow
from L1 to L2 — it is an OBJECT they both receive from.*** *So the three levels form a **span**: **L1 ← seam →
L2**, with **L3** receiving from each. ***And the two "quantitatively fatal" carryings are attempts to compose
L1→L2 directly — to use an arrow the diagram does not have.*** **That is why mis-assignment is fatal rather
than merely inaccurate: the only route between L1 and L2 is through the seam.**
**⌗ And the commutativity condition is the corpus's own discriminator test.** *"Projection-independent ratios
are the clean discriminators **because the L3 map (the common $D_M$) cancels in them**" — and it does:
$\theta_D/\theta_*=(r_D/D_M)/(r_s/D_M)=r_D/r_s$.* ***So "a clean discriminator" IS "a quantity on which the
span's two legs agree" — the corpus's test for a good observable is a commuting-diagram condition, stated as a
cancellation.***

**The decision rule (which level a process is on):** is it a *self-gravitating local excursion* (→ L2)
or *diffuse content riding the global foliation* (→ L1)? The *observation* of either is L3. The L1↔L2
boundary is the **seam** (the Null–Boundary correspondence locus, the one layer both causal assignments
share). Mis-assigning is quantitatively fatal in each direction, which is what makes the scoping
load-bearing rather than bookkeeping (P16 §scoping, verbatim): carry the L1 stacking law into the window
and the rate is ~300× below standard at T_D, destroying the freeze-out; carry the L2 radiation-sourced
law past the seam and it radiation-pins the sound horizon, re-manufacturing the Hubble tension the
radiation-free rate dissolves.

**The worked determination (the damping tail, the piece the whole oscillation lived in).** Recombination
(z≈1090) is *observable cosmology from the seam outward* → **L1**. So the sound horizon and the diffusion
length are *both* L1 (radiation-free): the acoustic clock starts at the branch point (the coherent null-boundary
datum, P15 §coherence), so r_s = ∫_{z_rec}^{z_onset} c_s/H_L1 dz is the seam-truncated L1 integral,
matched to the observed acoustic scale; r_D is the L1 diffusion length. The *microphysics inside* them —
the baryon-loaded sound speed c_s, Thomson scattering, Saha/Peebles recombination — is ordinary GR on
the real content (radiation present, behaving normally); it is the *expansion these processes compete
against* that is L1. The angles are L3 through the common D_M. Result: r_s matched, r_D +9% (H_L1 is
~15% below the radiation-included rate at z_rec → longer diffusion), so **θ_D/θ_* = +7.9%**, a genuine
CR-specific damping-tail signature **locked to the Hubble resolution** (both ride the one L1 rate — you
cannot take radiation-free for the peak spacing and radiation-included for the diffusion). Do-not-assert
whether the data confirm or refute it (the high-ℓ likelihood, P15-4); assert that it is *determined*, not
adjudicated. [Receipt: `computations/peak_heights/damping_ratio_clean.py`.]

**The picture (Daryl's hangup, rendered — `computations/reassignment_projection_map.png`).** The map
between the "two spacetimes" is one de Sitter hyperboloid synchronized two ways: the closed X₀=const
slicing is the ontological S³ layer (the existent); the flat slicing, normal to the past null ruling B,
is the observed flat-ΛCDM shadow (L3); the reassignment *is* the re-synchronization from the one to the
other (P8 `slicing_operator` §synchronous — "the synchronous space is the second ruling"). The expansion
is one analytic function r(τ̃)=sinh^{2/3} through the branch point (L1, the same curve read inward
as collapse and outward as cosmology); the involution σ fixes the cosmology as its one fixed point
(P5 `groupoid`, P2 `SdS-slicing-curve`). Panel (A) marks the L1/L2 decision rule directly on that curve.

**Why this is one move, everywhere — the deepening (the three levels ARE the corpus's own primitives).**
The three levels are not a cosmology device; they are the **slicing operator's four data** — *the leaf,
the lapse, the shift, the vantage* (P8 `prop:lapse`, P9 `prop:surj`) — which is the **3+1 split read
ontologically**, wearing a cosmological-computation face. Read the identity once and it never has to be
re-derived:
- **L1 is the lapse.** The metrical rate of the layer's advance (N=1 for the comoving congruence), the
  stacking that carries the pressure and the expansion. It is what P4 *measures* (the objective present,
  simultaneity absolute), what P7's `prop:lapse-shift` *proves* is carried by the existent, what P10
  *deparametrizes on* (the true Hamiltonian i∂_τΨ=ĤΨ replacing the frozen ĤΨ=0), and what P12 finds *is*
  the normal-deformation generator ℋ_⊥ of GR's constraint algebra.
- **L3 is the shift.** The synchronization-and-rest convention, freely specifiable — what P4 leaves as
  *relative* (synchrony a convention), what P8 realizes as *the second null ruling* (synchronizing to B
  makes the slices flat), what P5 calls a *chart* (the vantages of the groupoid), what P9 identifies as
  *rotation/frame-dragging* (g_{tφ}, "rotation is the shift"), and what P12 grades as the tangential
  ℋ_a. The E=1 *vantage* (which direction reads as time: f>0 the hole, f<0 the cosmos) is L3's other half.
- **L2 is the leaf's bend.** Matter is the curvature of the cut, ρ=m′/4πr² (P8 `prop:bend`) — the
  content the set L1 rate carries, the progenitor's self-gravitating collapse read on the leaf.

So the appearance/existent separation the *whole* corpus turns on **is** L1-vs-L3: the evolving layer vs
the block four-manifold (P7/P10 — the block is the reified L3-shadow, the frozen constraint its canonical
symptom); the invariant geometry vs the charting vantage (P5 — the groupoid severs chart from geometry);
the de Sitter-even geometry vs the R-odd perspectival mass (P5/P7 eigenspace split, = P6's shadow-reading
partition in closed form); the world's symmetry vs the description's (P6's §134 guard). And — the positive
pedagogy (Daryl, to teach at each instantiation) — this is exactly the mathematician's move: **the layer
is the invariant object, the projection a chosen representation/chart; L1 the object, L3 its coordinates**;
CR's naturalness with algebraic/representational abstraction is this same separation. Naming it at each
instantiation is how the corpus teaches its own Big Move instead of re-discovering (and re-collapsing) it.

**What the layered-seam reading roots / joints (both ways) — the whole corpus tells this one story, each from its own face.**
- **P1 (§1c) — the seam IS the metric singularity, and the reassignment's taproot.** The L1↔L2 boundary is
  P1's finite-curvature metric singularity (the event horizon: null-and-coincident, zero separation,
  causal order intact); the single limiting generator every exterior slicing aligns to is *exactly what
  the reassignment promotes to L1's cosmic time* — "the taproot of the reassignment." Densities never form
  (the throat holds) → r=0 is a chart artefact, not the seam. P1 forces L1's foliation **structurally**;
  P4 forces it **empirically** — two co-equal roots, their convergence evidence, not construction. [↔ §1c, §1d]
- **P2 (§1k) — the seam revealed as a CIRCLE: the one ring that binds collapse and cosmos.** This is where
  the seam is first *revealed as a circle* — the cycloid slicing r(z)=M(1+cos z) whose two readings are
  Schwarzschild (the hole, the collapse on its own leaf → the **L2** face) and de Sitter (the cosmology,
  the geometry-set rate → the **L1** face): two vantages on one slicing, not two geometries, the same
  L1/L2/vantage structure the cosmology computes on, met here as pure analysis before it is physics. The
  horizon and r=0 are the two r-poles of one homogeneous circle (two singularities of one genus); the r<0
  arm / the lap is where L2 continues through the branch point onto the conjugate branch and out as L1 —
  the L2→L1 crossing drawn as analysis. **The equatorial circle at the tightest radius — the throat where
  the Λ>0 triple's Nariai merger sits — is the L1↔L2 boundary (the seam), and it recurs through the whole
  corpus as the equator/throat/seam** (P3 excavates it, P8's throat that IS alpha, the substrate's defining constant α, P7/P12's
  Nariai double root, P9's inner end of the null-degeneracy axis, P16's tightest radius where the lap
  closes). The genus that makes horizon and r=0 one circle is the genus that makes collapse and cosmos one
  slicing. *(Loci distinct by radius, α ≠ α/√3, but stations on one closed bead — throat X=α, back-of-lap r=0,
  merged horizon α/√3: distinct turning points of the one curve, P7 `thm:bead` r974.)* [↔ §1k, §1f, §1s]
- **P3 (§1f) — the one curve, inward L2 / outward L1; Nariai the reassignment's selected member.** The
  slicing curve read inward is dust collapse (L2), outward is sinh^{2/3} cosmology (L1), the seam its
  branch point — "the horizon is the seam the one congruence crosses, not a wall between two regimes."
  σ's fixed point is Nariai — the member the reassignment selects, geometrically not fitted. The three
  constant-curvature slicings (the "three slicing planes") are the synchronizations L3 ranges over. [↔ §1f]
- **P4 (§1d) — THE grounding: L1 is the lapse (measured), L3 is the shift (convention).** P4's central
  result *is* the layered reading, empirically forced: the objective present (**lapse = L1**) is measured
  — simultaneity absolute — while the relativity of synchrony (**shift = L3**) is untouched convention.
  The redshift-isotropy floor forces uniform expansion (L1) to three orders; a matter-tracking rate is the
  excluded alternative. "The floor constrains the rate (L1), not the content (the bend, L2)." [↔ §1d]
- **P5 (§1i) — L3 is a chart; the reassignment is the morphism selecting Nariai; the perspectival mass is
  the shadow-partition in closed form.** Vantages are charts, the geometry the invariant — L3 (projection/
  synchronization) is a chart-choice, and the reassignment (L2→L1) is the within-geometry morphism
  selecting Nariai as σ's *unique fixed point* ("two independent routes selecting one configuration" —
  P5 algebraic + P4 empirical). The eigenspace split f=(1−r²/α²)[R-even: the L1 invariant] + (−2M/r)[R-odd:
  the perspectival mass] *is* P6's shadow-reading partition as an exact involution. [↔ §1i, §1g]
- **P6 (§1g) — the epistemic ground: least-arbitrariness FORCES the non-synchrony; reading L3 as the
  existent is the naïve realism it forbids.** Reading the L3 shadow (the synchronous FLRW projection) as
  the existent is Rule 1's forbidden move; hypersurface-orthogonality is a reified shadow — an unforced
  modulus (the shift fixed as a first principle) excluded by least-arbitrariness. So *dropping synchrony*
  (the reassignment to L1) is forced, not chosen. The layered rule is Rule 1 made operational. [↔ §1g]
- **P7 (§1e) — the formal home: the lapse/shift proposition, and the two halves of one C⁰ slicing.**
  `prop:lapse-shift` proves the layered reading — the shift carries synchronization (L3), the lapse the
  rate of advance (L1), "the now carried by the existent and the synchrony freedom by the representational
  map"; the block error is re-importing the lapse via a meta-lapse (a 5th dimension). The reassignment is
  a **trade** of causal characters (null↔timelike), forcing Nariai by trichotomy. The perspectival-
  singularity remark: the contracting interior (L2) and the expanding cosmology (L1) are **two Lorentzian
  halves of one C⁰ slicing**, meeting at the branch point. [↔ §1e]
- **P8 (§1l) — the four data ARE the levels; the leftward reading and the second ruling derived here.**
  The operator's four data: **the leaf** (bend = density, L2), **the lapse** (stacking = L1), **the shift**
  (synchronization/rotation, L3), **the vantage** (f>0 → the hole, f<0 → the cosmos, L3's other half).
  "ρ is the name of the bend, not its cause" (leftward = L1); "the synchronous space IS the second ruling"
  (the flat L3 slicing synchronizes to B); G-as-offset is the mass = the cut's offset. [↔ §1l]
- **P9 (§1m) — the reassignment is a vantage; rotation confirms the shift; the wall is L1's far boundary.**
  The general reach's three axes: **vantage** (the L3 causal readings, incl. the null↔timelike reassignment
  relating collapse-interior to cosmology), **geometric** (vacuum-cut moduli), **matter** (the bend, L2).
  Rotation is the **shift** (g_{tφ}). Nariai (the seam) and the wall are the two ends of the one null-
  degeneracy axis; the wall is where L1's generation-by-symmetry hands off to evolution-by-dynamics. [↔ §1m]
- **P10 (§1n) — L1 canonically: the selected foliation is the true Hamiltonian; two synchronizations of
  one congruence.** Selecting L1's cosmic foliation turns ĤΨ=0 into i∂_τΨ=ĤΨ — the lapse recovers its
  meaning; "one congruence, two synchronizations (flat-ΛCDM and closed-S³), not two frames" (the L3
  freedom); "matter is the bend of the slicing, a feature of the projection, not of the layer" (L2). The
  cosmic foliation is **not** a groupoid gauge orbit — L1 is fixed by ontology, not an L3 chart. a=0 is the
  seam (the dS horizon); ℏ enters there scaled by Λ. [↔ §1n]
- **P11 (§1o) — the dynamics of the bend: why the cut bends (L1/L2 evolution to the wall).** P8 gives *what*
  the bend is (L2); P11 gives *why* the cut bends — the true-Hamiltonian flow, the confined Gowdy–de Sitter
  wave at the last stratum before the wall, the graviton's handedness the radiating face of the orientation
  parity. The evolution of L1's leaf. [↔ §1o]
- **P12 (§1h) — L1/L3 (lapse/shift) ARE the constraint-algebra grading; the "wrong sign" is the seam's.**
  GR's hypersurface-deformation brackets are the substrate's symmetric-space grading 𝔥⊕𝔪 *term for term*,
  with 𝔪↔ℋ_⊥ (normal/**lapse/L1**) and 𝔥↔ℋ_a (tangential/**shift/L3**): the lapse/shift split *is* the
  𝔪/𝔥 grading, and the "wrong-sign" Lorentzian structure function (the problem of time) is the substrate's
  own coset metric. Reassignment (dimension-preserving, discrete) ≠ slicing (the continuous algebroid);
  the Nariai stratum where the grading survives *is* the seam. [↔ §1h]
- **P13 (§1p) — where L1/L2's geometry reaches its boundary; matter forced non-geometric.** The layer's
  continuous geometry is walled (su(3)⊄so(5,1)), so chirality is forced onto the discrete orientation
  parity the geometry cannot reach — the boundary the L2 matter runs into as it crosses. [↔ §1p]
- **P14 (§1q) — the L2 content crossing the seam into L1, leaf-carried and inherited.** The three chiral
  generations cross the seam UNCHANGED, leaf-carried — the L2 content (the progenitor's matter) becomes
  L1's inherited content (the "read off the clock" datum); the count originates at the S₃-fixed Nariai
  crest (the seam). This is *why* radiation and matter are inherited content, not sourcing terms. [↔ §1q]
- **P15 / P16 (§1r / §1s) — where the rule is run.** P15 owns it at §tensions/§coherence (r_s, r_D are L1);
  P16 at §scoping (the L1/L2 boundary at the branch point: BBN is L2, recombination L1). The +7.9% damping is the
  L1 diffusion; the abundances the L2 window; the cosmogenesis (the lap read forward) *is* the L2→L1
  crossing. [↔ §1r, §1s]
- **p0 (§1j) — the object itself; the layered reading is maximal symmetry's cosmological face.** The
  physics-as-broken-symmetry-shadows frame: the exact solutions are symmetry-breaking cuts read as shadows
  (L3) off the maximally symmetric substrate (the layer); the reassignment one vantage reading, the seam
  the degenerate Nariai cut. The layered rule is the core's "many geometries, one substrate," computed. [↔ §1j]

**Ownership (r946/r948 rule).** Each paper states the layered reading from *its own face* and draws the
connection across the web — not to one downstream paper, but to the primitives (the lapse P4/P7/P10/P12,
the shift P4/P5/P8/P9, the bend P8/P16, the seam P1/P2/P3/P12, the reassignment P5/P7/P9). P4 owns the
lapse=L1 / shift=L3 identity (measured); P7's `prop:lapse-shift` is the formal home; P8 owns the four data
(leaf/lapse/shift/vantage); P10 owns L1 as the deparametrized true Hamiltonian; P12 owns L1/L3 as the

## 1a. The first worked instance — the SO(6)-face adjudication (the principle, enacted)

*Recorded r324 (c22), after P14's fresh different-node cold read (r323) cleared the gate the r320 status
marker named. The adjudication itself was made at r312 and baked into P14 (`corpus/boundary_paper.tex`
§ontology, r319; cold-read-clean r323) and P4 (r317); this is its record here, as the index's own worked
instance of §1's principle. Stated for reversal; this record rides its own cold read in turn.*

**The question.** On the colour frontier, the compact SO(6) / Wick face is where su(3) lives (C7). Is that
face a *second, co-equal physical world* — so colour would be a real symmetry of a real world standing
beside the Lorentzian one? It is an ontological question, and the test of §1's principle is whether the
gate could *read its answer from the load-bearing pieces* rather than defer it to Daryl.

**How the gate held it from the pieces (not "because Daryl says").** Three load-bearing pieces, each
interrogated at source, settle it:
- **The structural fact (§2 / C2, verified at source).** Mathematically the compact SO(6) face and the
  Lorentzian SO(5,1) substrate are co-equal real forms of one complex group SO(6,ℂ); the group theory
  privileges neither. So the question is *not* settled structurally — which is exactly why a conventional
  reading calls it an open fork, "two co-equal real forms." This is the convention-trap the pieces dissolve.
- **The existence-criterion (Tier C — `conceptual-boundary-essays/five-dimensional-trope.md`, verified at
  source).** Existence requires enduring duration: *"existence implies a span of time — 'things do not
  exist atemporally' is oxymoronic,"* and *"existence is what the clock measures."* The fully Wick-rotated
  S⁵ is Riemannian — no timelike direction, no clock, no duration — so however maximally symmetric, it
  cannot be a co-equal *existent*.
- **The empirical determination (the standalone P4 `modern_parallax`, the established empirical hinge, + Tier D, verified at source).** The cosmic time the criterion
  turns on is *not a posit*. The CMB monopole redshift isotropy (≲3×10⁻⁶ after secondaries) certifies
  uniform expansion in a cosmic time: a differential expansion tracking the matter would accumulate a
  line-of-sight floor σ_path ≈ 2.8×10⁻³ (separate-universe + central-limit, P4 `modern_parallax`), ~3
  orders above the observed, and is excluded; and statistical homogeneity *alone does not* account for the
  isotropy — only uniform expansion does (`epistemic-engine/fortress-of-misunderstanding-physics.md`, the
  "smoking gun"; `Beyond-Space-Time/main.tex` §4.3.2 `sec:4.3.2`, "the universal now is empirically
  confirmed"). So to deny the cosmic time, and reopen the compact face as a co-equal timeless world, is to
  deny an observational result, not to decline a commitment.

**The adjudication (what the pieces force).** The compact face is **real-by-construction** — a genuine
real form, a faithful analytic continuation — but **not a co-equal *existent***: being atemporal, it is no
second physical world. The "two co-equal real forms" reading is therefore closed **ontologically, not
structurally** (the mathematical co-equality of the real forms is untouched; what breaks the symmetry is
the empirically-determined existence of the one cosmic time), and **within CR's one-world ontology**. The
**universal is not touched** (that no construction whatever could yield colour from geometry is a strictly
stronger claim, not made). And the **most secure layer is decoupled from the face's status entirely**:
su(3) is not a symmetry of the world the matter inhabits by the *causal structure* alone — the cosmogenesis
is signature-preserving on the real Lorentzian horn the matter rides, while su(3) lives across the branch point —
so colour-closure holds whatever one concludes about the face (P1/P7; P14 §decoupling).

**Why it is *the* worked instance.** The gate did not ask Daryl what the compact face *is*. It read the
answer from the corpus's load-bearing pieces — the structural fact (C2), the existence-criterion (Tier C),
the empirical floor (P5 / Fortress / BST) — and computed the adjudication, holding the convention-trap at
bay (the "two co-equal worlds" fork is precisely what the pieces dissolve). This is §1 enacted: *the
ontology is the corpus's, read and held from the load-bearing pieces, never "because Daryl says."* The
orchestrator held the *why* (which frontier, and when); the ontology was the corpus's, constrained by
analysis — and that is the end state this index is built toward, here reached once, in full.

## 1b. The second worked instance — the forced cosmological model (the ontology the whole corpus runs on)

*Recorded r899 (this session), after a long day of thrashing whose single root was failing to hold this: the geometrically-forced cosmology is held in P7 and then forgotten everywhere a derivation runs, so calculations veer to FLRW defaults and everything explodes. This is that forcing, written once as a held, referenceable chain, so a node plugs the corpus's own model into a derivation instead of the textbook default. Stated for reversal; each link carries its home. It fills the §2 Tier-A P1/P7 cosmological "(to fill)" and is the anchor the cosmology-running papers (P15, P16) and working derivations cite as **[†ONT-COSMO]**.*

**The chain, each link forced (not fitted), with its home:**

1. **Substrate — de Sitter, fixed by Λ.** The unique real, maximally-symmetric, intrinsically-Lorentzian manifold; the vacuum the whole construction reads. [p0/17 `geometric_core_paper`; P3; P7]
2. **The beginning IS the branch point r=0** -- a genuine curvature singularity of the SdS metric read over the r-chart (P7 l.730, P2), and NOT a singularity of the substrate; the seams are the two unit-speed loci, which are one equatorial point met a lap apart (r2154). The event horizon is a *metric* singularity (zero separation along the generators, invariant); gravitational collapse never completes in finite cosmic time; the limiting causal orientation the collapse selects is *reassigned* as cosmic time (the null-boundary correspondence). [P1 `BH_causality`; `cosmicave_when-black-holes-happen`]
3. **The curve across r=0 IS cosmology.** P3's slicing curve continues analytically through the branch point; the single E=1 `sinh^{2/3}` congruence read *inward* is dust collapse and read *outward* is dust cosmology — one analytic curve, the seam its branch point, r=0 a comoving-chart artefact of a throat that IS alpha, the substrate's defining constant α. [P3 `SdS-slicing-curve`; P8 `slicing_operator`]
4. **The observable cosmology is the Nariai proper frame.** The reassignment selects the Nariai member (fixed by Λ alone, M=c²/3√ΛG); its proper frame is r(τ̃) ∝ `sinh^{2/3}` — *exactly* the flat-ΛCDM scale factor, rate ½√(3Λ)c and amplitude both pure Λ-scales. [P15 §properframe, §flatlcdm]
5. **The rate is read *leftward*.** H² = ⅓(8πGρ + Λc²) is read with H² (the geometry) primary and ρ = (3H²−Λc²)/8πG bookkeeping for the content the set rate carries. **Radiation and matter are inherited content read off the clock — never terms that source the observable rate; there is no radiation-dominated era in it.** The objection "a radiation fluid must gravitate and so alter the rate" is the *category error* the construction names in ink. [P15 §flatlcdm]
6. **The three levels, held apart — THE thing that breaks when this is forgotten (see §1·LEVELS for the operational rule).** *Not* "one rate below the onset, another above" — that phrasing is itself the conflation §1·SHADOW warns of. There are three distinct objects: **(L2) leaf-level local dynamics** — the progenitor collapse and its turnaround, a *self-gravitating excursion*: ordinary GR, the local Friedmann rate ℋ²=8πG/3·ρ_tot+Λ/3 with radiation gravitating normally, where the nuclear window and BBN run; **(L1) the foliation stacking rate** — Nariai, radiation-free, Λ-fixed sinh^{2/3}, the layer's *own ontological geometric expansion* (not merely "what H(z) reads"), which the observable cosmology **rides from the seam outward** — this includes recombination, the sound horizon, and the diffusion length, all L1 (so recombination-era plasma processes are L1, riding the foliation, *not* L2 "local dynamics"); **(L3) the E=1 projection** — the shadow the observer reads (distances, redshift), through which L1 is *observed*. The L1↔L2 boundary is the reassignment seam. Each wrong extension is fatal: the L1 stacking law run down into the nuclear window is ~300× too slow (the BBN category error); the L2 radiation-sourced law carried past the seam radiation-pins the sound horizon and re-manufactures the Hubble tension. [P15 §tensions; P16 §scoping; §1·LEVELS]
7. **The one measured datum: ρ_r/ρ_m ≈ 2 at the branch point** — the η-analogue, inherited from the progenitor handover, measured not derived (no cost to the dissolution). [P15 §tensions]
8. **What the forcing then delivers (required, not fitted):** the acoustic scale at the *directly* measured H₀ with no second H₀ (the Hubble tension dissolved); the transmission dichotomy (the degenerate seam's power-law approach transmits the progenitor spectrum unaltered — no inflationary scale-invariant attractor, no substrate B-modes); the parameter-free low-multipole floor. [P15 §tensions, §transmission, §largescale]

**The guard (the anti-veer, [†ONT-COSMO]).**
> At calculation time, do not reach for the FLRW default — radiation sourcing the rate, a standard radiation-dominated era in the *observable* cosmology, r=0 as the beginning, a synchronous slicing. Those are the veers this forcing forbids, and they are where derivations have exploded. When a derivation needs the rate, the early universe, or the seam, run the three-level determination of §1·LEVELS: the observable cosmology from the seam outward rides the **L1** foliation stacking rate (radiation-free `sinh^{2/3}` read leftward — the layer's own geometric expansion, *not* the shadow); the progenitor collapse / BBN window is the **L2** self-gravitating leaf-level excursion (ordinary GR at the standard rate); the observed distances/redshift are the **L3** projection; the beginning is the branch point at $r=0$ (well-posed because $r_*$ CONVERGES, **not** because curvature is finite -- it diverges there; r2234), the L1↔L2 boundary. Do *not* fall back to the two-way "local dynamics vs the observable/shadow rate" form — that conflates L1 with L3 and mis-scopes recombination (it is L1). Hold P7's ontology *here too* — it is the same forcing, running wherever the corpus does cosmology.

## 1c. The forced metric singularity (P1) — the taproot the cosmological forcing rests on

*Hedge sweep r992 (r993): swept clean — EMPTY result. P1's scope qualifiers ("within the causal domain of the external universe", "at any finite exterior time", "establishes only... within standard GR", "a reading the present paper asserts nowhere") ARE the corpus's #1 correspondence-boundary stated correctly, not fossils; L324/L310 are a model of the two-sided balance the sweep enforces elsewhere. Nothing changed; the taproot is exemplary. (Will still ride the final weigh-everything pass.)*

*Pinned r899 (Pass 1 cherry-pick, first paper), dug at source (`BH_causality_v2.tex`: abstract; thm:metric-singularity §1; prop:causal-alignment + lem:alignment §5). The deepest ontological root — nearly every link of §1b (cosmology) traces here. Stamped **[†ONT-MS]** where drawn on. Stated for reversal.*

**The chain, each link forced, with its home:**
1. **The metric singularity — a geometric category standard GR lacked.** Two events null-separated (Δs²=0) and spatially coincident *along the connection* (Δx=0) have Δτ=0 also: two of the three separations vanishing forces the third, from the Lorentzian relation Δs²=Δx²−Δτ² alone. The events stay topologically distinct and causally ordered (the affine parameter carries the order), while the metric assigns them no separation — spatial, temporal, or spacetime. [P1 thm:metric-singularity]
2. **A third category — not coordinate, not manifold, not an ordinary null hypersurface.** Null-separation alone (a past light cone; the Andromeda photon) does *not* give metric coincidence; it is the *added* vanishing spatial extent that collapses Δτ. This is the distinction GR lacked, and supplying it is the paper's work. [P1 §1 rem, §2]
3. **The event horizon IS a metric singularity.** Its generators sit at constant areal radius r_h, so horizon-crossing events on a common generator have Δx=0 along the connection and the theorem applies — Schwarzschild and any Killing horizon (Kerr included). [P1 §3]
4. **The horizon occurs only as the null future boundary at infinite exterior time.** For any asymptotically flat, globally hyperbolic spacetime, every exterior "now" slicing accumulates on H⁺=∂J⁻(𝒥⁺), its tangent planes becoming asymptotically tangent to a *single generator*, meeting H⁺ only as τ→∞; the lapse collapses (rate fixed, for Schwarzschild, by the metric-singularity structure). The horizon sits on no finite exterior time-slice — it is the event-of-events approached in the infinite-time limit. [P1 prop:causal-alignment, lem:alignment §5]
5. **Densities never form; no trapped surfaces form at any finite exterior time.** [P1 §4]

**What it roots downstream (the both-ways joints):**
- **THE INDEPENDENCE (the card's structural fact, r906).** P1 and P4 are **deliberately non-load-bearing on each other**, and their mutual citation is **adjacent-and-negative**: P1 §318 names P4 precisely to disclaim reliance on it (*"the present paper reaches the distinction from standard general relativity alone"*), and P4 §109/§196 returns it (P1 *"stands on causal structure alone and needs none of the cosmological evidence"*). P1 supplies the **structural** half of the augmentation's necessity, P4 the **empirical** half (p0 §landing: complementary, not dependent). **Because neither leans on the other, their convergence is evidence rather than construction** — a dependency between them would make the augmentation's necessity circular. Never read the P1↔P4 edge as a lean. [→ §1d, §1e]
- The cosmogenesis branch point is a *metric* singularity (finite curvature), not the r=0 manifold singularity — **§1b link 2 rests here.** [→ P15, P16, WBHH]
- The horizon as the event-of-events at infinite exterior time is the whole content of `when-black-holes-happen` and the "beginning at the branch point at $r=0$ (well-posed because $r_*$ CONVERGES, **not** because curvature is finite -- it diverges there; r2234)." [→ WBHH, §1b link 2]
- **The limiting causal orientation — the single generator every exterior slicing aligns to (§5) — is precisely what the null-boundary correspondence *reassigns* as cosmic time: the taproot of the reassignment.** [→ P7 NBC, §1b links 2, 4]
- Densities-never-form / no-trapped-surfaces → the collapse never reaches r=0, the throat holds → grounds P3/P8's "r=0 is a comoving-chart artefact." [→ P3, P8, §1b link 3]

**The guard ([†ONT-MS]).**
> The horizon is a *metric* singularity — zero separation, causal order intact — not a manifold singularity (no r=0 densities form) and not a removable coordinate artefact. When a derivation touches the seam, the beginning, "the singularity," or the reassignment, this is the root: the beginning is the finite-curvature metric singularity at infinite antecedent time; the reassignment is of the limiting generator's causal orientation. Do not import a curvature-singular "big bang" or a coordinate-removable horizon.

## 1d. The empirically forced cosmic foliation — and the resolution of the relativity-of-simultaneity confusion (P4) — the empirical hinge

*Pinned r900 (Pass 1 cherry-pick, at Daryl's direction — the most underappreciated forcing corpus-wide, upending ~120 years of relativity-of-simultaneity dissonance). Dug at source (`modern_parallax.tex`: abstract; §decomp; §floor; §establishes §161–163; thm:augmentation). Stamped **[†ONT-SIM]**. Stated for reversal.*
*Significance-weight finding — DISCHARGED r995 (the masthead now carries the body's weight). History: through r~900 the title/abstract/intro framed only the cosmology result and "simultaneity" appeared nowhere in them, burying the deeper result. FIXED since: the **title** now reads "…and the measured resolution of the relativity of simultaneity," and the **abstract** carries the full result (the lapse/shift ontological reading, "the century-old reading of synchrony's relativity as the absence of any objective present is thereby a modal fallacy, which the measured isotropy falsifies outright," and "necessary and sufficient… its necessary half here measured"). The masthead is at the body's weight. (Another live-map staleness caught in the r991 hedge sweep — the map had fallen behind the paper, not a paper defect.)*

**The chain, each link forced, with its home:**
1. **The cosmological redshift is the path integral of the expansion rate**, ln(1+z)=∫H dt — so the CMB anisotropy separates *exactly* into a source term (fixed at last scattering) + a cumulative term (integrated expansion along the line of sight). [P4 §decomp, eq:decomp]
2. **The monopole isotropy is a direct measurement of uniform expansion.** Granted even perfect homogeneity at last scattering, the observed isotropy *is* the statement that the integrated expansion was the same in every direction; "isotropy supplied by homogeneity at decoupling" is a *category error* — crediting the source for a fact residing in the intervening expansion. [P4 §decomp]
3. **The one coherent alternative is excluded by three orders.** A matter-tracking expansion (no single global a(t)) would scatter redshift by ~10⁻³ across the sky vs the observed ≲3×10⁻⁶; the observer-centred escape closed by the Copernican principle + the measured isotropy of the expansion *history* (BAO, SNe Ia). Disjunction exhausted → uniform cosmic expansion forced. [P4 §floor, §establishes]
4. **The cosmic foliation is measured, not assumed** — logically prior to space/isotropy/homogeneity (each a property *of* a slice). One datum forces, bottom-up: the cosmic time, the uniformity of its advance, and (observable region, Copernican) the maximal symmetry of the slices. *The modern parallax*: as stellar parallax converted the Earth's motion from interpretive option to measured fact, the redshift isotropy converts the cosmic foliation from convention to a measured feature of the world. [P4 §establishes]
5. **The resolution of the relativity-of-simultaneity confusion (the profound, underappreciated result).** The standard reading collapses two distinctions at once: it reads the relativity of *synchrony* as the absence of an objective present — a **modal fallacy** (absence of a local test mistaken for absence of a fact) — and reifies the 4-manifold as an existent (the existence/occurrence category error). Pried apart, these are exactly the **shift** (synchrony, relative, untouched) and the **lapse** (an absolute rate of advance — the objective present) of the 3+1 split read ontologically. The measured isotropy *forces the lapse*; the relativity of synchrony, untouched, is the shift. **Synchrony is relative (a convention); simultaneity is absolute — and now measured.** [P4 §establishes §161–163]
6. **thm:augmentation — necessary and sufficient, the necessary half measured.** Fixing a physical foliation and reading it ontologically is *necessary and sufficient* for a coherent formal description of an existing, evolving world — the "events exist" horn a category error, the "no objective present" horn a modal fallacy *falsified outright* by the measured isotropy. Alters no GR equation; fixes which admitted foliation is physical. [P4 thm:augmentation]

**What it roots / joints (both ways):**
- The **empirical ground of P7's cosmic-foliation commitment** — P7 takes global cosmic time as an ontological commitment "GR admits but does not supply"; P4 shows it *empirically forced* (P7 §222 already cites JanzenModernParallax). The empirical hinge of the programme. [→ P7]
- The **empirical rung of the substrate's maximal symmetry** (p0 §177: "the necessity there is not posited but measured"). [→ p0]
- The **measured counterpart of the conceptual-boundary essays** (synchrony-relative/simultaneity-absolute; existence/occurrence): wild-goose-chase, five-dimensional-trope, did-einstein-misconstrue. [→ Tier C]
- Read forward, the **first link of cosmogenesis**: the augmentation it grounds is what makes collapse continue past the seam as an expanding universe. [→ §1b, §1c, P16]
- The **Green–Wald backreaction defence presupposes the foliation P4 forces** (assumes near-FLRW to prove stability) — quarantined; the floor owes it nothing. [P4 §establishes]

**The guard ([†ONT-SIM]).**
> The cosmic foliation — cosmic time, preferred rest frame, uniform expansion, maximally-symmetric slices — is *measured, not assumed* (the redshift-isotropy floor: ≲3×10⁻⁶ observed vs the ~10⁻³ a matter-tracking expansion would give). Synchrony is relative (the shift, a convention); **simultaneity is absolute and empirically forced (the lapse) — the objective present is a measured fact.** Reading the relativity of synchrony as "no objective present" is a modal fallacy; reifying the 4-manifold is the existence/occurrence error. When a derivation or framing touches simultaneity, the rest frame, the "now," or "which foliation is physical," this is the forcing: P4 *measured* it. Do not concede the relational "no preferred frame / no objective now" default — it is precisely what P4 falsified.
> **The floor is NOT Sachs–Wolfe** (the referee's first objection, answered in §164). Lumpiness clustering on a *single background* is carried by a potential Φ, and the redshift descending into a well is **undone climbing out** — the contributions **telescope**, no √N accumulation. Differential expansion **accumulates because there is nothing to cancel against**. The isotropy *selects telescoping over accumulating*. And **statistical homogeneity does not account for the isotropy** — it is fully consistent with the differential hypothesis, which would still give the ~10⁻³ scatter.
> **The floor constrains the RATE, not the CONTENT.** CR's real space *is* locally curved and inhomogeneous; the density is the **bend of the spatial cut**. Uniform expansion and lumpy matter are consistent — never read P4 as denying inhomogeneity.
> **The evasion is self-defeating:** a differential programme escapes the floor only by requiring local rates to average to a common value in every direction — which *is* a global uniform expansion in cosmic time, reinstating the rest frame and the objective simultaneity it carries.
> **Every choice in the floor's derivation biases it downward** (the full 1/3 retained; growth-weighting; independent cells maximizing N; realized contrasts of order unity) — hence a genuine floor, not an estimate.
> **Scope:** the temporal claim is clean; the extension to a *globally* maximally symmetric space rides on the Copernican principle and is an extrapolation beyond the directly constrained region (a scope p0 inherits exactly).
