## 1e. The CR augmentation and the null-boundary correspondence (P7) — the co-keystone: collapsed matter must become a universe

*Pinned r901 (Pass 1 cherry-pick #3) from a **five-section dig of a 1053-line paper**; **read entire and rebuilt r911** (Pass 2). Dug at source (`CR_framework.tex`, entire): §intro; **§CR-axioms** (five axioms; occurrence/representation; the problem-of-time dissolution §199; the lapse/shift proposition and the meta-lapse §250); **§CR-consistency** (imported P1 theorem; Smoothness of Layers); **§CR-applications** (projection principle; Minkowski/Schwarzschild; gravitational waves; time travel; the hole argument; **§CR-FLRW**); **§sds-cosmology** (the causal reassignment as a *trade*; the Nariai trichotomy §507; the α/√3≠α guard §509; r=0 real §511; §what-this-opens-onto); **thm:null-boundary** in full with its **six remarks**; **the (F1)–(F6) data, the dependency figure and the 17×17 matrix**; thm:augmentation-p7; thm:cosmogenesis; cor:nonspherical; §synthesis + §general-reach; §frontiers. Stamped **[†ONT-CR]**. Stated for reversal.*
*What the Pass-1 card missed — nearly the whole paper: the **five axioms**; the **problem of time dissolved at the axioms** (before any construction); the **lapse/shift proposition** and the **meta-lapse** that locates the block error formally; the **hole argument** and **no-ontological-time-travel** results; the **Smoothness of Layers** theorem; the **applications** ("flat spacetime does not imply flat space"; gravitational waves as the layer's TT shear, *exhibited* not asserted; **FLRW as a projection**, hypersurface orthogonality *structurally disfavored*); the reassignment as a **trade** of causal characters; the **trichotomy that forces Nariai**; the NBC's ***Moreover*** (the bijection; **"the family of horizons is the congruence"**); the **generic/seam resolution** of "causal not metric"; the **α/√3≠α** guard; the **three distinct crossings**; the **perspectival-singularity** decomposition; `cor:nonspherical`'s **mechanism** (Ψ carries no metric data; multipoles radiated away); the **three-axis** decomposition of the general reach; and **the paper's own dependency figure and 17×17 matrix**, which draw the P1↔P4 edge as **"complementarity, not dependency"** — the fact the assistant got wrong at r906 and needed Daryl to correct, because it had not read P7.*
*Propagation finding: P7 is **exemplary** in its grounding — it assembles P1–P6 as an explicit (F1)–(F6) foundational-data list and uses (F5=P4) and (F6) in its theorems, so P1's and P4's forcings are already propagated forward properly. One light strengthening carried: §171 (the cosmic-time introduction) under-used P4's empirical forcing — drawn in.*

**The chain, each link forced, with its home:**
1. **THE AXIOMATIC CORE — five axioms (the Pass-1 card had one sentence).**
   - **Lorentzian Spacetime.** (M,g) smooth, time-orientable; the standard causal structure **retained without modification**. An *event* is a point p∈M; M represents *the totality of events that occur*.
   - **Occurrence** (definition). p *occurs* relative to a temporal slicing if it lies on (or is a limit point of) a spacelike hypersurface of that slicing. **Occurrence is a *representational* property; "it does not by itself assign ontological status to events."**
   - **Layered Geometric Framework.** A one-parameter family {𝒮_t} of smooth 3-manifolds with Riemannian metrics h_t. **t is a global ordering parameter — "not defined operationally and not identified with any coordinate time on M."**
   - **Diffeomorphic Representability.** For each t there is a spacelike Σ_t⊂M and a diffeomorphism Φ_t: 𝒮_t→Σ_t. **"No uniqueness or physical preference of Σ_t is assumed."**
   - **Non-Identity of Ontology and Representation.** Φ_t's existence does **not** imply ontological equivalence: **Σ_t *represents* 𝒮_t but does not *constitute* it.**
   
   Plus the **Projection Principle** (a spacetime is a *valid projection* if its causal structure faithfully encodes signal propagation on the layer; **no uniqueness assumed**) and **Ontological Event Individuation** (link 1c). [P7 §CR-axioms, §CR-applications]

1a. **THE PROBLEM OF TIME IS DISSOLVED AT THE LEVEL OF THE AXIOMS** — before any construction. The frozen constraint is *"the canonical expression of treating the four-dimensional manifold itself as the existing object: if the block is what exists, every foliation is an equally gauge re-slicing of one existing thing, no slicing records a real advance, and the generator cannot move physical states."* Once the layers exist and advance, **the lapse recovers its meaning as the metrical rate of advance, dτ = N dt**; the constraint, deparametrized along cosmic time, is **solved for a true Hamiltonian that generates the layers' advance rather than annihilating it**, and evolution in cosmic time is **unitary**. *"The frozen constraint and the true Hamiltonian are the same canonical content under two readings, **differing only in whether the manifold is granted existence**."* So the problem of time *"is not solved by an addition to the formalism; it is **dissolved as a category error** — the manifold of occurrences mistaken for an existent — of which the frozen formalism was the canonical symptom."* [P7 §199; → P10, §1h]

1b. **THE LAPSE/SHIFT PROPOSITION — the formal reconciliation.** In any ADM representation the lapse and shift carry **ontologically distinct content**. The **shift Nⁱ is freely specifiable** and carries the *synchronization-and-rest convention* — *"re-choosing it re-chooses which later events count as occurring at 'the same place'"* — so **the bare four-geometry distinguishes no foliation and hence no global "now"**: this is the relativity of synchrony. The **lapse N is the metrical rate of the layer's advance** (N=1 for the comoving congruence), and **the distinguished cosmic foliation is fixed not by the geometry but by the *existent*** — the comoving congruence whose rest frame is the measured CMB frame, which the horizon-selected limiting orientation independently singles out.
   > *"The objective cosmic 'now' (the lapse) and the relativity of synchrony (the shift) are therefore **reconciled rather than opposed**: the 'now' is carried by the existent and the synchrony freedom by the representational map."*
   
   **And the block-universe error, located formally:** to reify M and still recover an evolving "now" **requires a meta-lapse on M×ℝ — a fifth dimension general relativity does not contain.** *"The block reading therefore does not dispense with the lapse; it **re-imports it one level up, informally**, without recognizing the formal structure it requires, and the frozen constraint is the canonical symptom of declining to supply it."* (The formal counterpart of the five-dimensional regress.) [P7 prop:lapse-shift, §250; → §1d]

1c. **The hole argument dissolved, and no ontological time travel.** **`Ontological Event Individuation`:** events are individuated **by their occurrence within layers 𝒮_t, not by their coordinate representation in M**. Hence diffeomorphic models that project one layered framework **represent the same ontological events** — the hole argument's indeterminacy is **"representational redundancy rather than ontological ambiguity."** Full diffeomorphism invariance preserved; **no privileged coordinate system introduced.** And **no ontological time travel**: the layers are *totally ordered* by t and **no layer exists prior to itself**, so CTCs in (M,g) *"have no ontological interpretation"* — CR excludes them **"not by imposing additional dynamical constraints, but by modifying the ontological interpretation."** *(Guard: the mathematical existence of CTCs in Einstein solutions is **untouched**.)* [P7 §CR-hole, §CR-timetravel]

1d. **The consistency theorems.** P1's **Asymptotic Non-Intersection** is *imported*, and yields **Smoothness of Ontological Spatial Layers**: for every finite cosmic time, 𝒮_t is smooth and **contains no point diffeomorphic to a horizon generator or a curvature singularity.** *(Guard: this **does not** assert singularities don't exist in spacetime — only that they "do not occur on any finite ontological spatial layer and therefore do not puncture the ontological structure of the universe at finite cosmic time.")* [P7 §CR-consistency]

1e. **The applications — what the projections reclassify.** **"Flat spacetime does not imply flat space in CR"**: Minkowski and Schwarzschild are **distinct causal projections of the same evolving spatial geometry, *not* distinct ontological universes**, and **spacetime curvature is a property of the projection** (ontological curvature lives only in h_ij(t)). **Gravitational waves reclassified — and now *exhibited*, not asserted**: the propagating degree of freedom is the **transverse-traceless shear of the spatial layer**, its energy and momentum carried entirely by the shear rate and gradient — *"the carrier of wave energy is the evolving spatial geometry by computation rather than by stipulation"* — with the linear limit returning the standard damped graviton, **dynamics unchanged**. **FLRW is a projection:** *"Real space may be locally curved, dynamically distorted, and inhomogeneous, while remaining diffeomorphic to the spatial slices of an FLRW foliation."* Its success *"does not require that space itself be homogeneous, isotropic, or synchronously expanding; it requires only that the causal projection admits a maximally symmetric representation."* And **hypersurface orthogonality is not merely unnecessary but *structurally disfavored***: P1's horizon geometry makes the canonical temporal direction under collapse **generically non-orthogonal**. **(r942/r943 — the section reframed to convict, not accommodate:** retitled *"CR/FLRW as a Symmetric Projection --- and the Category Error of Synchronous Cosmology,"* with a capstone remark naming hypersurface-orthogonality as a **reified shadow excluded by least-arbitrariness** — an unforced modulus of the equant's type (the shift-freedom fixed as a first principle), convicted by P6 Rule 1 + least-arbitrariness + §157, and the methodological warning that standard machinery built around the synchronous collapse re-imports it and returns a straw cosmology. The intro now flags FLRW as the one application carrying a category error the others do not. This is **§1·SHADOW made explicit in the paper**.) [P7 §CR-applications, §CR-FLRW capstone; → §1·SHADOW, §1b]
2. **thm:augmentation (necessary and sufficient, the necessary half measured).** Given (F5=P4's forced foliation) and (F6=the existent), the augmentation is necessary and sufficient for a coherent description of a world that exists and evolves — the "events exist" horn a category error, the "no objective present" horn a modal fallacy falsified by (F5). Alters no equation. *(The same theorem P4 carries — double duty, both at weight; = §1d.)* [P7 thm:augmentation-p7 §695]
3. **Metric reassignment on a fixed manifold.** Distinct Lorentzian metrics on one manifold sharing a foliation; a congruence null in g becomes timelike in g'. The SdS metric is the reassignment selected by the horizon-induced temporal orientation (P1); the Nariai member the cosmological case. [P7 §reassignment]
3a. **THE REASSIGNMENT IS A *TRADE*, and the collapse FORCES Nariai by a trichotomy.** The de Sitter hyperboloid is doubly ruled by straight null lines. The reassignment takes the **future-directed null generators whose causal sense matches collapse-horizon generators** → the **timelike fundamental congruence**; and the **at-rest comoving worldlines — *timelike* in de Sitter** → the **null photon congruence**. *The two congruences trade causal characters*, the foliation by expanding 3-spheres preserved.
   **The forcing (§507).** P1 gives the limiting orientation as a null direction **grazing** the horizon — *tangent, never transverse*. Classify the family by how the null-generator congruence meets its horizon: **transverse crossing** (ΛG²M²/c⁴<1/9, two distinct horizons), **tangency at a merged double root** (=1/9, Nariai), or **no real horizon** (>1/9). **A collapse forms a horizon → the overcritical case is excluded; the limiting orientation is tangent → the undercritical case is excluded.** Only Nariai remains — *"the asymptotic alignment of collapse therefore forces the Nariai member,"* supplying the identification P1 deferred. **The cosmology is not a member selected by fitting a mass; it is the unique non-pivoting member, fixed by Λ alone.** [P7 §CR-SdS, §507; ↔ §1i link 7a (P5's algebraic uniqueness — two independent routes)]

4. **THE NULL-BOUNDARY CORRESPONDENCE (thm:null-boundary) — read whole.** Ψ: ℋ⁺ → ℋ_c⁺(p) maps the collapse horizon to the **oriented future cosmological horizon of a comoving geodesic p**, preserving the generator fibration, the affine ordering, and the future orientation. It is **canonically constrained** (both are *the future horizon of a worldline*), hence fixed to a **single causal type** — "far from an arbitrary isomorphism of like-typed boundaries."
   - **THE *MOREOVER* — the clause that reorganizes the picture.** p ↦ ℋ_c⁺(p) is a **bijection** between the comoving geodesics (the waist S³) and the oriented future cosmological horizons — *orientation* is what distinguishes p from its antipode −p. The reassignment promotes each horizon's generators to the timelike worldline p, so **the S³ of comoving worldlines *is* the S³ of reassigned horizons — "not merely covered by it."** **"No single horizon seeds the congruence; the family of horizons *is* the congruence."**
   - **GUARD:** the areal radius read along a reassigned ruling is the **sinh^{2/3}** law — ***not*** the cosh(t/α) of the closed orthogonal de Sitter slicing, *"whose comoving geodesics are a different family."* Two distinct congruences; conflating them wrecks the cosmology.
   - **GENERIC vs SEAM — this settles the "causal not metric" tension.** The **generic** Ψ is **causal and structural, *not* metric**: the horizon areas (16πG²M²/c⁴ vs 4πα²) and surface gravities differ, and a **residual reframing freedom** remains on the bifurcation 2-sphere (which the collapse horizon lacks). **At the seam** the forced member is the **Nariai double root**: the two horizons **merge** at the common areal radius α/√3 with equal area 4πα²/3, and **Ψ reduces to the identity on that single coincident horizon — metric *and rigid***. The reframing freedom **cannot carry over**, because the degenerate horizon (κ=0, f=f′=0) **carries no bifurcation 2-sphere** — the very sphere on which the freedom was defined. *"The causal-and-structural reading is the statement of the generic map; **the seam is its metric coincidence, not a counterexample to it**."*
   - **GUARD (the paper is "at pains to prevent" this):** **r⋆ = α/√3 ≠ α.** The de Sitter scale α is *the size of the throat 3-sphere*; α/√3 is *the areal radius of the merged horizon* — **distinct quantities**. And **the forcing fixes *which member* occurs, not the value of the invariant** (α is fixed by Λ alone; it is no output of the collapse). [P7 thm:null-boundary, §509]
5. **thm:cosmogenesis — collapsed matter must become a universe.** On the augmentation gravitational collapse **cannot terminate**: the limiting null direction (F1), reassigned on the forced foliation (F5) by a foliation-preserving groupoid morphism (F3, F4), is the fundamental timelike congruence of a SdS representation of the same layer; its member is fixed to **Nariai** (F3); its evolution is the **sinh^{2/3}** law; and ℋ⁺ and ℋ_c⁺(p) are **one ontological layer under distinct causal assignments**, *the S³ family of reassigned horizons being that congruence*. **The completion of collapse is not an interior curvature singularity — reached on no finite cosmic layer (F1, F2) — but the finite-curvature cosmological seam.** [P7 thm:cosmogenesis]

5a. **`cor:nonspherical` — and its mechanism matters.** Ψ **carries no metric data** (no horizon area, surface gravity, or metric multipole), so **its sole datum on the collapse side is the horizon's *causal structure*** — a null S²×ℝ foliated by generators of one-way ordering — *"the general structure of any four-dimensional event horizon,"* independent of spherical symmetry. **An aspherical progenitor radiates its higher multipoles as it settles**, so its horizon carries only the diffeomorphism-invariant (M,J,Q) encoded on the null boundary (the **no-hair structure** — *"precisely the diffeomorphism-invariant data encoded on the null boundary"*, which is *why* the SdS representation depends only on global quantities). **"The aspherical detail is absent from the boundary the correspondence reads."** Non-spherical collapse is **dissolved, not deferred**. [P7 cor:nonspherical, rem:no-hair]

5b. **THE PERSPECTIVAL SINGULARITY — guards not to lose.** For the massive Nariai configuration **r=0 *is* a genuine curvature singularity**: *"a real metric singularity, not a coordinate artefact"* — the reassignment alters the **causal roles of the congruences, not the reality of the singularity**. But the Kretschmann scalar is **48G²M²/c⁴r⁶ + 24/α⁴**, so **the divergence at r=0 is the M-contribution alone** (the M→0 de Sitter remainder, 24/α⁴, is *regular* there). Decomposed under R (`r↦−r`, `2M↦−2M` — the mass-reflection, *not* the areal spatial parity P) — **the de Sitter part R-even, the Schwarzschild mass R-odd** — *"the singularity is a feature of the perspectival (odd) mass reading, not of the invariant (even) geometry."* (P5's eigenspace split, doing physics.)
   **THE CLOSED BEAD — three distinct turning points of ONE closed curve** *(revised r973, seating `conj:bead`; supersedes the earlier "three distinct crossings with different destinations / r=0 off the continuation" framing, which fragmented the one lap and disagreed both with P7's own `fig:dS_SdS`(f) "closed bead" caption and with P3 §sec:lap).* Because r is **signed** the slicing does not merely meet at the branch point but **closes**: in along a null ruling → the **throat seam at X=α** (sinθ→coshψ, the 2D slice's signature turns over) → around the throat **through r=0** (a branch point, *not* a barrier — the substrate is smooth there; the divergence is the perspectival odd/mass reading of 5b) → onto the **conjugate (r<0) branch** → out the far ruling. The throat seam (X=α), the back of the lap (r=0), and the merged-horizon radius (α/√3, where Nariai is seeded) are **distinct turning points *of the one bead*** — at distinct radii (**α** the throat-3-sphere *size*, **α/√3** the merged-horizon *areal radius*, never conflated) — **not** separate crossings with unrelated destinations. **TWO LEVELS, held apart** (their conflation was the fault): *geometrically* the slicing curve is one closed bead passing through r=0 (whether the **spacetime** extends across r=0 is left open, P2); *physically* the actualised expanding layers seed at the **finite-curvature horizon seam**, never reaching r=0 — geometric closure and physical seeding are claims at **different levels, not competing ones.** This is **P7's flagship theorem** (`thm:bead`, r974): collapse in a prior universe and expansion in ours are the two readings of one closed bead, r real, the phase carried by τ̃. **★ ANTIMATTER PROGENITOR (`thm:antimatter-progenitor`, r1010–r1011).** The bead's third structural fact already names the conjugate (r<0) branch **antimatter** — the R=γ⁵ antifundamental 3̄=R(3) of the matter leg, at matter's weight (charge field-level on both). Joined to `thm:cosmogenesis` (the collapse whose future is our expansion *is* that branch), this gives a theorem: **the black hole from which our universe issued was, in the signed-radius continuation, an antimatter black hole** — our matter and the progenitor's the two ends of one R-conjugation across the bead. The naming **rides the settled geometric closure through r=0** (the slicing curve is C^∞ across it — do NOT hedge this) and is **independent of the remaining open item**: the *distinct* spacetime C⁰-extension question (Sbierski, P2). *(⚑ **r1840, probe `Q2`: this read "the two genuinely-open items" and counted the A/B sheet-to-ruling map as the second. That family was KILLED at r1280 and P7 §two-sided-closure dissolves it — the rulings and the wings are not two candidates for one assignment but the linear and antilinear faces of one plate.** So the count here was two and is one.)* Scope held: "antimatter" = the R=γ⁵ representation branch (rep/chirality/mass-sign geometric on both; charge field-level on both), **not** a geometric charge-conjugation C. **NO baryogenesis claim** — the matter/antimatter relation is the *standing, relational* R-conjugation of the two branches, NOT an asymmetry made at a seam; this is *consistent with and explains* §1q's "no matter/antimatter asymmetry is a cosmogenesis event" (R≠T; the crest is S₃-fixed but not R-fixed). Wired both ways: P14/P13 name the R-partner branch antimatter and now point forward to the concrete progenitor; P16 names the progenitor the antimatter branch (identity, not thermal history). The τ̃-contour is **computed** (receipt `bead_contour.py`): a bounded off-real-axis excursion, |Im τ̃|≤πα/3, locking at πα/3 at the comoving turnaround r=−(2Mα²)^{1/3}; the geometric closure is P3's. **Remaining open (scope, not a blocker):** whether the *spacetime* (not just the slicing curve) extends through r=0 (P2); and the lap↔physical-seeding map (the dynamical depth). [P7 `thm:bead`, `thm:antimatter-progenitor` + `rem:perspectival-singularity`; → §1f, §1k, §1i, §1q, §1s]

6. **The general reach — and it decomposes on THREE ORTHOGONAL AXES.** The slicing operator's range is exactly the **symmetry-reducible sector** (a geometry is a cut of the substrate precisely when its isometry group contains a sweep-subgroup of the substrate's), filled across every Petrov type. GR's own covariance — *one geometry under change of chart* — is **lifted one level: one substrate under change of geometry**, with the slicing curve the gauge object. The catalogue's apparent multiplicity sorts into:
   - **Vantage** — a *finite groupoid of causal readings of one fixed cut* (r↦−r exchanging dS/Schwarzschild; the ±M orientation parity; the Kantowski–Sachs ↔ flat-FLRW reassignment; the null↔timelike reassignment relating collapse interior to expanding cosmology). *Each changes the reading, not the geometry.*
   - **Geometric** — the *moduli of genuinely distinct vacuum cuts* (SdS, Kerr–NUT–(A)dS, the Weyl class, Bianchi), with mass, rotation, NUT charge **transverse to the substrate's orbits**.
   - **Matter** — on a *third axis orthogonal to both*: charge and acceleration are **not vacuum cuts but bends off the kernel**.
   
   So: **"one substrate read through a finite vantage groupoid, over a moduli family of vacuum cuts, with matter the bend."** Algebraic type is **no constraint** on the reach (O, D, I all filled). **The Friedmann initial singularity is, on this classification, the finite-curvature cosmogenesis branch point — the degenerate Nariai member — "a boundary of the cut, not a breakdown of the geometry."** What remains open is the **irreducible interior remainder** (Kerr-inner, RN-interior), gated on the matter-sector dynamics. Held at **coherence, not correspondence**. [P7 §general-reach, §frontiers]

**What it roots / joints (both ways):**
- Rests on **P1 (F1** — the metric singularity, the limiting null direction the NBC reassigns**)** and **P4 (F5** — the empirically forced foliation the augmentation theorem uses**)**. [→ §1c, §1d]
- The NBC + thm:cosmogenesis is the framework-level ground of **§1b's** "the reassignment fixes the radiation-free Nariai rate" and the finite-curvature seam. [→ §1b, P15, P16]
- "Collapsed matter must become a universe, any symmetry" is the general-reach claim **p0** synthesizes. [→ p0]
- Self-consistency ≠ soundness [P6]; the SM matter sector the largest unbuilt undertaking [P14]. [→ P6, P14]

**The guard ([†ONT-CR]).**
> CR is an ontological *augmentation*, not a competing theory and not a "purely representational" reading (the paper's canon note flags copilot-artefact overshoots to the latter). **The primary existent is the evolving layer; spacetimes are its causal projections; occurrence is representational and "does not by itself assign ontological status."** The reassignment preserves the foliation and changes no equation.
> **Ψ: generic vs seam — never state one without the other.** The **generic** correspondence is **causal and structural, *not* metric** (areas and surface gravities differ; a residual reframing freedom lives on the bifurcation 2-sphere). **At the seam** — the Nariai double root — the horizons **merge** and **Ψ is the identity: metric *and rigid***, the reframing freedom absent because the degenerate horizon **has no bifurcation sphere**. *"The seam is the metric coincidence of the causal map, not a counterexample to it."* Quoting "not metric" unqualified is the error the theorem is written to prevent.
> **The family of horizons *is* the congruence.** p↦ℋ_c⁺(p) is a **bijection** (orientation distinguishes p from −p); **no single horizon seeds** the congruence. And the areal radius along a reassigned ruling is **sinh^{2/3}**, ***not*** the closed-slicing cosh(t/α) — *a different family of geodesics.*
> **r⋆ = α/√3 ≠ α.** The throat 3-sphere's *size* and the merged horizon's *areal radius* are distinct quantities — *"conflating the two is the crossing this paragraph is at pains to prevent."* **The forcing fixes which member occurs; it does not set the value of the invariant.**
> **The closed bead** (`thm:bead`, r974): the **throat seam (X=α)**, the **back of the lap (r=0)**, and **α/√3** (Nariai-seeded) are distinct turning points of **ONE closed slicing curve** — the signed r closes: in → seam → through r=0 → conjugate branch → out. Distinct radii, never conflated (α ≠ α/√3); **not** separate crossings with different destinations *(that framing, superseded r973, fragmented the one lap)*. Hold two levels: **geometric** closure through r=0 (spacetime-extension open, P2) vs **physical** seeding at the finite-curvature horizon (never r=0).
> **r=0 in the massive Nariai config is a genuine curvature singularity** — real, not a coordinate artefact. What is perspectival is that the divergence is **the odd (mass) part alone** (K = 48G²M²/c⁴r⁶ + 24/α⁴; the even de Sitter remainder is regular). The reassignment alters causal roles, **not the reality of the singularity**.
> **The Nariai member is FORCED by a trichotomy**, not fitted: a collapse *forms* a horizon (excluding overcritical) and its limiting orientation is **tangent, not transverse** (excluding undercritical).
> **`cor:nonspherical` works because Ψ carries no metric data** — the aspherical progenitor radiates its multipoles away; only (M,J,Q) survive on the boundary the correspondence reads.
> **Smoothness of layers does *not* deny singularities** — only that none occurs on a finite layer. **CTCs' mathematical existence is untouched**; they merely have no ontological interpretation.
> The general reach is held at **coherence, not correspondence** — two open tests: no-trapped-surfaces (P1) and the CMB (P15). *Self-consistency is not soundness.*

## 1f. The substrate, the slicing curve, and **the lap** (P3) — the geometric engine that points outward

**The guard ([†ONT-GEOM]).**
> **Hold the one swing.** The construction has a *single moving part* — one slicing plane (a door) swinging about one hinge — and the sky angle w, the throat angle u, the horizon angle 3w are **three projections of that one object**, not three independent parameters. Each is **blind to something different** (u to which member is in play; w folds σ to a mere reflection; r₀ to where on the conjugate lap one stands), so **never read one window without its coupled complement** — *"taking that piece for the whole is the characteristic error the coupling guards against"* (§coupled). **α is never sent to a limit**: reaching Schwarzschild by α→∞ would dismantle the very throat the construction lives on; Schwarzschild is a *reading*, not a limit.
> One curve, one substrate, one invariant (the throat α). Horizons and the r=0 reading are *turning points* of that curve, not distinct loci of distinct geometries; M is a turning point, not a coefficient; de Sitter and Schwarzschild are two *readings* (a vantage-swap), not two limits. **r=0 is a branch point, not a barrier**: the manifold is C^∞ there and the curvature divergence is a perspectival chart artefact — so the slicing *closes* through it onto the real conjugate branch, and that closed lap *is* the cosmogenesis. The signature flip at the branch point is the 2D slicing surface's, never the spacetime's.
> **r is the spine; l is derived.** At Nariai the merging horizons stand infinitely far apart in proper distance (∫dr/√|f| diverging logarithmically at the double zero) while the Gaussian curvature there is **finite** and the geometry regular — *"a coordinate that runs to infinity where the invariant curvature is finite is reporting on the slicing, not on the manifold."* Never mistake an l-divergence for a geometric one.
> **The hexad resonance is not an identity** (P spacelike/horn-preserving vs T timelike/horn-flipping: they share the fundamental **3** and complete distinctly). The SM bearing is a question the geometry *poses but does not settle*.
> When a derivation stops the collapse at horizon crossing, treats r=0 as an endpoint, reads the conjugate branch as Euclidean, sends α to a limit, or reads one parametrisation as the whole symmetry, it has lost this forcing.

**Whole-paper read (r906) — what the routed read missed; now in the card *and* in the paper's abstract:**
- **The swinging door (§ontology):** *"The construction has a single moving part."* One door, one hinge; the whole family of cuts is the single arc of that swing; **the three angles are its shadows** — the paper is written object-first, and this is what keeps de Sitter and Schwarzschild from presenting as two spacetimes: *"they are two vantages on the one swing."*
- **§params:** what each parametrisation flattens — *"most of the confusion the earlier treatment carried came from letting them blur."*
- **The hinge geometry (§hinge-geometry):** the hinge at transverse distance 2α (the vantage from which the throat subtends exactly 60°); the three 120°-separated hinges an equilateral of circumradius 2α whose **incircle, of radius α, *is* the throat** — *the hole is the incircle of the hinge triangle* — and its three sides are **not imposed but found**, each a null generator of the doubly-ruled substrate tangent to the throat at its midpoint; the six hinges (three per horn, X₀=±√3α) closing into **one skew hexagon of null rulings**. **σ made physical:** each hinge designates one root as *its own* black-hole horizon, so a root-transposition is **a hop to a neighbouring vantage**, the three-cycle the tour around all three — **the Weyl S₃ is the relation among the three hinges**, not an abstract relabelling. **⚠ *object corrected r2376+c54.19 (Daryl): the hinge is the **line** the door swings on — **three** hinges, each piercing the substrate once per horn, so the six are 3×2 **ends**. The statement here is unaffected; the counting is.* (`P03_hexagon_null_triple`)**
- **§groupoid:** the slicing curve is **intrinsic** to the manifold (r₀ marks a point on it), so moving the charting observer changes the *image*, not the geometry (`prop:rigidity`); the descriptions form a **groupoid whose single invariant is the geometry**, and its within-geometry morphisms admit **no continuous family** — forced discrete, generated by the unique involution and sky-angle periodicity (`prop:morphism-generation`); full classification deferred to P5.
- **§coupled:** σ, P, χ, ξ collected — **ξ (the throat seam at X=α) is *not* a symmetry** but the invertible continuation, and is **distinct from R** (the branch-point crossing at r=0; the mass-reflection γ⁵ — was "P" in P3/P5 prose, R under r968 canon); σ and R together generate the full symmetry, σ carrying it across the Nariai crest.
- **§remark:** **proper distance is a derived quantity**; the signed r is the spine and w its clock.

## 1g. The epistemic ground — least-arbitrariness, the modal fallacy, and the boundary (P6) — **the meta-card: what licenses the word "forced" everywhere else in this index**

*Pinned r902 (Pass 1 cherry-pick #5); **enriched r909 after the whole-paper read** (Pass 2). Dug at source (`shadow_of_existence.tex`, entire): **§intro** (the lineage — Kuhn, Quine, Laudan, the no-miracles / pessimistic-meta-induction debate — and what the paper adds; the backward/forward two-directions structure); **§imperative** (`def:partition`, `prin:imperative`, `prin:reclass`, and **the closed-form eigenspace reclassification, §108**); §rules; §least-arbitrariness; thm:modal; §ordering; §engine + lem:vindication; §reflexive; §boundary; §place. **P6 has no discussion or conclusion** — it ends at §place, so its concessions live in §boundary. Stamped **[†ONT-EPI]**. Stated for reversal.*
*What the Pass-1 card missed (it never read §intro or §imperative): the **partition** and the non-emptiness of both classes as an *observed fact*, not a postulate; the imperative's **first-order** status (parsimony, unified causes, and* hypotheses non fingo *are **second-order to it**); **`prin:reclass`** — explain the perspectival appearances, *"not discard them, and not merely reproduce them"*; the **closed-form reclassification** P6 calls *"the sharpest form the method affords"*, with the joint to P5 **closed both ways**; R3's reinforcement of least-arbitrariness; and §134's guard — **a symmetry of the world, never of the description**.*
*Why it is the meta-card: every card here (§1b–§1f) says a structure is **forced**. P6 is what makes that word a claim rather than a taste — and, crucially, it also fixes the **altitude** at which each forcing is held (coherence vs correspondence). Read it before trusting any "forced" in this index.*

**The chain, each link forced, with its home:**
1. **The reclassification.** Theory-choice epistemology is not philosophy standing outside science and adjudicating it; it is a discipline of the *same kind* — same object (structure behind appearances), same method (infer the world that casts the shadows), same data (read off the science's own record). Its empirical engine is **historiography**. [P6 §intro, §imperative, §reflexive]

1a. **THE PARTITION (`def:partition`) — the problem the method solves.** Write **Φ = π(W)**: the observer holds the appearances Φ, never the world W directly (Plato's cave stated as a map). The appearances **divide into *literal* components, on which π acts trivially and which image W faithfully, and *perspectival* components, which are artefacts of π. Both classes are non-empty** — and that is *"not a postulate but an observed fact"* (the Sun's ecliptic path is a perspectival illusion of the Earth's motion; the Moon's monthly circuit is a literal orbit). **Because both kinds occur, no blanket reading is admissible**, and reading every appearance literally — identifying π(W) with W — is **naïve realism of the appearances**, the first thing a method must forbid. [P6 def:partition]

1b. **`prin:imperative` — and it is *first-order*.** *"Do not take the appearances at face value. Infer the world W such that Φ=π(W): ask what world must exist for the appearances to arise."* The familiar maxims (parsimony, unified causes, inductive trust, *hypotheses non fingo*) are **second-order to it** — the means by which the imperative is carried out, **not the ground on which it rests**. It is made a *method* rather than a licence by two constraints: comparing appearances across perspectives and demanding a **single W** consistent with all; and the reclassification constraint. [P6 prin:imperative]

1c. **`prin:reclass` — explain the perspectival appearances; do not merely reproduce them.** An admissible W must **exhibit the projection π under which they arise and show why they appear as they do** — *"not discard them, and not merely reproduce them."* **"Saving the appearances" is not the task**: a model can reproduce the ecliptic path exactly while saying nothing true about why it appears. The retrograde loops are not real reversals to be tracked by finer machinery but *the parallax of the Earth's own motion*; **the right theory dissolves the puzzle by identifying what the appearance is.** *"A correct explanation makes the misleading appearance intelligible; it does not fence it off with auxiliary devices."* [P6 prin:reclass]

1d. **THE CLOSED-FORM RECLASSIFICATION — "the sharpest form the method affords."** In one companion case the projection is **an exact involution R** (the mass-reflection; P6's own footnote carries the canon), and `def:partition` **appears as its two eigenspaces**: f(r)=1−2M/r−r²/α² separates into the part R *fixes* — the de Sitter geometry 1−r²/α², **the literal content imaging the substrate** — and the part R *reverses* — the Schwarzschild mass −2M/r, **the perspectival artefact**. The reclassification constraint is then met **not by inferring the projection from a spread of perspectives but by exhibiting it outright**: R is written down, and an appearance is reclassified by reading which eigenspace it occupies. Stellar parallax and the retrograde loops are the perspectival case identified **from** the appearances; **this is the perspectival case identified *within* a single exact description** — *"the sharpest form the method affords, in which a misleading appearance is named an artefact of the projection at the same stroke that the casting world is exhibited."* **P6 §108 cites P5 and P1 for it; P5's `rem:P-dS-Schw` cites P6 back — the joint is closed both ways.** [P6 §imperative §108; ↔ §1i link 7b, §1c]
2. **The four rules (the operable instrument).** R1 against naïve realism (directness of fit is not evidence of truth); **R2 structural consequence over parameter fitting — prefer the world that *requires* the phenomena to the world that merely *permits* them; "this is the criterion of necessity, and it is the load-bearing one"**; R3 consolidation and parsimony; R4 against ad hoc modification (serial patching signals a wrong foundation). [P6 §rules]
3. **Least-arbitrariness = R2 in the ontological register.** A structure carrying an unforced modulus — *"a parameter that fixes how some symmetry is broken and that neither the appearances nor any deeper principle pins"* — **is not a single world but a family {W_λ}**, and answers "what is the world?" with a family rather than a world: **inadmissible as it stands, not merely disfavoured**; the moduli-free structure is the sole determinate answer, **which R2 then certifies as the explanatory one**. **The maximally symmetric structure is the unique one requiring its configuration from its own form** (every less symmetric structure requires a choice of how to break the symmetry — a modulus, *"whereas maximal symmetry leaves nothing to choose"*). The **equant** is the historical type of the unforced choice. **R3 reinforces from the other side:** a structure whose every feature is set by its own hand is ***maximally* arbitrary, not least** — symmetry is *the absence of independently-set features*, and the maximally symmetric structure is their consolidation.
   - **It inherits the vindication lemma directly.** The heliocentric structure was preferred a century before parallax *because it was the less arbitrary* — the standing objection answered *"not from parallax… but from symmetry"*, Copernicus's decisive complaint being against **the equant as a violation of the uniform motion it was introduced to save**. The symmetric arrangement was chosen because it **required** what the arbitrary device merely accommodated, and Bessel vindicated it. So least-arbitrariness is *"the reading of R2 that the record's cleanest instance instantiates."*
   - **THE GUARD (§134, easily lost):** least-arbitrariness selects a symmetry **of the *world*** — a fact about the structure — **never a symmetry of the *description***, which the constructive ordering forbids reifying. *"A relabelling the world does not see grounds nothing, and the two must be kept apart."* (This is precisely the discipline P5's groupoid enforces: descriptions are charts; the geometry is the invariant.) [→ §1i]
   - **Its scope is the boundary:** it is *"a rule-favoured theory-choice held at coherence"* — it selects the structure the criterion of necessity favours, **not one a measurement has confirmed**, and its soundness is the empirical question the discipline sets as its first programme. [P6 §least-arbitrariness]
4. **thm:modal — the modal fallacy (the imperative's dual).** From "the appearances contain no local discriminator between two candidate worlds" it does *not* follow that the worlds are identical or that the distinguishing structure does not exist. **The absence of a local test is not the absence of the fact.** The cave-dweller's error: reading a shadow's local flatness as the absence of a casting structure. [P6 thm:modal]
5. **The constructive ordering.** ontology ← evidence; kinematics ← ontology; coordinates ← kinematics. The reification error runs the chain *backward* — taking the coordinate scaffold as given and reading an ontology off it. Granting the 4-manifold existence is exactly that backward run. [P6 §ordering]
6. **lem:vindication (the empirical content).** When a direct, non-local discriminator becomes available it *confirms* the structure the rules already selected ahead of it; the heliocentric arc (favoured by symmetry a century before Bessel measured parallax) is the cleanest instance — and the record must be **shadow-read**, de-mythologized, before it can serve as data. [P6 §engine, lem:vindication]
7. **THE BOUNDARY (the card's own guard-rail): self-consistency is not soundness.** The lemma is built from celebrated successes, and reliability estimated only from successes is *survivorship, not measurement*. The cases where the rules **misled** — the luminiferous ether, caloric, Kepler's nested solids — must be sampled and a **base rate computed**. Until then lem:vindication is "a compelling hypothesis with confirming instances, not a calibrated reliability." **This is set as the discipline's first programme, not presumed.** [P6 §boundary]

**What it roots / joints (both ways):**
- **THE CLOSED-FORM RECIPROCITY (r909).** P6 §108 names **P5's eigenspace decomposition as "the sharpest form the method affords"** — the perspectival case identified *within* a single exact description rather than *from* a spread of appearances — and **cites P5 and P1** for it. **P5's `rem:P-dS-Schw` cites P6 back**, calling the split *"the shadow-reading partition in closed form."* **The joint is closed both ways**; it is one of the corpus's tightest. [↔ §1i link 7b, §1c]
- **thm:modal is the logic P4 empirically falsifies.** P6 proves that "no local discriminator ⇏ no fact"; P4 supplies the non-local measurement (redshift isotropy) that decides it. **P6 supplies the logic, P4 the measurement, and P7's thm:augmentation uses both.** [→ §1d, §1e]
- **§place fixes the spine's epistemic dependence explicitly:** P1 "stands on causal structure alone… needs no epistemic ground and is not given one"; **P4 is "the first place the programme genuinely leans on the method"**, so P6 sits immediately after it and before the cosmology it steadies. [→ §1c, §1d, P7]
- **Least-arbitrariness licenses the corpus's foundational *selection*** — that the substrate is the maximally symmetric manifold, and that the physics is that symmetry's symmetric breaking down to the matter sector's discrete flavour structure. [→ §1f, p0, P14/P15]
- **It sets p0's altitude:** reading the geometric core's separately-established results as one fact is a rule-favoured theory-choice, **held at coherence and not correspondence** — exactly the §boundary line. [→ p0]
- **It draws R2 at its sharpest in P16:** that the hot dense era is *required* by structure the programme already had (the far side of a collapse) rather than a phase introduced to yield the abundances. [→ §1b, P16]
- **R2 IS THE BAR P16's TITLE IS STAKED AT — the aspirational-title seam (r950).** P16's title ("the Big Bang as a *deductively forced* synthesis … the abundances it *produces*") claims the whole pattern at R2-necessity (*required*, not permitted-to-fit). Held honestly and fairly (P16 §verdict): the forced synthesis and He-4 are earned at that bar; the *whole* multi-abundance pattern jointly is the one computation not yet run — **buildable, therefore a DEBT OWED, not a frontier** (the buildable⇒not-done line). The title is read as the aspiration it names and committed to; the gap is marked in the open, not swept under the rug. R2 is exactly the criterion that sorts the **owed** (the Big Bang computation — the full network, which would make "produces" a proof for the whole pattern) from the **genuine frontier** (the *derivation* vs inheritance of η / the progenitor spectrum — permitted-inherited without cost to the dissolution, as ΛCDM carries its own η — **which does not make the derivation optional**). So the corpus's epistemological heart is also its honesty-gauge on its own flagship claim. [→ §1s, P16 `sec:verdict`; `P15_16_CLOSEOUT.md`]

**The guard ([†ONT-EPI]).**
> "Forced" in this corpus means **R2**: the structure *requires* the phenomenon rather than permitting it through an adjustable modulus — and in the ontological register, **least-arbitrariness** (maximal symmetry leaves nothing to choose; a modulus-carrying structure **is not a world but a family**, inadmissible as it stands). It is not a taste, and not a proof of truth: `lem:vindication` gives the criteria a *track record*, not a calibrated reliability, and **self-consistency is not soundness**. So every "forced within CR" in this index is a coherence claim at rule-favoured altitude, awaiting the world's non-local discriminator — never to be read as correspondence.
> **Hold the partition.** The appearances divide into *literal* and *perspectival* components, **and both classes are non-empty** — an observed fact, not a postulate — so **no blanket reading is admissible**. Reading every appearance literally is **naïve realism of the appearances**, the first thing the method forbids.
> **Reclassify, don't reproduce.** An admissible world must **explain** the perspectival appearances — exhibit the projection and show why they appear so — *"not discard them, and not merely reproduce them."* **Saving the appearances is not the task.** A correct explanation makes the misleading appearance intelligible; it does not fence it off with auxiliary devices.
> **Least-arbitrariness selects a symmetry of the WORLD, never of the DESCRIPTION.** *"A relabelling the world does not see grounds nothing, and the two must be kept apart."* Reifying a symmetry of the description is the constructive ordering run backward.
> Conversely, do not commit the **modal fallacy**: the absence of a local test is not the absence of the fact, and declining to assert is not licence to deny. And keep the chain running forward — ontology ← evidence — never reading an ontology off the coordinate scaffold.

## 1h. GR's constraint algebra *is* the substrate's symmetric-space grading (P12) — the structural keystone

**⚠ D.4 RESOLVED r1502 — the card-vs-heading mismatch, checked at source.** The card says ***is***; the paper's
title says ***as*** (*"GR's constraint algebra **as** the symmetric-space **structure** of a de Sitter
substrate"*). **The paper's abstract supports the card's stronger reading and says why:** *"the claim is a
**recognition** rather than an addition: the algebroid was already there, in general relativity's own
constraint algebra, waiting for the base."* **So the card is not overclaiming — but it had dropped the
paper's own weight-marking, which is what made it read as one.** Carried here: **a recognition, not an
addition.** *(Logged as D.4 since r903/r904; resolved by reading both, not by changing either.)*

*Pinned r903 (Pass 1 cherry-pick #6). Dug at source (`algebroid_paper.tex`: abstract; §intro; §substrate; §anchor; prop:closure §102; §strata §120–124; §discrete §126–131; §scope). Stamped **[†ONT-ALG]**. Stated for reversal.*

**The chain, each link forced, with its home:**
1. **The Dirac algebra is not a Lie algebra — it is a Lie *algebroid*.** The bracket of two normal deformations closes on the tangential generators with a coefficient that is the *inverse spatial metric* — a structure **function**, not a constant. As an action algebroid it has lacked two things: a **base** on which the structure functions vary, and a **section** selecting a flow. [P12 §intro]
2. **CR supplies both.** Base = the space of cuts 𝒞 of the substrate dS₅ = SO(5,1)/SO(4,1); section = the substrate's cosmic clock (P10). The object is the action Lie algebroid **𝔰𝔬(5,1) ⋉ 𝒞**. [P12 §substrate; → P10]
3. **The anchor is the ADM data read as functions of the cut**: energy ↔ the Hamiltonian constraint (the bend of the leaf), momentum ↔ the bend of the shift, the four data closing as one functional. [P12 §anchor; → P8, P9]
4. **prop:closure — THE KEYSTONE.** At the symmetric cut 𝔰𝔬(5,1)=𝔥⊕𝔪 with 𝔥=𝔰𝔬(4,1): the symmetric-space relations [𝔥,𝔥]⊂𝔥, [𝔥,𝔪]⊂𝔪, **[𝔪,𝔪]⊂𝔥** hold, and under 𝔪↔ℋ_⊥, 𝔥↔ℋ_a **these are the hypersurface-deformation brackets term for term.** (𝔪 is no subalgebra — the sign *is* the substrate curvature.) [P12 prop:closure]
5. **The structure function is identified with the substrate's coset metric — and its base-dependence is the problem of time.** *(Corrected r916: the Pass-1 card stated this as a flat equality; **P12 explicitly disclaims that reading.**)*
   - **NOT a naive tensor equality.** *"h^{ab} is the Riemannian **inverse spatial 3-metric**, whereas the coset metric of SO(5,1)/SO(4,1) is the **Lorentzian 5-dimensional form** (the Killing form on the coset is ∝ diag(+,−,−,−,−), signature (1,4)); **the identification is of the *reduced* structure function on the symmetric-cut pattern with that coset form**."*
   - **What makes it more than dimensional bookkeeping is THE SIGNATURE.** *"The indefinite, Lorentzian sign carried by the coset metric — **supplied by the substrate's geometry, not inserted by hand** — is the same indefiniteness standardly recognized as the **'wrong sign' at the heart of the problem of time**."*
   - **Constant at the symmetric cut** (so 𝔰𝔬(5,1) is there a genuine *Lie* algebra); **varying over 𝒞** (so the object is a genuine Lie *algebroid*). **"The problem-of-time obstruction is thus identified with a single geometric fact: the base-dependence of the substrate's symmetric-space metric, whose Lorentzian signature is the obstruction's own."** Read on the preferred foliation, the same content deparametrizes to a true Hamiltonian: *"the obstruction and its resolution are **one object under the static and dynamic vantages**."* [P12 §bracket §142–143; → §1n, §1e]
6. **The stratification, and where the grading survives.** The isotropy strata are the range/Petrov classes (Type O, D, I, the wall). The SO(5,1)-action on 𝒞 is **non-transitive** — mass is a modulus *transverse* to the orbits. The symmetric-space grading survives at **exactly two strata**: Type O (SO(4,1), dim 10) and **Nariai** (SO(2,1)×SO(3), dim 6) — the only admissible symmetric-pair dimensions. Everywhere else [𝔪,𝔪] leaks into 𝔪, and **that leak *is* the algebroid connection**, the genuine base-variation, vanishing exactly at the two symmetric strata. [P12 §strata; → P9]
7. **Inner seam vs wall — a guard the corpus needs.** The isotropy-jump locus **coincides** with the metric-singular seam at the inner end (the Nariai double root, discriminant zero at ΛM²=1/9, dS₂×S² — *this is the cosmogenesis branch point*), but **diverges at the wall**: the wall is isotropy-zero Type N with a **non-degenerate metric and vanishing curvature invariants — neither species of singularity**, a *regular radiative boundary*, the generative boundary where generation-by-symmetry hands off to ordinary evolution. "Isotropy boundary" and "metric singularity" are two species of degeneracy there. [P12 §strata; → §1c, P11, P16]
8. **The discrete skeleton, and its hard limit.** Inside 𝔰𝔬(5,1) sits the Weyl S₃ permuting the horizon cubic's three zero-sum roots (the A₂ configuration), plus the outer ℤ₂ — the central inversion r₀↦−r₀ (mass-reflection 2M↦−2M), realized as the **swap of the substrate's two null rulings** and the **orientation parity O(5,1)/SO₀(5,1)** — giving **Aut(A₂)=S₃×ℤ₂≅D₆**. That one orientation ℤ₂ threads every face: the mass-reflection, the graviton's two helicities, the chirality past the wall, and — acting on the cut's spinor as **γ⁵ itself** — the chirality grading. **The hard limit:** the A₂ is the **discrete Weyl shadow only**; SU(3)⊄SO(5) (its smallest faithful real rep is 6-dimensional), so **no continuous 𝔰𝔲(3) isometry lives on dS₅**. A 6-dimensional substrate would permit one — *which the construction does not force*. The resonance is necessary, not sufficient. [P12 §discrete; → P5, P14]
9. **Gauged chirality with global flavour — the SM's own arrangement, not assumed.** The two factors descend onto a spinor sector by *different kind*: the ℤ₂, being a substrate isometry, acts as the **gauged** grading γ⁵; the Weyl S₃ is **no** substrate isometry, so a family symmetry it would grade is **global**. [P12 §discrete; → P14, P15, p0]

**What it roots / joints (both ways):**
- Rests on the **substrate and horizon cubic** (P3's A₂ hexad, the pivot-and-ruling realization P3 draws as a figure — the six-hinge/six-Nariai resonance held do-not-assert there). [→ §1f, P5]
- The **Nariai double root = the cosmogenesis branch point** across which the reassignment fixes the Λ-set rate on the leaf-carried density. [→ §1b, §1e, P16]
- The mass-reflection's non-isometry of the ±M *charts* is "a property of the representational record, not of the existent" — **the "events exist" horn the foundational augmentation closes**. [→ §1d, §1e]
- The problem of time's canonical root here; deparametrized on the preferred foliation. [→ P10, P7]
- The wall as regular radiative boundary. [→ P11, P9]

**The guard ([†ONT-ALG]).**
> **A RECOGNITION, NOT AN ADDITION:** *"the algebroid was already there, in GR's own constraint algebra, **waiting for the base**."* GR's constraint algebra **is** the substrate's symmetric-space grading, **term for term**; the grading is exact at exactly two strata (Type O, Nariai) and **the [𝔪,𝔪] leak elsewhere *is* the algebroid connection.**
> **The structure function is identified with the coset metric — but NOT by naive tensor equality.** h^{ab} is the Riemannian inverse spatial 3-metric; the coset metric is the **Lorentzian 5-form** (signature (1,4)). *"The identification is of the **reduced** structure function on the symmetric-cut pattern with that coset form."* **What makes it more than dimensional bookkeeping is the SIGNATURE** — *"supplied by the substrate's geometry, not inserted by hand"* — the same indefiniteness as the problem of time's *"wrong sign."* Its **base-dependence over 𝒞 is the problem of time** (not a pathology to fix; a feature to deparametrize): *"the obstruction and its resolution are one object under the static and dynamic vantages."*
> **TWO OPERATIONS, SPLIT ON DIMENSION — never fuse them.** ***Slicing*** is codimension-one and **dimension-reducing** — *"the operation that forces the substrate up to five dimensions"* — and it is what the continuous 𝔰𝔬(5,1)⋉𝒞 realizes. ***Reassignment*** (the NBC) is **dimension-preserving and discrete**. **"The continuous algebroid is the slicing structure; reassignment is a distinct discrete operation."**
> **THE SMEARED CLOSURE IS A COROLLARY, NOT AN OPEN ITEM.** Because the normal–normal structure function is the inverse leaf metric — *a momentum-independent tensor field, hence already fixed by the finite-mode identification* — **the smeared closure for arbitrary lapses holds on this sector**, the finite-mode homomorphism lifting *"at the smeared, infinite-dimensional level on this sector as a corollary."* **Do not report it as an open bracket-closure.** *"What lies genuinely beyond the finite-𝔰𝔬(5,1) pattern is **not a residual bracket-closure here** but the free transverse degrees of freedom past the wall"* — **grounded in P11. The algebroid hands off to them at the wall.**
> **(i) The wall is NOT a metric singularity** — non-degenerate metric, vanishing invariants, a regular radiative boundary. **Isotropy-boundary and metric-singularity coincide at the inner seam (Nariai) but are *two species of degeneracy* at the wall.** And the wall's *"isotropy zero"* is the cut-fixing **substrate** isotropy — *"which coincides with a geometry's own isometry on the symmetry-reducible sector but **diverges** here, the Type-N geometry retaining its own large isometry."* **So the wall is no metric singularity *by the measure criterion*, not for want of a Killing field.**
> **(ii) The A₂ is the discrete Weyl shadow only** — SU(3)⊄SO(5) (smallest faithful real rep is 6-dimensional), **so on dS₅ it is settled *negatively***. The zero-sum roots furnish a Cartan element and S₃ its Weyl group — *"the Cartan–Weyl skeleton, a **necessary** ingredient"* — but a 6-dimensional substrate would **permit** a continuous 𝔰𝔲(3), *"which the construction does not **force** — and a structure a framework merely permits, as against one it requires, is on the programme's criterion of necessity **no explanation but a description**."* **Assert neither the continuous 𝔰𝔲(3) nor the dimensional rise.** No claim that the discrete operations unify into a single action.
> **THE MASS IS A TRANSVERSE MODULUS — and the qualifier is *connected*.** The 𝔰𝔬(5,1)-action on 𝒞 is **non-transitive** (R_{ab}R^{ab}=6M²/r⁶+12/α⁴ separates different-mass cuts; ³R=2Λ does not). *"No **connected** substrate isometry connects different-mass cuts, **but an orientation-reversing one does**"* — the reticle reflection **R = diag(1,1,−1,1,1,1) ∈ O(5,1)∖SO₀(5,1)**, which **swaps the substrate's two null rulings** (the comoving and synchronous congruences) and, 2M=r₀−r₀³ being odd, sends **2M↦−2M** across exactly the orbits the connected action cannot cross.
> **ONE ℤ₂, FOUR FACES.** *"The double-ruling swap, the orientation parity O(5,1)/SO₀, the A₂ diagram automorphism, and the graviton chirality are **one and the same ℤ₂**."* And a fifth, fermionic: **dS₅≃ℝ×S⁴ is spin with a *unique* spin structure**, and R — fixing all four spacetime legs — **acts on the cut's natural spinor as the chirality operator γ⁵ *itself***, so **the orientation parity *is* the chirality grading.** *(Grounded: "the parity measures chirality, the exchange reading **excluded by explicit computation**.")* **The same ℤ₂ grades mass in both faces** — the geometric 2M↦−2M and the R-odd fermion mass term — *"so **mass, geometric or fermionic, is its one R-odd datum**; the chiral content this parity would grade is not built here."*
> **Why Aut(A₂) is a DIRECT product:** the two factors act on **independent structures** — S₃ on the three roots, ℤ₂ on the two rulings — **and the inversion is central.**
> **The closure is on the symmetry-reducible sector** (finite 𝔰𝔬(5,1) is 15-dimensional; the full hypersurface-deformation algebra is infinite-dimensional). **The wall is the boundary of the action algebroid; beyond it the algebroid does not claim the free transverse degrees of freedom.** And p0's *"the rungs are one fact"* is **a reading advanced there, at that reading's weight**; *"here the algebroid rung stands on its own."*
> **[closure — r1095]** §sec:discrete now carries the **three levels of the symmetry structure**: the order-twelve `Aut(A₂)=D₆` read off the base-and-section is the substrate's **linear** face; composed with the antilinear reality involution `τ̃↦τ̄̃` (antilinear *and* geometric), R yields C's kinematic (FS) face, so charge conjugation **factorises**, `C=(Q↦−Q)_field∘(R∘K)_geometric` — three-levelled (linear skeleton / antilinear conjugation face / field charge), the D₆ the first and load-bearing face, the geometric factor the same `r=0` crossing the continuous flow closes into. [→ glossary §C·factorization; §1p]

## 1i. The description groupoid and the discrete symmetry of the solution space (P5) — where *chart* is separated from *geometry*, and where the SM's arrangement is read off

*Pinned r904 (Pass 1 cherry-pick #7); **corrected and enriched r908 after the whole-paper read** (Pass 2). Dug at source (`groupoid_paper.tex`, entire): §intro; prop:groupoid; prop:sigma, prop:tau, prop:relations, **prop:completeness**; prop:dim-collapse; §alpha-invariant + rem:mass-question; prop:nariai-fixed, **prop:single**, §single-implications; prop:partial (ξ); §overcritical; prop:sweep; §cosmology + rem:cosmology; prop:monodromy, prop:deck, rem "family symmetry is global", prop:autA2, rem:orientation, **rem:P-dS-Schw**, **rem:a2-distinct**; §alpha-action; §nariai-algebroid; §discussion (established / open / placement). Stamped **[†ONT-DISC]**. Stated for reversal.*
*Corrections the whole read forced (the Pass-1 card was built from a partial read): link 5's sweep-artefact was stated **flatly** where `prop:sweep` hedges it twice; the NBC joint was stated **without its within-geometry scope limit**; `rem:a2-distinct`'s **openness** was flattened to a bare "not su(3)'s"; and links 7a (single-reassignment uniqueness), 7b (the eigenspace decomposition), and `prop:completeness` were **missing entirely**.*

**The chain, each link forced, with its home:**
1. **The groupoid: one rigid geometry, many charting vantages.** The SdS family is a single rigid geometry (no continuous moduli act on it); admissible vantages are objects, vantage-changes morphisms, and the **single invariant is the geometry**. [P5 prop:groupoid, §category]
2. **Three discrete operations, distinct — σ, R, ξ — with their loci (never conflate them).** The within-single-geometry group is generated by the **root-exchange involution σ** (order 2) and the **sky-angle periodicity τ** (order 3), with (στ)²=id, giving D₃≅S₃ at fixed 2M — and shown **complete** (`prop:completeness`), closing the generation question P3 left open. *(The completeness proof turns on **invertibility, not continuity**: continuity alone permits a branch-switch at w=π/6; bijectivity forbids it, since the two branches have slopes ±1 and a switch folds the domain.)* The three operations, distinguished by **where they act**:
   - **σ** — the Weyl / *diagonal* reflection: mass-invariant, Nariai-fixed, and **no branch-point crossing at all** (an algebraic relabelling of which root is designated).
   - **R** — the diagram / *anti-diagonal* reflection (2M↦−2M, r₀↦−r₀): the vantage-swap, de Sitter-fixed, and **it is the branch-point crossing at r=0** (onto the conjugate branch).
   - **ξ** — the **throat seam at X=α** (Riemannian↔Lorentzian, onto the cosmological branch r>α): **not a symmetry** but the invertible analytic continuation; the signature flip is a property of *the continuation*, not of the descriptions.
   They *"share the single sin→cosh continuation mechanism but do not coincide as a map"* — distinct operations at distinct loci (σ no seam, R the r=0 branch point, ξ the X=α throat seam), but the loci they turn at are **turning points of the one closed bead**, not crossings with unrelated destinations *(the "different destinations" framing was superseded r973 by `thm:bead`, which closes the one lap; see §1e L525, §1k)*. σ and R generate D₆; ξ secures the correspondence's exactness. [P5 prop:sigma, prop:relations, prop:completeness, prop:partial, rem:P-dS-Schw; ↔ §1e `thm:bead`]
3. **Rigidity as dimensional collapse.** The slicing family collapses onto its single invariant (the de Sitter manifold, α); the continuous parameter is a *chart label*, not a modulus acting on the geometry. [P5 prop:dim-collapse, §label]
4. **α is the invariant; M is perspectival — settled.** The standard quasi-local and asymptotic definitions return the *slicing-dependent* M, so **the gravitational mass is the perspectival M, and α=√(3/Λ) is the invariant curvature radius — a length, not a mass.** [P5 §alpha-invariant, rem:mass-question; with P3]
5. **The Schwarzschild reading is the canonical *asymmetric* realisation.** An exterior vantage cannot use the manifold's own symmetry axis and is forced to pivot its sweep on the off-axis critical point r=0. This is **one break, cascading** — locating the hole is the single break; the partial arc and the forced pivot follow of necessity, not as further choices. The horizon-vs-singularity asymmetry is then read as **a signature of the chart, not a feature of the geometry** — the groupoid's central diagnostic (what looks like dynamical content in the Schwarzschild reading is the geometric shadow of the asymmetric sweep). **SCOPE, as the paper states it twice:** the two critical points are identical through first order and parted at second by the curvature term **−M/r³**, and *"the attribution of that term to the pivot rather than to the mass is the perspectival reading, not a claim proven independently of it"* — the reading's **interpretive payoff**, not an unconditional consequence of the sweep. [P5 prop:sweep, §diagnostic; with P2]
6. **The between-member morphisms are deck transformations of a branched cover.** The horizon cubic's three-sheeted cover is branched at Nariai; **the monodromy about a Nariai branch point is exactly σ** (each Nariai point a simple branch point, so the monodromy a transposition), and **the deck group is S₃** — the *global* organisation of the same-α between-member morphisms, of which the overcritical continuation is the real trace of the monodromy crossing Δ=0. **It acts non-uniformly:** all of S₃ under-critically (three real roots); only the order-two subgroup (complex conjugation of the pair) over-critically, **the single real root — the cosmic-time horizon — distinguished**. [P5 prop:monodromy, prop:deck]
7. **The full discrete symmetry: Aut(A₂)=S₃×ℤ₂≅D₆.** Adjoining the mass-reflection R (which swaps the two Nariai points and the two overcritical rays, fixes the massless/de Sitter configuration) to the Weyl S₃ gives the A₂ root system's full automorphism group. Between distinct α the action is a continuous **homothety**, under which this discrete structure is invariant (so there is *no further* between-α discrete morphism to classify). [P5 prop:autA2, §alpha-action]

7a. **SINGLE-REASSIGNMENT UNIQUENESS — the cosmology forced by the group structure.** The generic vantages fall into **σ-two-cycles**; the **Nariai member is σ's *unique* fixed point**, lying in a degenerate orbit of its own. The cosmological reassignment (promoting a null direction to the fundamental timelike congruence) is **forbidden by the two-cycle structure at every generic vantage**, so it selects Nariai *and no other*. **The uniqueness of the cosmological configuration is therefore a structural fact about the groupoid — "not a fact about dynamics or fine-tuning."** And it **converges with P4's empirical forcing** of a cosmic time: *"two independent routes selecting one configuration"* (the algebraic one here; the redshift-isotropy floor there). [P5 prop:nariai-fixed, prop:single, §single-implications, rem:cosmology; → §1d, §1e, P15]

7b. **THE EIGENSPACE DECOMPOSITION — the perspectival reading in one equation.** The mass-reflection **P *is* the de Sitter↔Schwarzschild correspondence itself**, not a structure standing outside the discrete group: it is the **outer ℤ₂ of Aut(A₂)=D₆**. It acts on the metric function by parity,
   > **f(r) = (1 − r²/α²)** *[P-**even**: the invariant de Sitter geometry]* **+ (−2M/r)** *[P-**odd**: the Schwarzschild mass]*
   
   so the invariant geometry is the R-symmetric content and **the Schwarzschild mass is the R-antisymmetric content the vantage carries and the swap reverses.** Read methodologically, this split **is P6's shadow-reading partition in closed form** — the R-even content imaging the substrate (literal), the R-odd mass a **perspectival artefact whose reification as a feature of the world is the naïve realism the method forbids** — with the projection an **exact involution** rather than a general perspective. [P5 rem:P-dS-Schw; → §1f, §1g]
8. **THE KIND DISTINCTION — where the SM's arrangement is read off, not assumed.** The **ℤ₂ parity *is* a substrate isometry** (R ∈ O(5,1)\SO₀, acting on a cut spinor as **γ⁵**), so the chirality it grades descends **gauged**; the **Weyl S₃ is the deck/monodromy symmetry of the solution space — *no* isometry** — so a family symmetry it would grade is **global**. **Gauged chirality with global flavour is the Standard Model's own arrangement, following from which factor is an isometry rather than assumed.** The discrete skeleton this grades (generation count, chirality γ⁵, family symmetry S₃) is **built as bound-state zero-modes of the existent leaf, forced within CR, in P14** (gauge content and mass spectrum the ordinary route); the descent onto a full **propagating spinor field sector remains open, do-not-assert** (P7 §frontiers item 6 — P14's fermions are *bound modes of the leaf, not propagating spacetime fields*). [P5 rem "family symmetry is global"; → P12 §1h, P14, p0]
9. **The parity as maximal symmetry's unspent residue.** Read through the substrate's maximal symmetry, the orientation parity is **the discrete residue the continuous isometry, once exhausted, leaves unspent** — the one geometric structure the symmetric core does not consume *at the isometry level*, and (do-not-assert) the structurally indicated home of a matter sector the continuous isometry cannot carry. The +M/−M charts' apparent non-isometry is **a feature of the representational record, not of the symmetric existent**. [P5 rem:orientation; → p0, P14; ↔ §1p — the residue is **enlarged** beyond this isometry-level skeleton by the complex-analytic antilinear face `K:τ̃↦τ̄̃`, which P5's isometry premise does not reach]

**What it roots / joints (both ways):**
- Takes P3's groupoid and slicing curve as established and supplies **the relational/algebraic content** (P3 draws the hexad geometrically; P5 gives its group theory). [→ §1f]
- **P12's discrete skeleton is this structure realized on the substrate** (the pivot supplying S₃, the ruling-swap supplying ℤ₂); P12 obtains *geometrically* what P5 obtains *algebraically*. [→ §1h]
- The chart-vs-existent diagnostic is the **"events exist" horn** the augmentation closes. [→ §1d, §1e]
- The **structural identification of the horizon's null direction with the cosmic temporal direction** is what P7's cosmological completion reads off this algebraic core — but **P5 supplies only the *within-single-geometry* component** of that identification (Nariai as σ's unique fixed point; the reassignment the unique morphism selecting it). **The remaining component is cross-geometry** (relating members of distinct throat radius α) and is **explicitly not established here** — "the present paper does not establish it." Note also that the branch point ξ relates *Riemannian/Lorentzian regimes of one curve at fixed 2M*, **not** collapse-to-cosmology. [→ §1e; P5 §cosmology §393]
- **`rem:a2-distinct` — more careful than a flat "not su(3)'s."** The A₂ here is the root system of the SdS **solution space** (a discrete symmetry of the gravitational–cosmological slicing family), the *same abstract* root system as 𝔰𝔲(3)'s Cartan–Weyl skeleton but **a different realisation**; and since 𝔰𝔲(3)⊄𝔰𝔬(5,1), the geometric A₂ is **not a realised colour gauge**. But the remark **leaves open whether the two A₂'s coincide for a structural reason or by accident of abstract type, asserting neither** — warning that *"to conflate 'not a realised colour isometry' with 'a meaningless coincidence' would claim more than the argument supports."* [P5 rem:a2-distinct; → §1h, P13]
- **The branch point meets the algebroid.** The Nariai configuration is both the deck cover's **branch point** and the stratum at which the **algebroid connection vanishes** and isotropy enhances — a structural meeting of the discrete (deck/monodromy) and continuous (algebroid) faces of one solution space. The full relation is identified, not developed. [P5 §nariai-algebroid; → §1h]
- The D₆ matter reading (three generations × two chiralities) is **built as bound-state zero-modes of the existent leaf, forced within CR, in P14** — the discrete flavour skeleton delivered; the full **propagating spinor field sector remains open, do-not-assert** (the earlier "conjectural" framing was pre-P14; discharged r996, refined r998 to distinguish the built bound-mode skeleton from the still-open propagating sector). [→ P14, P15]


**⛭ AND THE $S_3$ IS WORN THREE WAYS — the third face was absent from this card until r1883.** *P5 `rem:galois`: **"The deck group is, equivalently, the GALOIS GROUP of the horizon cubic over $\mathbb{C}(2M)$… the monodromy group of a branched cover is the Galois group acting on its sheets [Harris 1979]… so its Galois group is the full $S_3$. The one group governing the family is thus worn three ways: the deck/monodromy group of the three-sheeted cover, the Weyl group of $A_2$, and the Galois group of the horizon equation — the geometric permutation of description-vantages, the reflection symmetry of the fundamental ellipse, and the algebraic permutation of the horizon radii under continuation of the mass, one $S_3$ seen from three sides."*** **And `rem:galois-closure` (r1881) adds the fourth thing that follows: the DIAL is that cubic's SPLITTING field, so the sky angle is its Galois closure, its deck transformations $\tau$ and $\sigma$, and $R$ the extra $\mathbb{Z}_2$ by which $\mathrm{Aut}(A_2)$ exceeds the Galois group.** *[↔ §0 `σ`; `THE_MATHEMATICS_REACH` Ⓓ]*
**The guard ([†ONT-DISC]).**
> **One rigid geometry; the vantages are charts.** Moving the charting observer changes the *image*, not the geometry — the groupoid's single invariant is the geometry. **α is the invariant (a length, the curvature radius); M is the perspectival gravitational mass** (the standard quasi-local and asymptotic definitions all return M).
> **The three discrete operations are distinct — never fuse them, and hold their loci:** **σ** (Weyl/diagonal; mass-invariant, Nariai-fixed, **no branch-point crossing at all**), **R** (diagram/anti-diagonal; the vantage-swap, dS-fixed, **= the branch-point crossing at r=0**), **ξ** (**the throat seam at X=α**; *not a symmetry* but the invertible continuation). They share the sin→cosh mechanism but *do not coincide as maps* — distinct loci, but those loci are **turning points of the one closed bead** (`thm:bead`, §1e), **not** crossings with different destinations *(that framing superseded r973)*. σ and R generate D₆.
> **R *is* the dS↔Schwarzschild correspondence** (the outer ℤ₂), and it acts by parity: **f(r) = (1−r²/α²)** [R-even: the invariant de Sitter geometry] **+ (−2M/r)** [R-odd: the Schwarzschild mass the vantage carries]. That is the eigenspace form of the perspectival reading — **and P6's shadow-reading partition in closed form**, the projection an *exact involution*.
> **The cosmology is forced by the group structure, not fitted.** Generic vantages fall into σ-two-cycles; **Nariai is σ's unique fixed point**, and the reassignment is *forbidden* at every generic vantage. "Not a fact about dynamics or fine-tuning" — and it converges with P4's floor: **two independent routes selecting one configuration.**
> **The sweep-artefact reading has a stated scope.** The two critical points part at second order by the term −M/r³, and attributing that to the pivot rather than the mass **"is the perspectival reading, not a claim proven independently of it"** — the reading's *interpretive payoff*. Do not present it as an unconditional consequence of the sweep.
> **The kind distinction:** the ℤ₂ parity is a substrate isometry (γ⁵ on the cut spinor) so chirality descends *gauged*; the deck S₃ is monodromy, **no isometry**, so flavour is *global*. The D₆ matter reading is do-not-assert.
> **Scope on the NBC:** P5 supplies only the **within-single-geometry** component; the cross-geometry (different α) component is **not established here**. And `rem:a2-distinct` **leaves open** whether the geometric A₂ and 𝔰𝔲(3)'s coincide structurally — "not a realised colour isometry" ≠ "a meaningless coincidence."
