## 1j. The geometric core — the object itself, and maximal symmetry worn seven ways (p0/17) — **the synthesis pole, closing Pass 1**

*[↔ **§1t** — **UPDATED r1115, and p0's abstract and intro now carry it.** `§sec:power`, a new subsection of `§sec:rulings`: **the power of a point w.r.t. the throat IS the square of its height** (`pow = |X|²−α²` is Steiner's 1826 invariant; `|X|²−α² = X₀²` is the hyperboloid — the same combination twice). ⟹ **`prop:tangentnull`: the tangent from any point of the substrate to the throat is a NULL LINE** — *Euclid's tangent–secant relation and the null condition are the same equation.* **The rulings ARE those tangents**, so §rulings' *"doubly ruled by straight null lines"* and *"every tangent to the throat is null"* are **one statement**, the classical register adding **reach**. ⟹ **`rem:onecircle`: the identity is a fact about ONE circle, the waist** — which bounds what classical geometry can say here, and *"the boundary is not a defect of the reading: it is the statement that the substrate has exactly one waist, met in the classical language."* ⚠ **TWO modern ingredients, not one:** the minus sign, **and Steiner's power-as-invariant** — *and that second move is the corpus's own, the same as reading `r₀` as the offset that IS the mass.* Refs added: **Steiner 1826, Euclid III.36**.]*

*Pinned r905 (Pass 1 cherry-pick #8, the last — read as the foundation-synthesis every prior card points into). Dug at source (`geometric_core_paper.tex`: abstract; §real + prop:unique; §standard; §imaginary; §rulings; **§unification "Maximal symmetry, worn seven ways"**; §ledger; §shadows; §landing; §frontiers). Stamped **[†ONT-CORE]**. Stated for reversal.*
*p0's own last abstract line names this very pass: the paper is "opened deliberately so that the propagation of the core through those papers can fill it—and reveal the connections it does not yet contain."*

**The chain, each link forced, with its home:**
1. **The substrate is everywhere intrinsically REAL by construction.** A real 4-manifold with a real coordinate basis, whose Lorentzian signature is an *intrinsic property of its positive curvature*, not imposed from outside. The imaginary variables used to **reach** it (the fifth embedding coordinate, the conjugacy circle, the seam's continuation) are instruments of visualisation over a geometry **real at every point they land on**. The would-be timelike embedding coordinate **is not a coordinate of the manifold at all**. [p0 §real, §imaginary]
2. **prop:unique — de Sitter is *the* maximally symmetric intrinsically-Lorentzian manifold.** Λ=0 and Λ<0 require an *imposed* identification of time to get Lorentzian signature; only Λ>0 carries it intrinsically. [p0 prop:unique; → P7 rem, P3]
3. **The substrate is the universal standard of physics.** Its curvature radius α=√(3/Λ) and its locked null cone are the intrinsic length and causal structure against which every material structure *is what it is* — Eddington's reason Λ cannot vanish, here corrected (its timelike half as real as its spatial one) and grounded (a real intrinsic ground state, not a property of measurement). Read at last scattering, **the same standard dissolves the horizon problem**: the CMB's uniformity is the substrate's maximal symmetry, not a residue of early causal contact. [p0 §standard, §horizon]
4. **The null rulings: the light cone is the visible peak.** The straight null rulings of the surface, the light cone locked to the equilateral profile maximal symmetry forces — signature (the null cone) and curvature (Λ) are the asymptote and the waist of *one* equilateral pseudo-sphere. [p0 §rulings; → P3, P7]
5. **MAXIMAL SYMMETRY WORN SEVEN WAYS** — SO(5,1) complete and exhausted, the single root of seven separately-established results *(count corrected r1609: the card read "six," stale since the seventh face — ONE CIRCLE — was added r1134 and only the heading was updated)*: **(i)** the necessity and sufficiency of the augmentation, its necessary half *measured* — maximal symmetry makes the substrate the **least-arbitrary** vacuum, so the substrate's selection is an instance of the programme's own R2, not an added axiom [→ §1d, §1e, §1g]; **(ii)** GR's Dirac constraint algebra **is** the symmetric-space grading, the "wrong-sign" structure function the substrate's own coset metric, the problem of time dissolving into its base-dependence [→ §1h]; **(iii)** **one scale; the constants are gauges** — c the null-ruling slope, G only ever the offset GM/c² (the mass *is* the cut's offset), ℏ entering only at the branch point scaled by Λ alone (the dS horizon's thermal state closing the lone self-adjoint freedom **without a free parameter**), k_B converting horizon temperature to energy — so the gravitational–cosmological–quantum sector spends **no free dimensionless constant**; **(iv)** the cosmology is **parameter-free** (the one measured ρ_r/ρ_m an η-analogue *read* not tuned) [→ §1b]; **(v)** **the continuous matter symmetry is walled** — 𝔰𝔲(3)⊄𝔰𝔬(5,1) *precisely because SO(5,1) is exhausted* — but the complement is not empty: dS₅≅ℝ×S⁴ is spin with a **unique** spin structure, and the one orientation parity R acts on the cut's spinor as **γ⁵ itself**, so the exhausted isometry *fixes* a discrete symmetry, fermion chirality identified with the substrate's own orientation parity [→ §1h, §1i, P14]; **(vi)** **the universality of physics** *is* the maximal symmetry — one standard the same at every point, universality read as the invariance of the measure, not a postulate over the geometry **(vii)** **ONE CIRCLE — the equator is what the construction is built on** *(added r1134, Daryl-directed)*: the substrate has **exactly one waist** (`rem:onecircle` — stated there as a *bound* and it is the point), **its radius IS the curvature** (α=√(3/Λ), the sole scale), the **power vanishes on the circle itself**, the **tangents to it ARE the rulings** (so the double ruling and the classical power law are one statement, set by α and nothing else), it is the hinge triangle's **incircle** *and* its **nine-point circle** (so 2α is an **output**, the three sides the **Nariai double null rulings**, the triple angle returning Nariai of its own accord), **r=0 sits ON it** (so the three walls — the **three generations** — lie on it, their three-foldness the hole's own), and **the lap runs around it**, the **cosmological gravitational collapse of black holes in prior universes** completing into the **formation of universes like our own** through the crossing it carries. **The lines, the curvature, the placement, the count and the passage are read off the one waist.**;. [p0 §unification]
6. **The rigidity and the wall are ONE fact.** What makes CR *decisive against the world* (no knob at any rung) is what makes its matter sector *geometrically hard*: maximal symmetry, having spent everything, leaves the cosmology nothing to tune and the geometry no continuous structure to build matter from. The free-data budget is carried **entirely by the matter** — the discrete half (generations, chirality, family symmetry: the D₆ residue) forced within CR; the **content** half (gauge assignment, mass values) the ordinary route. *"The discrete half of matter is the stone's and the content half the search."* [p0 §unification close]
7. **The ledger: the Planck values are gauge-combinations, not physical scales** (cross-register, mixing thermal ℏ with real-geometric c,G). The one physical length is **α**, not ℓ_P; their ratio α/ℓ_P∼10⁶¹ is *the size of the universe in gauge-units — a number, not a tuning*. **[conjecture]** the one scale dissolves both deep fine-tuning problems at once: the **cosmological-constant problem** (no Planck scale for Λ to be small against; and Λ being geometrically primary, a *constant* vacuum energy is absorbed into the one observed curvature — no bare-Λ-vs-vacuum-energy split for the 10¹²² cancellation to act on) and the **coincidence problem** (one timescale ∼1/(√Λ c); any observer observes at a time of its order). **Honest residue: Λ's *value* is the ledger's one input scale, not predicted.** [p0 §ledger]
8. **Physics as broken-symmetry shadows.** The frame: beautiful symmetric core structures whose *broken symmetries are the physics* — the maximally symmetric dS₅, the dS₄ its leaves carry, and the exact solutions as symmetry-breaking cuts read as shadows off a wall. [p0 §shadows; → P8, P9]

**The altitude — p0's own scrupulous tagging (P6's boundary enacted):**
> The seven are **each established**; *reading them as one fact* is the **thesis**, tagged `[reach]` and **decidable by a named test** (§frontiers). The ledger's register-split is established; reading it as one partition is *the consolidation, at that weight*. The two-fine-tuning-problems-one-fact reading is **conjecture**. The generation factor is **built and forced within CR** (P14) — *corrected r1609; this card read "asserted nowhere," which P14 superseded* — and only the descent onto a **propagating** spinor sector is held do-not-assert, as open family 6. Whether the geometric hexad is the *same* six-fold as the flavour triplet (two distinct O(5,1) reflections — the time reflection T and the offset parity R — that both swap the null rulings) is **settled, not open**: P3 establishes it a **resonance and not an identity**, T and R being distinct on all six points (`verification/hexad_resonance_verify.py`, re-run r1610; its symbol canon corrected r1611) — *corrected r1609; this card read "left open."* A **full** geometric CPT is **not** auto-yielded — but the residue is **larger than the linear skeleton (P, T, γ⁵) alone** (r1088→r1102): charge conjugation is antilinear and no isometry, yet its **kinematic (FS) face is geometric** — the complex-analytic reality involution `K:τ̃↦τ̄̃`, so `C=(Q↦−Q)_field∘(R∘K)_geometric` (P13 `eq:C-factor`), the geometry supplying all of C's kinematic content and **only the electric-charge sign** closing from the field. So the geometry supplies the orientation skeleton **and the kinematic conjugation face** of matter's discrete structure, not its charge. [↔ §1p; §0 `C`]

**What it gathers (every card lands here — the both-ways closure, now completed across the whole corpus at the p17-in-order read, r922; the Pass-1 cards first, then the Pass-2 additions).**
*Read last, in order, as p17: p0's own `\S landing` (`sec:landing`) is found **already current to P16** — all sixteen entries worked "round complete, both ways," each downstream paper's reciprocal edge named at source (P15 §89/§288, P16 the lap + the produced abundances, … P1 §151, P2 §55). The paper knows its whole descent. **Restraint on the front matter: no tex upgrade warranted** — the abstract is exemplary and carries its own `[reach]` altitude guard ("reveal the connections it does not yet contain"); like P15/P8/P9, a finding, not an omission. The enrichment here is the card's own ledger, which had stood scoped to Pass 1.*

The constants are **unit gauges over one scale**; the Planck values are gauge-combinations, α the one physical length, α/ℓ_P a number and not a tuning; **Λ's value is an input, not a prediction**. And hold p0's own altitude: the six results are established, *their unification is the thesis* — `[reach]`, decidable by a test, **coherence and not correspondence** (§1g's boundary). ## 1k. The one circle — two metric singularities of one genus, and the r<0 arm (P2) — where the lap first appears, as analysis

*Hedge sweep r994: swept clean — EMPTY result, the strongest two-sided exemplar in the corpus. P2 falsifies the divergence->inextendibility INFERENCE at full weight while fencing the SPACETIME-extension question as do-not-assert ~5x, volunteers the Sbierski C0-inextendibility theorem against itself ("left untouched"), and names the two-sided balance as a discipline ("should not be overstated in either direction"). Nothing changed. (Rides the final weigh-everything pass.)*

*Pinned r907 (Pass 2, in-order read — P2 was skipped by the Pass-1 cherry-picks yet carries a genuine forcing). Dug at source (`janzen_circle_v3.tex`: abstract; prop:cycloid; prop:critical; prop:region_I_metric; prop:back_seam_metric; prop:four_regions; prop:Kretschmann; §metric-singularities §315–345; §ontology). Stamped **[†ONT-RING]**. Stated for reversal.*

**The chain, each link forced, with its home:**
1. **The cycloid.** The Schwarzschild interior in Lemaître–Tolman coordinates: **r(z)=M(1+cos z)**, z∈[0,π] — horizon r=2M at z=0, "singularity" r=0 at z=π. [P2 prop:cycloid]
2. **Two critical points of identical analytic character** — dr/dz=0 at both, d²r/dz²=∓M, r(z) everywhere C^∞ in z. [P2 prop:critical]
3. **One analytic curve: hyperbola–circle–hyperbola.** Each critical point opens, by continuation of z into the complex plane, into a two-branch hyperbolic arm: z↦±iρ at z=0 gives the two isometric exteriors; **z↦π±iρ′ at z=π gives two asymptotically-flat regions at r<0** — the continuation **Kruskal never reaches, because it treats r=0 as a boundary rather than a critical point one continues through**. The four regions at r≥0 *are* Kruskal–Szekeres. [P2 prop:four_regions]
4. **The Kretschmann divergence is a chain-rule artefact of the chart.** K=48M²/r⁶ diverges at r=0 by the chain rule at a non-degenerate critical point of r(z): it is the curvature of the **Schwarzschild perspectival metric — real as that metric's** — not an invariant of the smooth manifold parametrised by z, **which carries none that diverges**. [P2 prop:Kretschmann]
5. **THE ONE CIRCLE.** The horizon and r=0 are **the two r-poles of one homogeneous circle**: metric singularities of one genus, **identical through first order, parted only at second**. No first-order invariant can distinguish them (the connection is gauge; the r-chart degenerates at *both*, symmetrically); the lowest invariant that separates them is the curvature, which depends on **the single value the chart's origin assigns each pole — 2M against 0**. *"The asymmetry lies entirely in which value the chart's origin assigns each, not in the curve."* [P2 §metric-singularities]
6. **One category error, committed twice.** The standard classification reads r=2M as a Mercator pole (removable) and r=0 as the manifold tearing. Both are *places drawn as lines* by degenerate coordinates — but at the Mercator pole the chart **manufactures** an extension that is not there, while at r=2M it **spreads out a metric collapse that is.** Identical appearance, opposite reason. [P2 §why-the-chart-draws-a-place-as-a-line]
7. **The inextendibility *inference* fails.** The same continuation the standard treatment accepts as removing the horizon at z=0 passes smoothly through z=π onto the r<0 arm — **the curve passes through r=0 by the same step that carries it through the horizon.** [P2 abstract, §continuation]
8. **§ring — Λ>0 and the root triple.** The horizon here is **one of three**: under Λ>0, f=1−2M/r−r²/α² gives a **horizon cubic**; as Λ→0 (α→∞) the cubic degenerates, **the cosmological horizon and its backward-radial partner running off to infinity**, leaving the single finite horizon the cycloid shows. **Horizon multiplicity is a reading of the cosmological constant — one for Λ=0, three for Λ>0, the two extra roots present precisely because the substrate is de Sitter and not flat.** *(Attached, do-not-assert: on a fermion sector the three roots would be three generations — the multiplicity itself a de Sitter effect, **no Λ, no third**; conjectured in p0, asserted nowhere here.)* [P2 §ring]
9. **This cycloid IS the equator.** P2 §442 names it as the **swing-zero (w=0) member of the SdS slicing family — the equator taken diametrically** — carrying, besides the Schwarzschild reading, **a conjugate de Sitter reading related by an involution**: two vantages on one curve at fixed throat α, the Schwarzschild mass *"the pivot reading's own rather than a second invariant."* The backward-radial partner root is where P3's lap closes. [P2 §442]

**⚠ GUARD (placed r1502, from `retired/KICKOFF_GATE`).** The horizon and the centre are **topologically
identical** on the substrate — two turning points of one curve, exchanged by the involution — and **NOT
metrically identical**: the curvature is lopsided, finite at the horizon, an intrinsic spike at r=0.
***"Metrically identical" was retired as FALSE. Do not reintroduce it.*** The card's own chain says the true
thing (*identical through first order, parted only at second*); this guard names the wrong phrasing that was
once used, so it is not written back in.

**The scope P2 keeps — and a node must keep with it:**
- **r=0 remains a real, infinite-curvature metric singularity**: the place infalling worldlines **end** (finite proper time, τ=Mπ from horizon-crossing); the horizon is where they **pass through**. **Not identical as metrics** — the curvature genuinely differs at second order.
- **THE SBIERSKI BOUND, conceded explicitly.** C⁰-inextendibility of maximal Schwarzschild is established on **causal-geometric grounds that never invoke the curvature divergence**, and P2 states that theorem is **left untouched**: continuing the *curve* through z=π is not a C⁰ extension of the Lorentzian manifold across r=0. **The cycloid refutes the curvature-divergence inference, and only that.** Whether the *spacetime* extends is "the claim that must be reconciled with that theorem; the present paper does not assert it."
- **The singularity theorems are qualified, not falsified** — geodesic incompleteness at r=0 is real; the inference from it to an inextendible boundary is what fails.
- **Construction, not error.** The swept chart is legitimate and agrees on every exterior observable; the fault is **ontological** — promoting a charting artefact to a fact about the geometry.
- **The elevation of the z-manifold is NOT established here** (§444, in P2's own words). Stronger justification would require deriving Schwarzschild from a deeper structure on which the cycloid is a natural curve — **exactly what P3 supplies.**

**What it roots / joints (both ways):**
- Rests on **P1** (a genuine dependency — the finite-curvature metric-singularity species). [→ §1c]
- **The r<0 arm is the lap, appearing here as pure analysis.** P3 §sec:lap continues *this* branch point (z↦π+iρ′) and reads it forward as the cosmogenesis; P16 rests on it. **The lap is analysis at P2; it becomes cosmogenesis only at P3+P7.** [→ §1f, §1e, P16]
- P2 **raises the question it cannot answer** — why the sweep pivots on r=0 rather than respecting the symmetry — and hands it forward: **P3's forced off-axis pivot** answers it geometrically, **P5** group-theoretically. [→ §1f, §1i]
- The horizon/r=0 pair is P7's (F2) singularity taxonomy. [→ §1e]
- **THE ONE CIRCLE IS THE SEAM'S ANALYTIC SEED — the binding object of the whole layered reading (r949).** P2 is where the seam is first *revealed as a circle*: the cycloid slicing whose two readings are Schwarzschild (the hole, the collapse read on its own leaf → the **L2** face) and de Sitter (the cosmology, the geometry-set rate → the **L1** face), two vantages on one slicing, not two geometries — the same L1/L2/vantage structure the cosmology computes on, met here as pure analysis before it is physics. The equatorial circle at the tightest radius — the throat where, for the Λ>0 triple, two roots merge into the Nariai member the reassignment installs — is the **L1↔L2 boundary**, the seam; and this circle *recurs through the corpus* as the equator/throat/seam (P3 excavates it as the slicing curve; P8 the throat that IS alpha, the substrate's defining constant α; P7/P12 the Nariai double root; P9 the inner end of the null-degeneracy axis; P16 the tightest radius where the lap closes). The genus that makes the horizon and r=0 *one circle* is the same genus that makes collapse and cosmos *one slicing*: P2 binds them. *(The three loci — throat X=α, back-of-lap r=0, merged horizon α/√3 — are distinct turning points **of the one closed bead**, never conflated by radius (α ≠ α/√3), but stations on one lap, not separate crossings with different destinations: P7's flagship `thm:bead`, r974. It is one circle whose poles and throat they are.)* [↔ §1·LEVELS, §1f, §1l, §1e, §1s, P7 `thm:bead`]

**The guard ([†ONT-RING]).**
> The horizon and r=0 are the **two poles of one circle** — identical through first order, parted only at second, by the value the chart's origin assigns each. So: never call the horizon a removable coordinate artefact (that is the error, committed twice), **and never flatten r=0 into unreality** — its curvature divergence is real *as the perspectival metric's*, and it is where infalling worldlines *end*. What fails is the **inference** that divergence forbids continuation: the curve continues onto the r<0 arm. Whether the *spacetime* extends is a further question P2 explicitly does not settle. The swept chart is **construction, not error**; the fault is promoting its artefacts to facts about the geometry.

## 1l. The slicing operator — vacuum as the straight cut, matter as the bend, and the three data (P8) — where the curve stops classifying and starts *generating*

*Pinned r912 (Pass 2, in-order read — P8 had **no card**, only a feeder anchor, despite `prop:lapse` being what P16's rate-handoff derivation rests on and five other cards citing it). Dug at source (`slicing_operator.tex`, entire): §ontology; §gauge; thm:kernel; prop:bend; prop:lapse; §dictionary; prop:cosmo; §synchronous; prop:trichotomy; §open. Stamped **[†ONT-OPER]**. Stated for reversal.*

**The move.** P3 used the slicing curve to **classify** the vacuum family. **P8 uses it to *generate*:** *"the same curve, sent through the Einstein tensor, returns the entire static spherically symmetric sector of general relativity — the vacuum solutions as one distinguished locus, and the stress-energy of every other member as a functional of how the curve departs from that locus."*

**The chain, each link forced, with its home:**
1. **The construction gauge, and its scope limit.** The single curve enforces ds²=−f dt²+dr²/f+r²dΩ² — the **lock g_tt g_rr = −1**. That lock forces **T^t_t = T^r_r identically, i.e. p_r = −ρ for *every* f**: **the single curve reaches only the equation-of-state slice p_r=−ρ.** (This is *why* the lapse split exists.) [P8 §gauge]
2. **thm:kernel — straight cuts are vacuum.** T_μν=0 **is** the first-order linear ODE **rf′+f−1+Λr²=0**, whose entire solution space is f=1−2M/r−Λr²/3 — the SdS family, **M the single constant of integration**. *"The vacuum condition is not imposed and then solved by the SdS metric; it **is** the linear equation whose solution space is the SdS family."* The vacuum sector is **exactly the kernel of the matter functional — derived, not matched.** [P8 thm:kernel]
3. **prop:bend — matter is the curvature of the cut.** Promote the integration constant to a function, f=1−2m(r)/r−Λr²/3: then **8πT^t_t = −2m′(r)/r², i.e. ρ = m′(r)/4πr².** *"The energy density is the radial growth-rate of enclosed mass: the departure of the slicing curve from the constant-M vacuum profile."* m′=0 is vacuum; **any m′≠0 is energy density.** (Check: RN–de Sitter *is* the bend 2m=2M−q²/r, returning ρ=q²/8πr⁴ — the electromagnetic stress-energy read off the q²/r² term.) **"The stress-energy is not an independent posit fed into the geometry; it is the curvature of the cut."** [P8 prop:bend]
4. **prop:lapse — THE DATA SEPARATE (this is what P16's rate-handoff rests on).** Free the lapse: ds²=−A(r)dt²+dr²/f+r²dΩ². Then **the energy density depends on the spatial profile *alone*, independent of A** (8πT^t_t = (rf′+f−1)/r² + Λ), while **the radial equation of state is carried by the divergence of the lapse from the curve**: **8π(p_r+ρ) = (f/r) d/dr ln(A/f)**. Locked (A=f) → the rigid vacuum EoS p_r=−ρ; unlocked → general pressure, *"fixed by the rate of that divergence."*
   > **The *leaf* (the spatial cut) carries the three-geometry and, through its bend, the density. The *stacking* (the lapse) carries the radial pressure.**
   
   [P8 prop:lapse; → P16 §scoping ("leaf-carried, lapse-independent density"), §1b link 6]
5. **THE THREE DATA — and the third is the vantage.** *"The **vantage** fixes the signature; the **leaf** fixes the density; the **stacking** fixes the pressure."* The vantage is *which direction the cut reads as time*: where **f>0** the radial direction is spacelike and the cut reads as **the static exterior of the hole**; where **f<0** it is timelike and **the same cut reads as an expanding cosmology.** [P8 §dictionary]
6. **THE GEOMETRIC DICTIONARY.** *planar cut* ⇔ **vacuum** (ρ=0); *central/geodesic* ⇔ **de Sitter** (M=0); *offset/non-geodesic* ⇔ **SdS, the offset *being* the mass**; *non-planar cut* ⇔ **matter** (ρ=m′/4πr²).
   - **The sharp clarification:** *"The thing the offset cut loses is **geodesy, not planarity**: both cuts are flat, both are vacuum, and geodesic-versus-not is the finer split **inside** the vacuum family."* The non-geodesy is the geometric face of P3's off-axis sweep-pivot.
   - **G IS GEOMETRIZED.** That the mass *is* the cut's offset is *"the geometric content of Newton's constant"*: **G enters only as the offset-length GM/c² — "not a coupling of independent matter to geometry but the identification of the mass-label with the cut's offset."** The perspectival counterpart to c as the null-ruling slope. [→ p0's constant ledger, which cites P8 here]
   - *(Why P3's horizon locus is a conic: it is **the shadow of a planar cut of a quadric**.)* [P8 §dictionary]
7. **prop:cosmo — the cosmological sector is the same operator under the timelike vantage.** The marginally-bound **E=1** radial geodesic — *"the energy of a particle at rest where the field potential is trivial, the boundary between bound and unbound motion"* — gives (dr/dτ)²=2M/r+r²/α², solved by **r(τ)=(2Mα²)^{1/3} sinh^{2/3}(3τ/2α)**, the flat-ΛCDM scale factor. As Λ→0 the same geodesic gives **r∝τ^{2/3}, the Oppenheimer–Snyder pressureless-collapse profile.**
   - **E=1 *is* the Painlevé–Gullstrand frame** (comoving observers whose constant-time slices are flat; comoving speed v=√(1−f) → 1 at the horizon). **"This is why the cosmology is *flat* ΛCDM and not the closed cosh universe."**
   - **The single E=1 geodesic is dust collapse read inward and dust cosmology read outward — "the horizon is the seam the one congruence crosses, not a wall between two regimes."** [P8 prop:cosmo]
8. **THE LEFTWARD READING, derived.** The Friedmann readout H² = (Λ/3)coth²(√(3Λ)τ/2) = (8π/3)ρ + Λ/3 splits by **coth²=1+csch²** into *"the 1, which is Λ (pure de Sitter, the unbent expansion), and the csch², which is a pressureless dust (ρ∝a⁻³, p=0)."* **The dust is the bend of the expansion off pure de Sitter; its amplitude is fixed by Λ, not dialled.**
   > *"Equation (Friedmann) is read here **leftward**: the cut r(τ) is the primary object — a slicing of the substrate along the comoving worldlines — and **ρ is the name of its bend, not its cause**. The expansion rate is set by the geometry (which cut); the density is read off afterward."*
   
   And the amplitude/rate are both fixed by Λ **because the cosmological curve is forced to the Nariai tangency** — *"the tangency **is** the mass."* [P8 §cosmology; → §1b link 5]
9. **§synchronous — THE SYNCHRONOUS SPACE *IS* THE SECOND RULING.** The hyperboloid is **doubly ruled**. The comoving worldline is X(τ)=e^{τ/α}A + e^{−τ/α}B, strung between two null directions: it asymptotes to the **future generator A** (which *is* the per-worldline cosmological horizon of P7's NBC map p↦ℋ⁺(p)) and to the **past generator B**, *common to the whole congruence*. The flat synchronous slices are the level sets of η(X,B) — **horospheres normal to the second (past) ruling.** **"Synchronizing to B is what makes the slices flat, hence flat ΛCDM rather than the closed cosh universe."** The leaf, the stacking, and the second ruling are **one set of data**. [P8 §synchronous]
10. **THE BIG BANG, CORRECTED AT THE ROOT.** As τ→−∞ the horospheres pile onto the null plane through B and the congruence converges on its common asymptote — *"a single point at t=−∞."* **But that point is the smooth null generator B; de Sitter has no curvature singularity there.** Dressed with mass it becomes the r=0 origin the comoving reading reports as a Big Bang. **"It is the same artifact dressed with mass."** The fundamental metric is R(T)=α cosh(T/α), which **is the substrate's defining constant the finite throat α**.
    - **Lemaître's reading — flat space extending from r=0 with matter ejected from a point singularity — "inverts the geometry, reading the smooth common asymptote as a source."**
    - **"r=0 is not a point matter emerges from; it is a horizon the slicing is anchored to."**
    - **The cosmological beginning is a *finite-curvature* metric singularity** — the smooth past null boundary B, *"a horizon at which the comoving ruler collapses while the substrate curvature stays finite, the same species the event horizon was shown to be"* — **not the infinite-curvature centre.** The Big-Bang reading *"conflates the two species of the one genus."* [P8 §synchronous; → §1c, §1k]
11. **TWO CLARIFICATIONS (the guards).** **(i) The seam is crossed at *finite proper time*.** The "t=−∞" is the **synchronous coordinate**, not proper cosmic time; the elapsed proper time from the seam is *"the ordinary, convergent flat-ΛCDM age."* **"Taking the chart's −∞ for the physical age is the time-face of the same doubled category error whose radius-face is taking r=0 for the source."** **(ii) The seam *is* an event horizon.** *"Being the same species as the event horizon, it **is** one."* Read inward: **the future event horizon onto which a collapse in the antecedent universe asymptotes.** Read outward: **this universe's cosmological beginning.** *"The collapse's event-of-events at the close of the antecedent cosmic time is the big bang at the opening of this one."* [P8 §synchronous]
12. **prop:trichotomy — THE THREE LEAVES ARE ONE CONGRUENCE AT THREE ENERGIES.** The maximally symmetric spatial sections of the substrate: **X₀=const → round S³** (closed); **the null cone r=α (the de Sitter horizon) → the horosphere**, curvature 0 (flat); **X₁=b>α → H³** (open). Reading (dr/dτ)² = (E²−1)+2M/r+r²/α² as Friedmann gives
    > **−k = E² − 1**
    
    So **bound E<1 ⇔ closed (k=+1); marginal E=1 ⇔ flat (k=0); unbound E>1 ⇔ open (k=−1).** *"The mass M and the cosmological constant are common to all three; only the energy of the comoving congruence differs.* **The flat ΛCDM is the marginal member of the family, not a separate construction.**" Each carries pressureless dust **through the same bend** (the Hamiltonian constraint 16πρ = ³R + K² − K_ij K^ij − 2Λ, of which ρ=m′/4πr² is the spherical instance).
    - **Scope (i):** the **observed cosmology is the flat member** (k=0, E=1); the open and closed leaves are the substrate's *other admissible* slicings, **not the physical universe**.
    - **Scope (ii):** the open leaf is **spatial hyperbolic 3-space H³**, *not* Lorentzian **AdS₄** (which carries two timelike directions and embeds in 𝕄^{2,3}; the one-time substrate 𝕄^{1,4} admits no such section). **"The trichotomy is over the spatial curvature of the FLRW leaf, never over the signature of the spacetime."** [P8 prop:trichotomy]

**What it roots / joints (both ways):**
- **Takes P3's curve and turns classification into generation.** P3 draws the curve; **P8 sends it through the Einstein tensor.** [→ §1f]
- **`prop:lapse` is the foundation of P16's rate-handoff derivation** ("the reassignment acts on the time-stacking, not on the leaf") — *the leaf carries the density; the lapse carries the pressure.* [→ §1b link 6, P16 §scoping]
- **The leftward reading is derived here**, not asserted: ρ is *the name of the bend, not its cause*. [→ §1b link 5]
- **G-as-offset-length** grounds p0's constant ledger (which cites P8 at that identification). [→ §1j]
- **The seam-is-an-event-horizon reading** is P8's own bridge into the cosmogenesis, joined to P7's NBC. [→ §1e, P16]
- **The finite-curvature seam** (the smooth past null generator B) is P1's species, read cosmologically. [→ §1c, §1k]
- **The E=1 congruence read inward is OS dust collapse** — the same geodesic. [→ §1b link 3]

**The guard ([†ONT-OPER]).**
> **Vacuum is the *kernel*, not a match:** T=0 **is** the linear ODE rf′+f−1+Λr²=0, whose solution space *is* SdS. **Matter is the bend:** ρ = m′/4πr², the cut's departure from the constant-M profile — *"the stress-energy is not an independent posit fed into the geometry; it is the curvature of the cut."*
> **THE THREE DATA, never conflated: the vantage fixes the signature (f>0 → the hole; f<0 → the cosmos); the LEAF fixes the density; the STACKING (lapse) fixes the pressure.** ρ depends on the spatial profile **alone**, independent of A. The construction-gauge lock (A=f) reaches **only** p_r=−ρ.
> **Read the Friedmann equation LEFTWARD:** the cut is primary; **ρ is the name of its bend, not its cause.** The dust is the bend off pure de Sitter, its amplitude **fixed by Λ, not dialled**.
> **The offset cut loses geodesy, not planarity** — geodesic-vs-not is the finer split *inside* the vacuum family. **G enters only as the offset-length GM/c²** — the identification of the mass-label with the cut's offset, **not a coupling of independent matter**.
> **The synchronous space IS the second null ruling.** Synchronizing to the past generator B is **what makes the slices flat** — hence flat ΛCDM, not the closed cosh universe.
> **THE BIG BANG IS NOT A SOURCE.** r=0 is *"not a point matter emerges from; it is a horizon the slicing is anchored to."* Lemaître's reading **inverts the geometry, reading the smooth common asymptote as a source.** The beginning is a **finite-curvature** metric singularity (the smooth null generator B) — the event horizon's own species — **not the infinite-curvature centre**; and it is crossed at **finite proper time** (the "−∞" is the *synchronous coordinate*; the flat-ΛCDM age is convergent). **The seam *is* an event horizon**: read inward, the collapse's; read outward, this universe's beginning.
> **The trichotomy is over the SPATIAL CURVATURE of the leaf, never the SIGNATURE of the spacetime.** The open leaf is **H³, not AdS₄**. The observed cosmology is the **flat, marginal (E=1)** member — *"not a separate construction."*
> **Altitude:** the theorems **ground** the covariance-of-geometries reading; *"they do not entail it as a theorem entails a corollary, and we do not claim more."* Everything proven is **static and spherically symmetric, or its spherical-cosmological reassignment.**

**Altitude:** established at the stated scope (a *"first-pass account"*). Two open problems named by the paper: **the range** — *"whether every solution arises as some slicing of the de Sitter substrate, or whether only a privileged class closes up coherently"* (answered in P9: the **symmetry-reducible sector**); and **"the emergence of the bend"** — *"the bend-density identity is a statement of **what** the bend is, not of **why** the cut bends as it does. The operator generates the stress-energy from the curve; it does not yet supply a dynamics for the curve."* **"This is the deepest open question the construction raises."** (P11 exhibits the confined dynamical case.)

## 1m. The range of the operator, the Type-D vacuum kernel, and **the wall** (P9) — the answer to P8's principal open question

*Pinned r913 (Pass 2, in-order read — P9 had **no card and no stamp**, despite answering P8's range question, explaining the Carter constant, and defining the wall that P7, P11, P12 and p0 all lean on). Dug at source (`range_paper.tex`, entire): §intro; thm:bound; prop:surj; §homog + prop:ksvac; prop:kerr; thm:pd + cor:carter + rem:accel + rem:charge; §petrov + prop:typeI + prop:radiative; thm:range + cor:wall + cor:radiation; §open. Stamped **[†ONT-RANGE]**. Stated for reversal.*

**The answer, and it is "geometric and, once seen, forced."** P8 asked: does the operator reach the rest of general relativity, or only a privileged subclass? **P9: the range is exactly the symmetry-reducible sector.**

**The chain, each link forced, with its home:**
1. **thm:bound — the SO(4,1) symmetry bound (necessary half).** The operator generates a geometry by **sweeping a lower-dimensional object built from the substrate's own structure** with a subgroup H ⊆ SO(4,1). *"The sweep maps the slicing object along the orbits of H; the induced metric is constant along those orbits by construction, so H acts by isometries."* Hence **every reachable geometry carries an isometry group containing a sweep-subgroup of SO(4,1)**, and the range is **contained in the symmetry-reducible sector**. The reachable classes are the geometrically realized subgroups: SO(3) (spherical), SO(4)/E(3)/SO(3,1) (the closed/flat/open FLRW isometries), the Kantowski–Sachs group and the abelian translation groups (homogeneous), ℝ×SO(2) (stationary axisymmetric). [P9 thm:bound]
2. **prop:surj — in-class surjectivity (the filling).** For a class with orbit-space dimension k, **the operator's four data — the *leaf* (spatial cut), the *lapse* (time-stacking), the *shift* (frame-dragging), the *vantage* (radial signature) — supply exactly the functions the general H-invariant metric needs.** So the cut **spans** the class. *"Once the cut carries the class's function count, the cut ansatz **is** the general H-invariant metric, and spanning is near-tautological — the content is the identification of the vacuum members as the substrate's family (the kernel) and of matter as the bend."* [P9 prop:surj]
3. **The homogeneous class, and a vantage clarification.** The KS cut (two scale functions, spatial topology ℝ×S²) is surjective onto KS-symmetric stress-energy, generically anisotropic. Its **vacuum kernel is the SdS interior together with the Nariai member** (the two-sphere frozen at the Nariai radius). **And: the KS reading and the flat-FLRW reading are *different* SO(4,1)-classes of the *same* SdS geometry**, differing by **exactly the rest-energy "−1"** in (dr/dτ)². *"The anisotropy is the artifact of the fundamental observers' motion through the third direction — a feature of which slicing one takes rather than an assertion."* [P9 prop:ksvac, §homog]
4. **THE NULL-DEGENERACY AXIS — the seam and the wall are its two ends.** *"The reachable sector runs along the null-degeneracy (Petrov) axis — algebraic type being the count of coincident principal null directions — and the seam is one of its two null-structure boundaries."* At **Nariai two horizon null surfaces merge** (the cubic's double root): *"a maximal-but-frozen symmetry that is still Type D, **the inner boundary**."* **The wall is the outer one**, where all degeneracy is lost into the single propagating null direction of Type N. **"Nariai pins to the seam, not the wall — they are opposite ends of the one null-degeneracy axis along which the operator's reach is laid out."** [P9 §homog; → §1b, §1e]
5. **prop:kerr — ROTATION IS THE SHIFT.** *"Every cut so far is block-diagonal, because a symmetric sweep makes the sweep orbits orthogonal to the cut. The whole content of rotation is the cross term g_{tφ} — frame-dragging — which a block-diagonal cut cannot carry."* So **rotation is neither the leaf nor the lapse; it is the *shift*.** And **J = Ma/Ξ²**: *"genuine angular momentum needs **both** the offset **and** the twist — the offset alone is SdS, the twist alone is de Sitter."* The mass-free twist limit (M=0, a≠0) is not merely vacuum but **maximally symmetric** (constant-curvature Riemann) — *"the twist alone is a (rotating, oblate) slicing of the substrate itself, **not a new geometry**."* [P9 prop:kerr; → §1l link 5]
6. **thm:pd — THE ROTATING VACUUM KERNEL IS KERR–NUT–(A)dS.** The separable (Carter) cut is vacuum-Λ **if and only if** its two structure functions are **quartics**, with **the leading coefficient −Λ/3 = −1/α² of both pinned by the substrate**, and the four free coefficients being **the mass (c₁=−2M), the rotation (c₀=a²), the NUT charge (d₁), and a coordinate normalization (c₂)**. SdS is the c₀=d₁=0 member; Kerr–de Sitter adds the twist. [P9 thm:pd]
7. **cor:carter — THE CARTER CONSTANT *EXPLAINED*.** *"The separation is **not imposed** beyond the separable form: the vacuum condition on the maximally symmetric substrate **splits of its own accord** into a pure-r and a pure-p equation."*
   > **"The hidden symmetry of the Type-D family — the Killing tensor and the Carter constant of motion that render Kerr geodesics integrable, ***which general relativity carries without explanation*** — **is the substrate's maximal symmetry surfacing in the separable corner.**"**
   
   (The abstract's phrase: *"the Carter constant that general relativity carries as an **unexplained gift**."*) [P9 cor:carter]
8. **Two principled exclusions from the vacuum kernel.**
   - **`rem:accel` — ACCELERATION IS MATTER.** In the de Sitter C-metric the axis structure function **carries no Λ**; for a bare accelerating mass the two axis poles have unequal |G′|, leaving **a conical deficit — a cosmic strut — that the cosmological constant cannot remove** (tradeable only for a NUT-type Misner-string term, not eliminated). *"A free mass follows a geodesic; a forced acceleration therefore **requires a source**, and that source is the strut."* A strut is a one-dimensional line of matter on the axis — **a bend**. *(The substrate's own **free** relative-acceleration is not this parameter at all: it is the de Sitter geodesic drift already carried by the E=1 cosmological sector.)* Scope: *"shown for the standard de Sitter C-metric form; we do not claim an exhaustive proof that no strut-free accelerating vacuum variant exists."*
   - **`rem:charge` — CHARGE IS THE BEND, and charge conjugation is a symmetry.** The charged solutions are **electrovac, not vacuum**; the charge enters as the bend (m(r)=M−Q²/2r, so m′=Q²/2r²). It is **rotation-independent** (Q enters Δ_r only through +Q²), and **the geometry carries charge conjugation as a *symmetry* rather than a blindness**: the metric depends on Q only through Q², so **Q↦−Q leaves it invariant — the even-face degeneracy the boundary paper reads as a boundary (charge R-even, paired with the R-odd mass); the sign of the charge lives entirely in the Maxwell potential A** (linear in Q). *(Updated r1063 to match the edited P9 rem:charge.)* [P9 rem:accel, rem:charge; → §1m, §1p (`prop:conjugation-parity`, `rem:C-not-R`)]
9. **prop:typeI — ALGEBRAIC TYPE IS NO CONSTRAINT, and Type D is the *corner*, not the edge.** The operator reaches **Type O** (de Sitter, FLRW), **Type D** (SdS, Kerr–NUT–(A)dS), and **Type I** — the generic vacuum-Λ **Bianchi-I** cosmology (three Killing vectors) and the **Zipoy–Voorhees γ-metric** / Weyl class (two), verified by the speciality invariant I³−27J². **The reach persists with the shift on**: Tomimatsu–Sato δ=2 is Type I while Kerr stays Type D. **"The algebraic type is carried by the leaf, not conferred by the rotation."**
   - **THE MECHANISM — the shift–shear link (why the naive expectation fails).** By **Goldberg–Sachs**, an algebraically special vacuum has a **shear-free** null geodesic congruence. **The substrate's null rulings are shear-free**, so a cut inheriting one as its principal congruence is algebraically special — **this is the Type-D corner.** *"But a cut need not inherit a shear-free congruence: the anisotropy of Bianchi I and of Zipoy–Voorhees **is** shear, **shear forbids a repeated principal null direction**, and the substrate's congruences can be reassigned with shear."* Nothing forces speciality; the operator climbs into Type I. **"The shift moves angular momentum, not algebraic type."**
   - **"What governs reachability is the count of Killing *vectors* (the honest isometry), not the count of Killing *tensors*."** [P9 prop:typeI, §petrov]
10. **prop:radiative — the radiative types are absent.** The reachable kernels are the **non-radiative** types **O, D, I** (in each, I and J are not both zero, or Weyl vanishes). **Types N and III (I=J=0, Weyl≠0) do not occur.** *"High symmetry forbids it: spherical vacuum is static by Birkhoff, the Weyl class is static, and the homogeneous tensor mode is a non-propagating shear."* **This absence is not a gap but the sector's positive boundary.** [P9 prop:radiative]
11. **thm:range — THE RANGE IS THE SYMMETRY-REDUCIBLE SECTOR.** *"A geometry is a cut of the de Sitter substrate **when its isometry group contains a sweep-subgroup of SO(4,1)**. Within any such class the operator is surjective **across all algebraic types**, the vacuum members are the substrate's family in the class, and matter is the bend."*
    **And the kernel's size is set by *how much symmetry the class spends*:** where the class reduces to **ODEs** (one orbit-space variable) the kernel is a **finite parameter family** (one parameter for SdS; **four** for Type-D Kerr–NUT–(A)dS; the finite KS family); where it remains a **PDE problem** (two variables — static axisymmetric) the kernel is **an entire functional family, the Weyl class**. **The boundary of the range is the loss of continuous symmetry.** [P9 thm:range]
12. **THE WALL, READ FOUR WAYS.**
    - **`cor:wall` — the wall is inhomogeneity.** *"A geometry with no continuous isometry admits no sweep-subgroup to anchor the construction; **the operator has nothing to grip**. A geometry with no Killing symmetry is precisely one whose matter is genuinely inhomogeneous."* **The boundary of the range and the emergence of dynamical matter are one statement read two ways.**
    - **`cor:radiation` — the wall is free gravitational radiation.** *"The graviton's two propagating polarizations are exactly the transverse degrees of freedom a sweep cannot carry, **since a swept geometry depends only on its orbit-space coordinates while a free wave depends on the transverse coordinates through which it propagates**."* High symmetry forbids radiation (Birkhoff); **intermediate symmetry *confines* it** (Einstein–Rosen and Gowdy waves — Type I, two Killing vectors, **in the reachable sector and lying on its edge**); only the loss of all confining symmetry frees it.
    - **THE SHARP CHARACTERIZATION.** *"A sweep generates a deformation of **fixed orientation**, so a confined wave is self-consistent only while it propagates transverse to that orientation, and the loss of the last confining isometry is exactly the point at which the wave's polarization **must reorient from place to place**."* **"The rigidity of a single global sweep is what the wall is."** And it is **not a defect**: *"since the construction leaves the dynamics of general relativity unchanged, the radiative sector beyond the wall is reached by **ordinary evolution of the leaf**, not by generation from a sweep."* **The wall is the seam at which generation-by-symmetry hands off to evolution-by-dynamics.**
    - **THE ONSET OF CHIRALITY.** Within the reachable sector *"the swept symmetry **completes the reflection** that would exchange the wave's two handednesses **into an orientation-preserving rotation** and so **identifies** them — **a mirror, not a chirality**"*; past the wall, where the polarization reorients from place to place, **no isometry remains to undo it and the handedness becomes genuine** — the **ℤ₂ sign of the turning of the polarization plane** (helicity ±2), *"the parity being the substrate's own orientation surfacing in the freed transverse modes — the A₂ diagram automorphism, the same orientation-parity ℤ₂ that in the static sector is the de Sitter↔Schwarzschild correspondence."*
    **The connected/disconnected division ties it to matter:** *"a fermion chirality realised by a **connected** gauge isometry is rendered vector-like by the Atiyah–Hirzebruch obstruction, while the graviton's handedness lives in the **disconnected** orientation parity that obstruction cannot reach."* [P9 cor:wall, cor:radiation, §open; → §1i, §1h, P11, P13]

**What it roots / joints (both ways):**
- **Answers P8's principal open question** ("either answer is a theorem with physical content"). [→ §1l]
- **The three-axis decomposition of P7's general reach** is P9's geometric axis: *"the genuine multiplicity of the reachable catalogue is **the moduli of distinct vacuum cuts** established here… the apparent multiplicity that causal reassignment produces is **vantage**… while charge and acceleration are **matter, the bend**."* [→ §1e link 6]
- **The Friedmann initial singularity is the degenerate Nariai member of that moduli family** — the finite-curvature cosmogenesis branch point, **"not a breakdown of the geometry."** [→ §1b, §1e, §1l]
- **The wall is the algebroid's Type-N stratum** — where P12's isotropy-boundary and metric-singularity *diverge*. [→ §1h link 7]
- **The chirality reading feeds P11 (the criterion computed) and P13 (the connected/disconnected division).** [→ P11, P13]
- **The Carter constant explanation** is the substrate's maximal symmetry surfacing — p0's unification, one rung down. [→ §1j]

**The guard ([†ONT-RANGE]).**
> **The range is the symmetry-reducible sector** — a geometry is a cut **exactly when its isometry group contains a sweep-subgroup of SO(4,1)**. Within a class the operator is **surjective across all algebraic types**; the vacuum members are the substrate's family; **matter is the bend**. Kernel size = **how much symmetry the class spends** (ODE → finite parameters; PDE → a functional family).
> **Rotation is the SHIFT**, not the leaf or the lapse. **J needs both offset and twist** — *the twist alone is de Sitter (maximally symmetric), not a new geometry.*
> **The Carter constant is not a gift.** The vacuum condition on the maximally symmetric substrate **splits of its own accord**; the hidden symmetry *is* the substrate's maximal symmetry **surfacing in the separable corner**.
> **Acceleration and charge are MATTER, not vacuum parameters.** Acceleration leaves an irremovable **conical strut** (a bend); charge is **electrovac**, entering as the bend — and the geometry **carries charge conjugation as a symmetry** (Q only through Q², so `Q↦−Q` invariant — charge R-even; the sign lives in the Maxwell potential).
> **TYPE D IS THE CORNER, NOT THE EDGE.** The mechanism is Goldberg–Sachs: the substrate's rulings are **shear-free**, so an inherited principal congruence gives speciality — but **shear forbids a repeated principal null direction**, and the operator climbs into Type I. **The algebraic type is carried by the leaf, not conferred by the rotation.** **Reachability counts Killing *vectors*, not Killing *tensors*.**
> **The radiative types N and III are absent** — *"a swept geometry depends only on its orbit-space coordinates while a free wave depends on the transverse coordinates through which it propagates."* **This absence is the sector's positive boundary, not a gap.**
> **THE WALL, four ways:** loss of continuous symmetry / genuinely inhomogeneous matter / **the onset of free gravitational radiation** / **the onset of chirality** (inside: a **mirror, not a chirality**, the swept symmetry *identifying* the two handednesses; outside: **genuine handedness**, the ℤ₂ of the turning polarization plane). **"The rigidity of a single global sweep is what the wall is."** And it is **not a defect**: beyond it the radiative sector is reached by **ordinary evolution of the leaf** — *the wall is where generation-by-symmetry hands off to evolution-by-dynamics.*
> **Nariai pins to the seam, not the wall** — opposite ends of the one null-degeneracy axis (Nariai: two null surfaces merged, still Type D, the *inner* boundary; the wall: Type N, the *outer*).

**Altitude:** *"a first-pass account"*, established **within the symmetry-reducible sector and verified across algebraic types**. The structural reading — *"the covariance of geometries over the substrate is general relativity's covariance of charts lifted one level, and its range is precisely the symmetry-reducible sector"* — is **grounded, not entailed**: *"they do not entail it as a theorem entails a corollary, and we mark throughout what is computed and what is the reading the computations support."* (P6's discipline.)

**Two open items, named by the paper:** the **explicit matter functionals** for the homogeneous and axisymmetric classes (the bend is the same leaf/lapse/shift reading, but not collected); and **the wall** — *"reaching past it requires generalizing the cut beyond a symmetric sweep… **the deepest open problem the construction raises**, and we state it as open rather than gesture at it."* Two results sharpen it without dissolving it: the **confined Gowdy–de Sitter wave** (the last reachable object) and the **rigidity characterization** above.

**[closure — r1096]** `rem:charge` now carries the forward-ref: charge's even face (the `Q²`-degeneracy) is the **linear-face root** of the boundary paper's charge-conjugation closure — composed with `τ̃↦τ̄̃`, R gives C's kinematic (FS) face, `C=(Q↦−Q)_field∘(R∘K)_geometric`. [→ glossary §C·factorization; §1p]

## 1n. The canonical problem of time dissolved, and the quantum lock (P10) — **the corpus's one quantum result**

*Pinned r914 (Pass 2, in-order read — P10 had **no card and no stamp**, though p0's six-ways unification depends on its lock and P7 cites it ×7). Dug at source (`canonical_time.tex`, entire): §intro; §necessity; §two-readings; §selection; §deparam; **§lock §179**; §dissolution; §relation; §conclusion. Stamped **[†ONT-TIME]**. Stated for reversal.*

**The chain, each link forced, with its home:**
1. **The diagnosis: the frozen formalism is not a defect.** *"The frozen formalism is not a mistake of calculation. **It is the faithful canonical image of the block.**"* Ĥ Ψ=0 is *"internally consistent. Its content is exactly the content of the premise: **if** the block is what exists, time does not pass, and the formalism correctly reports that there is nothing for a Hamiltonian to do."* [P10 §two-readings]
2. **The necessity: two grounds, one correction.** *Conceptual* — to exist is to **endure**; an event **happens** and is gone; *"granting existence to the manifold of occurrences is not a discovery about time; it is a **misuse of the word 'exist'**."* *Empirical* — the CMB measures the cosmic rest frame; *"synchrony is relative; simultaneity is absolute."* **And they are one:** *"the relativity-of-simultaneity premise is precisely what licenses the reification of the block… Declining the block (the conceptual correction) and admitting the cosmic rest frame (the empirical correction) are **the same move**."* [P10 §necessity; ← §1d, §1c]
3. **THE SELECTION IS THE WHOLE OF THE MOVE.** The bare ADM machinery *"propagates canonical data along whatever foliation one supplies… **it has no preferred time of its own, and in this exact sense the bare formalism contains no resolution of the problem of time and no obstruction to one.** Everything turns on what is fed to it."*
   > *"There is no separate 'canonical machinery' result to be proved here; the deparametrization that follows is standard once a clock is in hand… **The move is the selection. Everything else is reading the textbook on it.**"*
   
   **GUARD:** the representational freedom among Lorentzian metrics *is* a genuine gauge symmetry organized by the description groupoid — **but the cosmic foliation is *not one of its orbits to be quotiented away*: "it is fixed by the ontology the gauge freedom is defined over."** [P10 §selection; → §1i]
4. **Deparametrization and the true Hamiltonian.** With p_τ the momentum conjugate to cosmic time, the scalar constraint takes the form **p_τ + H_phys(q^A,p_A) = 0** — *the same constraint that, read with no preferred foliation, gives ĤΨ=0.* Read with the clock selected it is solved for the generator: **i ∂_τ Ψ = Ĥ_phys Ψ**, *"a genuine time-dependent Schrödinger equation generating unitary evolution in cosmic time."* **"Deparametrization here is not a trick that restores a time that was really absent; it is the formal face of a time that was never lost."** [P10 §deparam]
   - **The minisuperspace illustration and its limits.** Flat FL minisuperspace with a Brown–Kuchař dust clock returns the flat-ΛCDM Friedmann equation and the **sinh^{2/3}** law. *Two honest qualifications:* it is **a toy** (homogeneous truncation; operator ordering and the half-line self-adjoint extension unsettled by the reduction), and — **a feature, not a gap** — **the CR cosmology is non-singular**, so *"the usual motivation for quantizing minisuperspace, to resolve an initial singularity, **does not arise**. The point is not singularity avoidance but that, on the selected foliation, the canonical generator **evolves rather than annihilates**."*
5. **THE CLOSED-S³ LIFT — the graviton tower.** The layer's propagating degree of freedom is the **transverse-traceless shear**. On the reassigned de Sitter background (closed synchronous slicing, a(T)=α cosh(T/α)), TT perturbations decompose into S³ tensor harmonics (μ_n²=n(n+2)−2, n≥2), each mode a harmonic oscillator with time-dependent mass a³ and frequency μ_n/a. Deparametrized: **a unitary evolution of a *discrete* tower of oscillators, one per harmonic** — each manifestly self-adjoint on L²(ℝ); **the closed topology enters as the discreteness of the tower.**
   - **TWO READINGS OF ONE TOWER.** In a fundamental observer's frame the same tower runs on **a(τ) = (2Mα²)^{1/3} sinh^{2/3}(3τ/2α)**, the discrete index passing to a continuous wavenumber. **What distinguishes them is the bend:** the observer's Friedmann readout carries a pressureless **2M/a³** absent from the background's pure-Λ expansion. **"The matter the observer attributes to the graviton's background is thus the bend of the slicing — a feature of the projection, not of the layer."**
   - **GUARD: one congruence, two synchronizations.** *"It is one congruence, carrying the flat-ΛCDM and the closed-S³ slicings as **two synchronizations of itself, not two frames**."* [P10 §lock; → §1l, §1e]
6. **Λ>0 FORCES THE EXTERNAL CLOCK.** *"The necessity of the external clock is sharpest here: an internal (area) clock is available at Λ=0 and would serve, but **at Λ>0 it no longer separates the dynamics, and the absolute cosmic time is forced**."* Not a preference — a necessity. [P10 §lock]
7. **THE QUANTUM LOCK — the corpus's one quantum result, and it closes *without a free parameter*.**
   - **Ordering cannot decide it.** In the geodesic coordinate x∝a^{3/2} the kinetic operator carries an inverse-square term γ/x² whose coefficient, **across the natural ordering family, attains a maximum γ=¼ — strictly below the essential-self-adjointness threshold γ=¾** — so the origin is **limit-circle for every ordering**, while the −(Λ/8π)a³ term maps to the marginal −x² and is **limit-point at a→∞**, forcing no condition there. **Deficiency indices (1,1), independently of ordering.** *"The ordering ambiguity cannot render the quantization unique; the residual reduces to the physical choice of that boundary condition."*
   - **The boundary is not a generic endpoint.** **a=0 is the de Sitter cosmological horizon** — *"the smooth, finite-curvature metric singularity at which the comoving congruence's past null ruling closes, the substrate curvature staying finite while the comoving ruler collapses."* A Killing horizon of surface gravity κ carries a **Hartle–Hawking thermal state** — the unique state regular in the Euclidean continuation, free of a conical defect. For the dS horizon **κ = 1/α**, β=2πα, **T = ℏ/2πα k_B** (Gibbons–Hawking).
   - **Euclidean regularity *is* the Friedrichs extension.** It retains the regular indicial branch x^{½+ν}, ν=√(γ+¼)<1, and excludes the irregular admixture x^{½−ν} — *"whose presence is a conical defect: an off-temperature state."* **"The deficiency-(1,1) freedom is thereby not merely parametrized but *closed*, and closed *without a free parameter*, since the de Sitter horizon fixes one temperature."**
   - > **"ℏ enters the gravitational sector here, at the branch point, scaled by Λ alone — the quantum instance of the substrate's constant ledger, in which the fundamental constants enter as unit gauges over the single scale and the one place a free quantum parameter could sit is closed by the horizon's own thermal state."** [→ §1j link 7, the constants ledger]
   - **The coupled sector, and what is *actually* open.** Deparametrizability is **not at risk** (the Brown–Kuchař dust momentum enters the constraint **linearly whatever the graviton content**, so the constraint solves for a true Hamiltonian **at every order**; the coupling enters H_phys as interaction terms, *"not as an obstruction"*). But each mode's kinetic term promotes the boundary coefficient from the c-number γ≤¼ to an **operator Γ̂** on the tower, **straddling the ¾ threshold**; commuting with the radial part, −∂ₓ²+Γ̂/x² decomposes as a **direct integral**: essentially self-adjoint where Γ̂≥¾ (*the graviton momentum makes the origin limit-point and removes the boundary freedom*), limit-circle where Γ̂<¾. **The same thermal regularity supplies the condition on the sub-threshold fibres at the one horizon period β=2πα** — *"the surface gravity belongs to the background horizon, not to the graviton content, and so is common to every fibre."*
   - **So what remains open is *not* the boundary condition but the DEFINITION OF THE INTERACTING TOWER** — the spectrum of Γ̂ (including whether it is bounded below) and the ultraviolet definition of the tower sums. **"The standard problem of the interacting theory rather than a residual freedom in the quantization at the boundary."** *"The propagating sector itself is a clean, unitary, deparametrized evolution."* [P10 §lock §179]
8. **DISSOLUTION, NOT SOLUTION — and the warrant.** *"A solution would add structure to make a defective formalism work. **A dissolution removes a mistaken premise and finds the formalism was working all along.**"* **One ontological correction, two canonical payoffs:** the same correction that turns the frozen constraint into a true Hamiltonian **also dissolves the inference that gravitational collapse completes in finite cosmic time** — *"both inferences rest on granting existence to a manifold of occurrences that no observer's present ever contains."*
   - **The warrant (the answer to the standard objection).** *"We claim **no novelty in the mechanism**."* The **multiple-choice problem** — many candidate internal times, inequivalent quantizations, no principle to prefer one — *"has force **precisely when the choice of clock is a matter of formal convenience internal to the theory**. It loses that force when the clock is fixed **from outside** the formalism by ontology and observation."* **"What CR adds is not a mechanism but a warrant."** Against Page–Wootters, Rovelli, Barbour: *"We do not claim these programmes are internally flawed. We claim that the premise they share — that there is no objective cosmic now — **is the very premise the CMB refutes**."* [P10 §dissolution, §relation]

**What it roots / joints (both ways):**
- **P7 dissolves the problem of time at the *axioms*; P10 carries out the *construction*.** Two faces of one result. [→ §1e link 1a]
- **P12: the problem of time is the base-dependence of the substrate's coset metric**; P10's selection *"is what deparametrizes it away — **the structural and the canonical sides of one dissolution**."* [→ §1h link 5]
- **The lock is p0's constants-ledger link (iii)** — the one place a free quantum parameter could sit, closed by the horizon's thermal state. [→ §1j]
- **Matter as the bend of the slicing** reappears here in the graviton's two readings. [→ §1l, §1e]
- **The same deparametrized structure the dynamics paper exhibits for the confined polarized wave** — here for the cosmological sector and the full tower. [→ P11]
- **The cosmogenesis re-expansion is one such advance**, generated by this true Hamiltonian *"rather than frozen."* [→ P16]

**The guard ([†ONT-TIME]).**
> **The frozen constraint is not a defect — it is the faithful canonical image of the block.** The equations never change; **what changes is the answer to "what exists."**
> **THE MOVE IS THE SELECTION.** *"Everything else is reading the textbook on it."* Claim no novelty in the mechanism — **CR supplies a *warrant*, not a mechanism**, and the multiple-choice problem is thereby *dissolved*, not solved within the space of formal clocks.
> **The cosmic foliation is NOT one of the groupoid's gauge orbits** to be quotiented away — *"it is fixed by the ontology the gauge freedom is defined over."*
> **One congruence, two synchronizations.** Flat-ΛCDM and closed-S³ are **not two frames**. And **matter is the bend of the slicing** — a feature of the projection, not of the layer.
> **Λ>0 forces the external clock** (an internal area clock serves at Λ=0 and fails at Λ>0).
> **THE LOCK: deficiency (1,1) independently of ordering (γ≤¼ < ¾); a=0 is the de Sitter horizon; its Hartle–Hawking state at κ=1/α selects the Friedrichs extension. The freedom is CLOSED — and closed WITHOUT A FREE PARAMETER. ℏ enters at the branch point, scaled by Λ alone.**
> **What is open is the *definition of the interacting tower*, not the boundary condition.** Deparametrizability is **not at risk** at any order (the dust momentum enters linearly). Do not report the coupled sector as a residual quantization freedom — the thermal condition holds fibre by fibre.
> **There is no initial singularity to resolve** — the CR cosmology is non-singular, so the usual motive for quantizing minisuperspace does not arise. The point is that the generator **evolves rather than annihilates**.

**Altitude:** the deparametrization is **standard as a technique** — *"we claim no novelty in the mechanism."* The paper's content is **the selection**, on grounds **ontological and empirical rather than formal**. The minisuperspace realization is explicitly a **toy**; the closed-S³ lift is *"the substantive canonical content."* The one genuinely open item is the **interacting tower as a defined theory** — met *"at its boundary face."*
