## 1o. Why the cut bends — the true-Hamiltonian flow, the wall as a *generative* boundary, and the chirality criterion computed (P11)

*Pinned r915 (Pass 2, in-order read — P11 had **no card and no stamp**, though it answers P8's deepest open question and supplies the chirality criterion P9 defers to it). Dug at source (`dynamics_paper.tex`, entire): §intro; §gowdy (+ the two regimes); §nonlinear + prop:admit; §strata + prop:twoKV; §wall + prop:wall; §chirality; §discrete; §scope. Stamped **[†ONT-DYN]**. Stated for reversal.*

**The answer to P8's deepest open question.** P8: *"the bend-density identity is a statement of **what** the bend is, not of **why** the cut bends as it does… the deepest open question the construction raises."* **P11: "The static operator says *what* a bend is — matter, the anchor's image. The dynamical question is *how* a bend evolves."** Answered *"in the regime where the answer can be made concrete and exact."*

**The chain, each link forced, with its home:**
1. **Three ingredients assembled** — the **anchor** (cut → stress-energy; energy sector = the Hamiltonian constraint, momentum sector = the bend in the shift) [P8, P9]; the **cosmic clock** (empirically forced, deparametrizing to a true Hamiltonian) [P4, P10, P7]; the **stratification** (the range's symmetry classes as isotropy strata, from the maximally symmetric vacuum down to the wall) [P9].
2. **The confined bend: a polarized Gowdy–de Sitter wave.** ψ is the single transverse-traceless polarization — **the leaf's shear, the propagating graviton**. Einstein+Λ reduces to WAVE, AREA, ENERGY, MOMENTUM. **ENERGY and MOMENTUM are *not* evolution equations: they are exactly the ADM Hamiltonian and momentum constraints** — and they say *"the wave's energy and momentum are carried **entirely by the shear of the spatial leaf**."* **"This is the anchor at work in the dynamical setting: matter and radiation are the bend, and here the bend is the leaf's transverse-traceless shear."** [P11 §gowdy]
3. **A true Hamiltonian, not a frozen constraint.** The lapse **N=e^{γ−ψ}** is *"not a free multiplier to be gauged away but the metrical rate at which the existing layer advances, dτ=N dt."* Deparametrized: **i ∂_τΨ = Ĥ_phys Ψ** — *"the same content as the minisuperspace deparametrization, now carrying a **local propagating degree of freedom**: the same absolute foliation that makes flat ΛCDM a true-Hamiltonian flow makes the graviton's confined polarization a true-Hamiltonian flow."* [P11 §gowdy; ↔ §1n]
4. **THE TWO REGIMES — Λ>0 forces the clock, and positively.** At Λ=0 the area R serves as an **internal clock** and WAVE becomes the cylindrical (Bessel) equation. **At Λ>0 that construction fails** — R no longer separates the dynamics; the area-clock R=t is *inconsistent* (AREA then forces 0=2Λt e^{2(γ−ψ)}≠0) — *"and the substrate's cosmic time is the clock that replaces it, **established positively and not merely by the failure of the alternative**."* **"The absolute foliation is not a convenience but the structure on which the constraint becomes a true Hamiltonian, and it earns that necessity precisely where an internal clock breaks down."** *(The field-theoretic twin of §1n link 6.)* [P11 §gowdy §111, §nonlinear §119]
5. **Cosmic no-hair, exactly.** AREA−2·WAVE cancels the Λ source identically, leaving the conserved **canonical shear charge C₀ = R_t − 2R ψ_t**. With V the spatial volume, **ds/dτ = −C₀/2V**: as the background expands the anisotropy rate decays as **the inverse volume**. *"The isotropic de Sitter background is an attractor, the shape freezing as the expansion-rate anisotropy redshifts away."* [P11 §nonlinear]
6. **THE GRAVITON IS EXACTLY MASSLESS — a computation, not an assertion.** Linearizing on the fixed TT background gives an effective mass **m²=2Λ=6H²** (principal series). **But restoring the constraint back-reaction (δγ, δR) and reducing to the gauge-invariant Q yields the *massless* minimally-coupled de Sitter Mukhanov equation, with no a²m² term.** *"The physical effective mass is therefore **exactly zero** — the apparent shift 6H²→4H² is a gauge/truncation artifact, the ∝a solution being pure residual gauge."* Q is bounded, decaying as a⁻². *"Established at the gauge-invariant level **by computation, not merely asserted robust to an ambiguous shift**."* [P11 §nonlinear]
7. **Two exact structures beyond the Bianchi count.** (i) The shear charge **extends to the full inhomogeneous system**: AREA−2·WAVE *is identically a continuity equation*, so ∫(R_t−2Rψ_t)dz is conserved **in the full propagating model**, not only the homogeneous sector. (ii) The graviton's reduced energy **e_grav = 2R(ψ_t²+ψ_z²) ≥ 0 is positive-definite**, so the propagating sector is **ghost-free to all orders**. *"These close the ghost and zero-mode runaways exactly."* [P11 §nonlinear]
8. **THE RESIDUAL IS NOT OPEN — and energy is the wrong instrument.** The one structure the exact charges do not close is a **propagating nonlinear parametric resonance**; and *"the natural monotone instrument fails for a precise reason: **the total field energy grows as 2Λa⁴, carrying the background's own expanding energy**, so no total energy is monotone."* The right object is the **gauge-invariant perturbation energy**, decaying as a⁻² with the monotone (never periodic) Mukhanov coefficient — excluding single-mode resonance at linear order.
   - **The residual just isolated *is* the future stability of de Sitter, "and at the level of the physics it is a settled theorem rather than an open frontier."** **Friedrich**: nonlinear stability of de Sitter in vacuum with Λ>0, small data, by the **conformal field equations** — *"which is also why no exact energy in this model closes the question: **energy is the wrong instrument**, and the failure of any monotone energy above is the expected shape of that fact, not a sign of openness."* **Andréasson–Ringström** extend cosmic no-hair and future stability to **all data in the T³-Gowdy class with Λ>0** (with Vlasov matter — *"a corroboration rather than a vacuum theorem"*). **Beyer**: the one non-generic exception, the **Nariai branch**, unstable within the Gowdy class — *"precisely the locus the range construction already isolates as the no-pivot seam."*
   - *"This is **convergence across results** — vacuum small-data, all-data Gowdy with matter, vacuum Nariai-genericity — rather than a single theorem covering the exact vacuum polarized Gowdy–Λ case, and the in-model computations are the right in-model rigor the theorems **ground rather than replace**."* [P11 §nonlinear]
9. **prop:admit — the dynamics ADMITS, it does not FORCE.** *"In the symmetry-reducible sector the nonlinear Λ>0 dynamics of the de Sitter cut admits a clean, consistent classical evolution, governed by a deparametrized true Hamiltonian on the substrate's cosmic foliation, with no obstruction at any order and unitary quantization in every regime that can be checked. **No feature of the continuous dynamics *forces* a quantum structure; the dynamics *admits* quantization, as the Λ=0 case does.**"*
   - **Two cautions.** *"This is **not** a constructed full non-perturbative quantization — that object is neither built here nor needed for the verdict; and it is **not** the claim that no structure could ever be forced, only that no **classical dynamical** obstruction exists, so that **any forcing must live elsewhere than in the continuous evolution**."* **The one remaining candidate is the discrete root structure** (the horizon cubic's S₃ and its A₂) — *"a separate, discrete object rather than a feature of the continuous dynamics"* — developed in **P12**, not here. [P11 prop:admit, §145; → §1h]
10. **prop:twoKV — the Type-I edge.** The polarized Gowdy–de Sitter metric with a generic ψ(t,z) admits **exactly two Killing vectors** (∂_x, ∂_y), so it occupies the **two-Killing-vector (Type-I) stratum — the last confined stratum before the wall.** *"The wave is the worked first **bend** at the last confined stratum: a single residual symmetry (the T²) still pins the polarization to a fixed global orientation."* [P11 prop:twoKV]
11. **prop:wall — THE WALL IS NOT A METRIC SINGULARITY, of either species.** A metric singularity (P1's sense) is **a collapse of the metric's *measure***: a null hypersurface along whose generators the spatial extent contracts to zero. **A Killing horizon is the worked sufficient case, not the definition.** The wall is a Type-N plane-fronted (Brinkmann) wave: its metric is **non-degenerate** (det g = −1), so **no measure-collapse — not the finite-curvature species**; and it is **VSI** (all polynomial curvature invariants vanish), so **not the infinite-curvature (terminating) species either.** **It is a regular radiative boundary.**
    - **The Type-N field *does* carry a Killing vector** — its covariantly-constant, fourfold-degenerate principal null direction, *"the characteristic of ordinary wave propagation"* — **but that direction is null *everywhere*: it makes no non-null-to-null transition, forms no Killing horizon, and produces no measure-collapse.**
    - **GUARD (§149, §169): substrate isotropy vs the geometry's own isometry part company at the wall.** The *"isotropy → 0"* that marks the wall is **the cut-fixing *substrate* isotropy**, which vanishes there — **not the wall geometry's own isometry, which as a plane wave is large.** *"The two coincide on the symmetry-reducible sector and part only at the wall."* [P11 prop:wall]
12. **COSMOGENESIS DOES NOT LIVE AT THE WALL.** *"The Null-Boundary Correspondence re-founds a clock by reassigning the freed null direction of a **degenerating** Killing field; **the wall has no degenerating Killing field** — its null Killing vector is null throughout, never crossing from non-null to null — **so the cosmogenesis mechanism has nothing to act on there.** Cosmogenesis lives at the branch point $r=0$ (r2234: NOT a finite-curvature locus) — the cosmological/black-hole horizon and the Nariai seam — **and not at the wall**."*
    **The wall is instead the slicing operator's *generative boundary*** — *"the locus where generation-by-symmetry, the sweep of a substrate isometry, runs out and hands off to ordinary inhomogeneous evolution."* **"'Why the cut bends past the wall' is therefore a genuinely different question from cosmogenesis"** — free, fully inhomogeneous gravitational dynamics, with no residual symmetry to confine it. [P11 §wall; → §1e, §1m, P16]
13. **THE CHIRALITY CRITERION, COMPUTED.** Resolve the wall's harmonic profile into the two polarizations: H = h₊(u)(x²−y²) + 2h_×(u)xy, **the Ricci tensor vanishing for *any* h₊,h_×** — the two polarizations independent and unconstrained. *(The trace part of H would be the matter bend, absent in vacuum.)* The reflection x↦−x sends h_×↦−h_×: **the helicity-flipping parity**, a symmetry of the wave **iff h_×=0**. A transverse rotation by θ acts as the **spin-2** phase e^{−2iθ} on h₊+i h_×, with polarization angle ½ arg(h₊+i h_×).
    > **"CHIRALITY IS THE TURNING OF THE POLARIZATION PLANE"** — the handedness being **the ℤ₂ sign of d/du arg(h₊+i h_×)**, left- versus right-circular, **helicity ±2**.
    
    If the polarization is **fixed** (h_×=0, or any constant ratio — a linear polarization along a fixed, possibly tilted axis) **that axis furnishes a fixed reflection symmetry, parity identifies the two helicities, and there is no genuine chirality.** If the polarization **turns**, no fixed axis is a reflection symmetry for all u, no isometry identifies the helicities, and **the radiation is genuinely chiral.**
14. **WHERE CHIRALITY ONSETS — and it is *not* the wall (P11 sharpens P9).** *"The criterion therefore bites **not only at the wall** but **from the loss of that swept SO(3) onward**: the polarized Gowdy edge (residual T², one fixed axis) is **still achiral**, **the unpolarized *turning* wave is the first chiral case**, and the wall (no residual isometry) is **where chirality is *generic***."* The reflection x↦−x is *"the radiative descendant of the slicing reflection that fixes a comoving worldline in the symmetric sector; there the swept SO(3) completes it to an orientation-preserving rotation and rotates it away, so the two handednesses are **identified — a mirror, not a chirality**."* **P9's "the wall is the onset of chirality" is the coarser statement; qualified at r915 and cited to P11.** [P11 §chirality; → §1m]
15. **The handedness rides the *disconnected* parity.** It is *"the orientation parity of the substrate — the reflection lying **outside the connected isometry group** that sweeps the symmetric sector — surfacing in the radiating sector as the graviton's two helicities."* **"That this handedness rides the *disconnected* orientation parity, outside any connected-group action, is also why it lies beyond the reach of the matter-sector index obstruction that renders a connected-gauge fermion spectrum vector-like: the gravitational sector is chiral precisely through the component that obstruction cannot touch."** Same ℤ₂ as the A₂ diagram automorphism P (the dS↔Schwarzschild vantage-swap), and **the same ℤ₂ that acts on the cut's fermion as γ⁵ — "here read dynamically."** It is **the gauged factor** of D₆ (a substrate isometry), while the Weyl S₃ is the deck symmetry and no isometry — **"gauged chirality with global flavour is the Standard Model's own arrangement, following from which of the two factors is an isometry."** [P11 §chirality; → §1i, §1h, §1j, P13]
16. **THE WALL CARRIES NO DISCRETE MARKER.** The special strata are marked by **distinct** discrete operations: the **Nariai seam** = two horizon roots collide (a fixed point of the root permutation); the **cosmogenesis horizon** = the null↔timelike reassignment; the **Riemannian↔Lorentzian seam** = the signature flip. **"The wall is the one boundary carrying no such discrete marker — no colliding roots, no degenerating Killing field, no measure-collapse — consistent with its being purely the end of continuous generation."** **"These markers are distinct operations, not one; they relate only through the strata they mark."** [P11 §discrete; → §1i, §1f]

**What it roots / joints (both ways):**
- **Answers P8's "emergence of the bend"** — *"the deepest open question the construction raises."* [→ §1l]
- **Supplies the chirality criterion P9 defers to it**, and **sharpens P9's onset claim**. [↔ §1m]
- **The field-theoretic realization of P10's deparametrization** — *"the planar Gowdy model of the present paper is the companion field-theoretic realization in the symmetry-reducible sector."* [↔ §1n]
- **Isolates any quantum forcing to the *discrete* root structure** — P12's object, not the continuous dynamics. [→ §1h]
- **The handedness is the disconnected parity the index obstruction cannot reach.** [→ P13, §1i, §1j]

**The guard ([†ONT-DYN]).**
> **The bend's dynamics is a TRUE-HAMILTONIAN FLOW** on the substrate's cosmic foliation. **ENERGY and MOMENTUM are the ADM constraints, not evolution equations** — the wave's energy and momentum are carried **entirely by the shear of the leaf**.
> **Λ>0 forces the clock** — the internal area clock is *inconsistent* there; the substrate's cosmic time replaces it **positively**, not by elimination. *"It earns that necessity precisely where an internal clock breaks down."*
> **The graviton is EXACTLY MASSLESS at the gauge-invariant level.** The 6H² of the fixed-background truncation is **a gauge artifact**. And the sector is **ghost-free to all orders** (positive-definite reduced energy), with the shear charge conserved in the **full inhomogeneous** model.
> **Do not report the nonlinear resonance as open.** *"Energy is the wrong instrument"* — no total energy can be monotone, because the total field energy carries the background's own expanding energy. The question **is** the future stability of de Sitter, **settled** (Friedrich; Andréasson–Ringström; Beyer's Nariai exception = the range paper's no-pivot seam). **The dynamics ADMITS quantization; it does not FORCE it** — *any forcing must live in the **discrete** root structure, not the continuous evolution.*
> **THE WALL IS NOT A METRIC SINGULARITY of either species** — non-degenerate metric (no measure-collapse), VSI (no curvature blow-up). **A regular radiative boundary.** Its null Killing vector is **null everywhere**: no degenerating Killing field, hence **no NBC, hence NO COSMOGENESIS AT THE WALL.** *Cosmogenesis lives at the branch point $r=0$ (r2234: NOT a finite-curvature locus) — the horizon and the Nariai seam.* **Never conflate the wall with the seam.**
> **GUARD: the "isotropy → 0" at the wall is the *substrate* isotropy, not the geometry's own isometry** — the Type-N plane wave's own isometry group is **large**. The two coincide on the symmetry-reducible sector and **part only at the wall**.
> **CHIRALITY IS THE TURNING OF THE POLARIZATION PLANE** (the ℤ₂ sign of d/du arg(h₊+ih_×), helicity ±2). It onsets **at the loss of the swept SO(3), not at the wall**: a pinned polarization is **achiral** (a mirror, not a chirality); **the unpolarized turning wave is the first chiral case**; the wall is where chirality is **generic**. The handedness rides the **disconnected** orientation parity — *precisely the component the index obstruction cannot touch.*
> **The wall carries no discrete marker.** The seam's markers (colliding roots, the null↔timelike reassignment, the signature flip) are **distinct operations, not one.**
> **[closure — r1096]** §sec:discrete now reads one further structure off the **cosmogenesis marker**: composed with `τ̃↦τ̄̃`, the orientation parity `R=γ⁵` (graded here as the graviton's handedness) is the **geometric factor** of charge conjugation's kinematic closure, `C=(Q↦−Q)_field∘(R∘K)_geometric` — the same `r=0` crossing that re-founds the clock, read in the matter sector rather than the radiative one. [→ glossary §C·factorization; §1p]

**Altitude:** exact and Hamiltonian **in the regime worked** — the confined Gowdy–de Sitter wave at the Type-I edge. Three things **explicitly not claimed**: a closed-form nonlinear solution or a **full non-perturbative quantization** (*"neither is built here, and the latter is not needed for the admissibility verdict"*); the dynamics **strictly beyond the wall** (*"ordinary free evolution, not a stratum-crossing the construction generates"*); and the closed-S³ lift (**done in P10**). The scalar (density) perturbation sector is P15's.

## 1p. The matter boundary — where the geometry's reach stops, and why fermion chirality is *forced* to be non-geometric (P13)

*Pinned r917 (Pass 2, in-order read — P13 had **no card and no stamp**, though it supplies the chirality mechanism P11/P12 defer to and the C/CPT closure p0 inherits). Dug at source (`boundary_paper.tex`, entire): §question; §setup (C2, C3, C7); §sigma; §cascade; §a2; §wall + prop:boundary + §115; §decoupling; §face-status; §open; §meaning. Stamped **[†ONT-BDRY]**. Stated for reversal.*

**THE REGISTER, FIXED AT THE OUTSET.** *"A negative result is as easy to overstate as a positive one."* **What is established is bounded:** colour does not arise as a continuous internal gauge symmetry through **any examined geometric-isometry route**. **What is not established, and is claimed nowhere:** *"the universal statement that no construction whatever could yield the Standard Model from this geometry."* **Colour by the ordinary route — an SU(3) matter bundle placed by hand — is in no jeopardy and is not the subject.**

**The chain, each link forced, with its home:**
1. **The three hard constraints.** *(C2)* **𝔰𝔲(3)⊄𝔰𝔬(5,1), structurally**: the smallest faithful real rep of 𝔰𝔲(3) is **six**-dimensional (**3** carried as ℝ⁶), so 𝔰𝔲(3)⊂𝔰𝔬(6) but **not** 𝔰𝔬(5) — and 𝔰𝔬(5) is the maximal compact subalgebra of 𝔰𝔬(5,1). *"Colour as a compact symmetry needs a six-dimensional real carrier; the Lorentzian substrate's compact sector supplies only five."* *(C3)* **Where 𝔰𝔲(3) *can* act geometrically, it acts *spatially*, not internally**: on a six-real-dimensional carrier it acts transitively on S⁵=SU(3)/SU(2) (the Hopf action S⁵→ℂP²), **admits no equivariant map to the cosmological S³**, so *"the cut to the cosmological slice breaks it. A would-be geometric colour is a symmetry of **space**, which the physical cut destroys, not an internal symmetry of the matter on the cut."* *(C7)* 𝔰𝔲(3) **lives only on the compact (Wick) face**, reached **off** the real Lorentzian substrate by a change of signature. [P13 §setup]
2. **The substrate is *not* a product M₄×K — and that is NO escape.** *"We flag this because it is a feature that does **not** do the work one might expect: the chirality obstruction turns on **compactness and continuous symmetry**, not on a product/Kaluza–Klein structure, so the absence of a product is **not by itself a reason any obstruction fails to apply**."* [P13 §setup, §wall]
3. **The σ-lift closes — and THREE OPERATIONS ARE CO-LOCALISED AT THE SEAM.** Computed in embedding coordinates rather than judged by family resemblance: the Wick rotation **complexifies the timelike coordinate**, is imaginary, and **changes the geometry**; **σ is a *real* Weyl(A₂) reflection** of the spatial sky/root plane which **fixes x₀, preserves both signatures, and fixes the manifold**. *"The clincher re-derives (C2): 𝔰𝔲(3) must use the 𝔰𝔬(6)∖𝔰𝔬(5) generators that the Wick rotation **creates** and that σ precisely **fixes**."*
   > **The three distinct operations at r=√(3/Λ):** the **real Weyl reflection σ** (fixes x₀, signature, geometry); the **seam continuation** θ↦π/2+iψ, which flips the signature of a *single slicing curve* and reaches the equatorial **S⁴, isometry SO(5)**; and the **global Wick rotation** x₀↦ix₀, which reaches **S⁵, isometry SO(6) ⊃ 𝔰𝔲(3)**. **"Only the last lands on the face where 𝔰𝔲(3) lives."** Reading σ as the bridge conflates the first with the third; **reading the seam continuation as the bridge conflates an SO(5) operation with an SO(6) one.** *"Co-localised… but not the same operation."* [P13 §sigma]
4. **The cascade drops colour at the real cut — and the rank count caps the raise.** Granting the raise to dS₆ (isometry SO(6,1) ⊃ compact SO(6) ⊃ SU(3)), the cut dS₆→dS₅ leaves SO(5,1) whose maximal compact is SO(5), **and SU(3)⊄SO(5)**. *"Colour is therefore **dropped** the instant one descends to the real substrate: 'the gauge structure is which isometry survives the real-substrate cut' returns **gravity, not gauge**."*
   **The rank count:** the SM gauge group has **rank 4**; SO(6) has **rank 3**; a subgroup's rank cannot exceed the group's, **so the weak SU(2) does not fit**, and the raise buys **at most SU(3)×U(1)**. *"Even granting the raise, the geometry does not deliver the Standard Model at dS₆; it delivers **at most colour-plus-a-U(1), and the rest is a tower of posits**."* [P13 §cascade]
5. **The A₂/S₃ skeleton is genuine — and discrete.** Geometrically forced, *"not generic cubic numerology, as a dedicated genericity check confirmed."* But it is **the discrete Weyl(A₂)=S₃ skeleton acting in the observer-vantage groupoid, permuting descriptions of one rigid geometry** — *"not the continuous Lie algebra 𝔰𝔲(3) acting on field content. **A genuine discrete shadow is not a continuous internal symmetry**, and reading the one as the other is the error §sigma diagnosed in coordinates."* [P13 §a2]
6. **THE CHIRALITY WALL — the fourth face, with fifty years behind it.** **Atiyah–Hirzebruch**: on a **compact, connected, even-dimensional spin manifold** carrying a non-trivial smooth action of a **compact connected Lie group by isometries**, the **equivariant index of the Dirac operator vanishes**; for a non-abelian compact group it is enforced a second way (Lawson–Yau: positive scalar curvature + Lichnerowicz). **"The load-bearing hypotheses are *compactness and a continuous isometry* — not a product or Kaluza–Klein structure."** Witten ran almost exactly this: *"gauge group reachable, **chirality the rock**."* And the corrected reading: *"The earlier reading that 'CR is not a product, so the obstruction does not apply' **removed a premise the theorem never used**."* The literature's escapes are **uniformly non-geometric**, and *"every one abandons the 'gauge group = isometry' premise."* [P13 §wall]
7. **THE NON-COMPACTNESS ESCAPE IS CLOSED — by localisation, and it is proof-level.** The escape presupposes a continuous 𝔰𝔲(3) acting **by isometry** on the non-compact substrate. But **𝔰𝔲(3) is not an isometry of the real substrate at all** (C2), so it acts only on the compact face (C7), **which *is* compact.**
   > **"Where 𝔰𝔲(3) acts as an isometry the manifold is compact, and where the manifold is non-compact 𝔰𝔲(3) is not an isometry."**
   
   *"Not because the AH index is shown to bite on a non-compact manifold (**it is not invoked here**), but because the route's own premise fails."* **This closure is proof-level**, resting on 𝔰𝔲(3)⊄𝔰𝔬(5,1). **Bounded to the geometric-isometry route**; colour placed by hand is untouched. [P13 §wall §107]
8. **THE MECHANISM — why chirality is *forced* to be non-geometric.** *"The two chiralities at play are not merely distinct objects; they occupy **complementary parts of the isometry group**, and seeing why turns the reconciliation into a mechanism."*
   - CR's **gravitational** handedness is the substrate's **orientation parity**: a single reflection in **O(5,1)∖SO₀(5,1)** — *a discrete datum lying **outside** the connected isometry group.*
   - **AH is a theorem about a compact *connected* Lie group**: *"a positive-dimensional connected group **contains a circle**, and **it is the circle action** that forces the equivariant Dirac index to vanish — **a discrete orientation parity is no such action and does not trigger it**."*
   - **"They live in complementary pieces of one group: the obstruction on the identity component SO₀(5,1) and its connected subgroups, the gravitational chirality on the disconnected complement."**
   - **"This is sharper than the mere absence of tension, for it says *where* geometric chirality can live at all — only in the discrete orientation parity, never in a connected-group isometry action."** The SM's chiral fermions are charged under a **connected** gauge group, so a geometric-isometry realization of their chirality **falls squarely under the obstruction's hypothesis and is rendered vector-like**.
   > **"Observed fermion chirality is therefore not merely *found* to be non-geometric but *forced* to be, by the very part of the group structure in which chirality can and cannot be carried."** *"The gravitational sector is chiral precisely through the component the matter obstruction cannot reach."* **"The conclusion of a mechanism, not only the report of a wall."** [P13 §115]
9. **COLOUR-CLOSURE RESTS ON THE CAUSAL STRUCTURE — the most secure layer.** It *"does not depend on any claim about the compact face."* The cosmogenesis is a **signature-preserving reassignment on the real Lorentzian substrate**; **the matter rides that real Lorentzian horn**, while 𝔰𝔲(3) lives **across the signature seam**. *"So 𝔰𝔲(3) is not an internal symmetry of the matter sector, and this holds **whatever one concludes about the ontological status of the compact face** — it is fixed by **where the matter is** and **where 𝔰𝔲(3) is**."* [P13 §decoupling]
10. **The compact face is real-by-construction, NOT a co-equal world — and the closure is ONTOLOGICAL, not structural.** Mathematically the compact SO(6) face and the Lorentzian SO(5,1) substrate are **co-equal real forms of one complex group SO(6,ℂ)**; *"nothing in the group theory privileges one."* What breaks it is **CR's criterion for what exists**: *"a thing exists only insofar as it endures… 'things do not exist atemporally' is oxymoronic."* The Wick-rotated S⁵ is **Riemannian: no timelike direction, no clock, no duration.** *"However maximally symmetric, it cannot be a co-equal **existent**; it is **real-by-construction** — a genuine real form, a faithful analytic continuation — **but not a second physical world**."*
    - **And the clock is *measured*.** *"The cosmic time this criterion turns on is not a metaphysical posit but an **empirical determination**"* (the CMB isotropy). **"To deny it, and thereby reopen the compact face as a co-equal timeless world, is to deny an observational result, not to decline a commitment."**
    - **Scope:** the closure *"does not deny the mathematical co-equality of the real forms, only the co-equality of **existents**"*, and **does not touch the universal.** [P13 §face-status]
11. **THE PARITY *GRADES*, IT DOES NOT *EXCHANGE*.** The orientation parity reflects **the cut-normal** — the transverse direction along which the offset r₀, *which **is** the mass*, carries one cut to the next — so *"while orientation-reversing on the five-dimensional substrate, it is orientation-**preserving** on the four-dimensional spacetime, **fixing all four spacetime legs** and reflecting only that one transverse leg, whose Clifford generator on the cut is **γ⁵ itself**."* **"It does not *exchange* the two chirality eigenspaces but *grades* them — the substrate's one orientation ℤ₂ is the operator that measures left against right."**
12. **Two routes, and the corrected reading.** **The direct route still fails**: *"the Standard Model's chirality **is** the differential gauge assignment (left and right in different representations), gauge-internal and non-geometric, **not a bare handedness carried apart from it**."* **The orbifold-type route reads *more openly* than a first pass had it**: *"because the parity acts as γ⁵ — **a chirality projector** — a discrete projection by it would yield a **chiral** spectrum, not the vector-like one an exchange would force, so the route is **not closed by the projection itself but *gated*** on the one prerequisite the substrate does not yet supply — a propagating spinor sector to project, **the fifth substrate direction being the non-compact slicing normal rather than a compactified circle**."*
    > **"What the discrete residue geometrically supplies is the chirality *arena together with its grading operator* γ⁵; what it does not supply is the chiral *content* — the unequal population of the two gradings — which remains the ordinary, gauge-differential route."** [P13 §open]
13. **The bilinears, and mass as the R-odd departure.** The substrate cut inherits the **unique spin structure** of dS₅≃ℝ×S⁴. **The kinetic term ψ̄γ^μ∇_μψ is R-*even*; the mass term ψ̄ψ is R-*odd*.** *"So the massless (chiral) fermion is precisely the **R-respecting** sector, and a fermion mass is the **breaking** of the substrate's one orientation parity."* And the loop closes: the same R is the geometric mass-reflection r₀↦−r₀, under which **2M is itself R-odd**. **"Mass, geometric or fermionic, is the R-odd datum of the substrate."**
    - **"The M=0 central cut is the one R fixes, and massless fermions are the ones γ⁵ fixes, so the R-symmetric sector is exactly the offset-free, massless vacuum, and mass (geometric M or fermionic m) is the R-odd *departure* from it."**
    - **But the R-odd fermion mass is NOT the cut's single geometric offset** — *"one cut carries one offset while the fermions on it carry many masses."* What the geometry carries is a **three-fold R-odd mass *structure*** (the cubic's three zero-sum, **mass-tied** roots that S₃ permutes). **The three families realise its *count* within CR (P14); whether their *masses* realise its three-fold splitting stays do-not-assert.**
    - **CR's R-structure does not constrain the breaking:** *"the electroweak transition is the ordinary thermal event, on which the substrate sets no scale, chirality, or epoch, and **the cosmogenesis places no parity-breaking event at all** — its seam being the Weyl-fixed Nariai crest, **a fixed point of the S₃ factor and not of R**."* [P13 §open §141]
14. **THE SECOND BOUNDARY: charge conjugation, and no geometric CPT.** At the cosmogenesis exit the substrate's discrete isometries are **three linear reflections generating ℤ₂×ℤ₂**: the **orientation parity R = γ⁵** (the cut-normal reflection, which ***grades*** chirality); **the areal-radius reflection r↦−r** — *"the signed radius is the spatial antipode, flipping all three spatial legs, so its Clifford generator **γ¹γ²γ³ anticommutes with γ⁵ and *flips* chirality where the cut-normal parity *grades* it**"*; and the **time reflection x₀↦−x₀**.
    - **Charge conjugation is not among them, and *cannot* be.** *"C on a Dirac field is **antilinear** — ψ^c = Cγ^{0⊤}ψ*, acting on the complex conjugate of the field, on its charge structure — whereas **every substrate isometry acts linearly**, ψ↦Mψ with M a real Clifford element."*
    - **And the reason is the bend** *(quote updated r1063 to the reframed §258).* *"The complex structure C acts on is the matter field's **charge**, which the substrate does not carry: the metric depends on the charge only through Q², so **Q↦−Q leaves it invariant — charge conjugation present in the geometry not as a blindness but as a *symmetry*, the even-face degeneracy of `prop:conjugation-parity`** — and it is the charge **sign**, the odd datum the metric does not carry, that lives entirely in the Maxwell potential, **a bend on the matter side and not a substrate datum**. **C is therefore field-level for the very reason charge is the bend.**"* One Q²-evenness serves **both** classical (Maxwell) and fermionic (Dirac) charge. **And the outer ℤ₂ of D₆ is R, not C** — `Q↦−Q` fixes the horizon roots, so C adjoins an independent ℤ₂, `D₆→D₆×ℤ₂` (`rem:C-not-R`). *(⚠ First-pass reading: "C is therefore field-level" is **enlarged** by the ✅ positive closure immediately below — C's kinematic face is geometric; only the charge **sign** is field-level. Read the two together.)*
    > **"A geometric CPT is not auto-yielded by the substrate's orientation structure; *PT* is geometric, *C* closes it from the matter field."** *"The substrate's geometry supplies the **orientation skeleton** of matter's discrete structure, and **not its charge**."* [P13 §147]
    - **✅ THE POSITIVE CLOSURE (r1088→r1092) — the boundary encloses a shape, and states it.** *Not-an-isometry is not not-geometric.* The antilinear reality involution **`τ̃↦τ̄̃`** on complexified cosmic time is antilinear **and geometric** (L2, complex-analytic — the Feynman–Stückelberg particle↔antiparticle relation, fixing the neutral photon axis, swapping the `r<0` wings), a face the isometry premise reaches neither to include nor to exclude. So the residue is **enlarged**, and charge conjugation **factorizes** (A3, worked on the full `ℂ_r × ℂ_τ̃`; receipt `A3_factorization.py`): **`C = (Q↦−Q)_field ∘ (R∘K)_geometric`** — the geometry supplies **all of `C`'s kinematic (CPT/FS) content**, only the electric-charge sign closes from the field. And `R∘K` **is the bead's own `r=0` crossing** (`R` the species/mass-sign flip across `r=0`, `K` the lap-conjugation), so *the cosmogenesis carries `C`'s kinematic face* — the drawn CPT↔cosmology connection (P13 `prop:conjugation-closure`, §sec:closure). **[6] resolved** to the *kinematic shadow* (not the full `C` — blind to `Q`). **Realized on P14's actual zero-modes** (r1092, receipt `P14_payoff.py`): `R` carries each generation to its bound opposite-chirality antimatter partner (`prop:wall`), `K` is the seam continuation P14 already uses — the conjecture's particle/antiparticle wings **are** the two ends of P14's own `R`-conjugation across the bead. **Bounded (do-not-assert):** the specific-charged-particle identification, the full antilinear `C`, and a geometric CPT stay external — the charge closes from the field. So *"a geometric CPT is not auto-yielded" still stands*; what is new is that `C`'s **kinematic** face is geometric, and it is the cosmogenesis. [↔ §1e, §1f; glossary §0 `C`; storyboard `D-Canat`]
15. **The multiplicity is where geometric content *did* enter.** Three things the geometry does **not** fix: **the individual mass values, the electroweak scale and assignment, and the cosmological breaking history** — *"and those stay the ordinary route; on them the boundary is sharp."* **"But the family *multiplicity* is not among them."** The mass-offset relation is the pure **triple-angle 2M=(2/3√3)sin 3w**, driven to that single-harmonic form by *"the **unique** slicing scale 2/√3 that the gnomonic image of the hole on the observer's sky fixes"* — **so the three roots are the three preimages of 3w under the sine, and the threefoldness is the triple-angle's own: the count is *forced* rather than fitted.** *"The door the cubic left open, the multiplicity of the fermion families, is exactly where a geometric content **did** enter"* (built in P14). And a **flavour breaking structure comes with the triplet**: fixing the mass **is** taking r₀ as one of the three roots, factorising the cubic as (r−r₀)(r²+r r₀+r₀²−1) — *"a **2+1 prying-apart forced by the slicing**, not a symmetry imposed and broken by hand."* **The triplet is degenerate in the mass *parameter* 2M but not in the roots themselves** (physically distinct horizons of differing surface gravities), *"so S₃ is a solution-space deck symmetry rather than a claim that the three coincide. A generation hierarchy is therefore **not forbidden** by the degeneracy; it is only not read off 2M."* [P13 §open §143, §145]

**✅ RESOLVED at r968 — CANON: R = mass-reflection/orientation/diagram-automorphism parity (=γ⁵); P = areal spatial parity r↦−r (γ¹γ²γ³); T = time. Forced by CPT-coherence. Applied corpus-wide (P3/P5/p0/matter/dynamics/shadow renamed; boundary/algebroid already canonical), verified clean. The historical collision note below is retained for provenance.**

> **THREE OPERATIONS ARE CO-LOCALISED AT THE SEAM — never fuse them.** σ (real Weyl reflection; fixes signature and geometry); **the seam continuation → equatorial S⁴, SO(5)**; **the global Wick rotation → S⁵, SO(6) ⊃ 𝔰𝔲(3)**. *"Only the last lands on the face where 𝔰𝔲(3) lives."*

> **The non-compactness escape is CLOSED, proof-level, by localisation** — *"where 𝔰𝔲(3) acts as an isometry the manifold is compact, and where the manifold is non-compact 𝔰𝔲(3) is not an isometry."* **The AH index is not invoked on a non-compact manifold.**
> **THE MECHANISM:** a positive-dimensional **connected** group contains a **circle**, and it is the **circle action** that kills the index; **a discrete orientation parity is no such action.** So gravitational chirality and the obstruction live in **complementary pieces of one group** — and **observed fermion chirality is *forced* to be non-geometric.** *"Geometric chirality can live only in the discrete orientation parity, never in a connected-group isometry action."*
> **The parity GRADES, it does not EXCHANGE** (γ⁵, orientation-*preserving* on the four-dimensional spacetime). Hence the **orbifold route is *gated*, not closed** — *"a discrete projection by a chirality projector would yield a **chiral** spectrum"* — gated on a propagating spinor sector, **the fifth direction being the non-compact slicing normal, not a compactified circle.** The geometry supplies **the chirality arena and its grading operator; not the chiral content.**
> **Colour-closure rests on the CAUSAL structure**, not on the wall — the matter rides the real Lorentzian horn; 𝔰𝔲(3) is across the signature seam. **This holds whatever the compact face's status.**
> **The compact face is real-by-construction, not a co-equal existent.** The closure is **ontological, not structural** — the real forms *are* mathematically co-equal. And **the clock is measured**: to reopen the face *"is to deny an observational result."*
> **CPT — THE CLOSURE STATE (r1088→r1102; supersedes the "C field-level, no geometric CPT" first pass).** The substrate carries **P, T, γ⁵** as *linear* reflections. **C is antilinear and no isometry — but not not-geometric:** the reality involution **`K:τ̃↦τ̄̃`** is antilinear *and* geometric (complex-analytic), so charge conjugation **factorises**, **`eq:C-factor`: `C = (Q↦−Q)_field ∘ (R∘K)_geometric`** (A3, worked on the full `ℂ_r×ℂ_τ̃`; `A3_factorization.py`). The geometry carries **all of C's kinematic (CPT/FS) content**; **only the electric-charge SIGN closes from the field** (the metric is `Q²`-even — `Q↦−Q` a *symmetry*, not an absence, `prop:conjugation-parity`). So a **full** geometric CPT is still **not auto-yielded** (the charge sign is external) — but its **kinematic (PT/FS) face IS geometric**, and it is the bead's `r=0` crossing (the CPT↔cosmology connection). **The operator/species bound:** the spinor lift `γ⁵S=−(Cγ⁰ᵀ)` implements `ψ↦ψᶜ` (`A3_spinor_lift.py`, `(γ⁵S)ψ*=−ψᶜ`) — that is the **operator** half, grounded; whether the two frequency wings are a **particle/antiparticle pair** (species half) is a real-slice question no operator identity reaches, **do-not-assert** (the pairing rests on P14's `prop:wall`, not the operator). **C is still not the outer ℤ₂ of D₆** (that is R, which moves the roots); `Q↦−Q` fixes the roots, so C adjoins an independent ℤ₂, `D₆→D₆×ℤ₂` (`rem:C-not-R`). **Why R conjugates `𝟑↔𝟑̄`: `−1∉W(A₂)`** (negation outer, `A₂`-specific; `negation_outer_A2.py`). [↔ §0 `C` tiers+grid; §1e, §1f]

**Altitude:** *"The bounded negative is itself a result: **the honest perimeter of what one maximally symmetric Lorentzian substrate's geometry forces**."* **CR is a gravitational-cosmological unification** — *"one substrate, all the spherically symmetric stationary geometries, the dynamics up to the radiative wall. It is **not**, and on the evidence here does not claim to be, a geometric unification of the Standard Model."* The compact-face (gauge-acted) fermion sector the obstruction would act on **remains unbuilt**; what P14 built lives on the **discrete** component and supplies **no equivariant index**. ## 1q. The fermion sector — three chiral generations, and the leaf index (P14) — the boundary's one opening, built

*Pinned r918 (Pass 2, in-order read — P14 had **no card and no stamp**, though it is the corpus's newest and least-worked paper and P13 cites it **thirteen times**). Dug at source (`matter_sector_paper.tex`, entire — 114 lines, the shortest): §intro; §chirality + prop:wall; §count + prop:forced; §family; §cosmogenesis; §scope. Stamped **[†ONT-FERM]**. Stated for reversal.*

**IT BUILDS P13'S ONE OPENING.** P13 walls the gauge sector and names *"the substrate's **discrete** structure — the orientation parity and the threefold symmetry of the slicing — as the one opening through which matter might enter, carried as a conjecture."* **P14 takes it up concretely: put a Dirac field on the slicing curve and compute its zero-mode content.**

**DELIVERED vs NOT DELIVERED — stated at the outset:**
- **Delivered:** the generation **count** (three), the **chirality** of each (γ⁵), and the **family symmetry** (S₃). *"These are the discrete flavour structure."*
- **Not delivered:** the **gauge representations** (colour, weak isospin, hypercharge — *"the geometric route is walled; they remain the ordinary route, a bundle imposed by hand"*), and **the mass spectrum** (*"the zero-modes are massless, and their splitting is electroweak physics, external to the geometry"*).

**The chain, each link forced, with its home:**
1. **The chiral wall.** On the slicing curve **r is a *signed* coordinate**: the curve runs forward through the seam and backward through r=0 as **one closed object**, r=0 *"a branch point the field crosses smoothly, not a barrier."* The massless radial Dirac problem separates into a first-order pair with superpotential **W(r)=λ√f/r**, λ=j+½. **Because r is signed, W changes sign at r=0: a domain wall.** The zero-mode integrates to ψ=exp(−∫m dx′)χ₊, χ₊ the σ_y=+1 eigenspinor; for m=tanh(x/a) this is **ψ = cosh^{−a}(x/a) χ₊, an exact normalizable solution** — *the conjugate branch grows as cosh^{+a} and is rejected.* [P14 §chirality]
2. **THE NORM IS LOAD-BEARING — and it is the layered ontology doing physics.** *"The norm in which the mode is normalizable is the one CR's ontology selects, and the point is load-bearing."* The fermion is **a field on the evolving spatial leaf** — *the existent of the layered ontology, of which the spacetime is a projection* — so its norm is **the induced proper-distance measure on the cut, ∫|ψ|² dℓ with dℓ = dr/√|f|**.
   - **In the leaf measure:** the turning points (f=0, the horizons) lie at **finite** proper distance, and the r=0 crossing is an **integrable √-singularity** — *"so the bound state is genuinely normalizable."*
   - **In the conserved *spacetime* Dirac norm** (tortoise, dr_*=dr/f): **the same static mode is NOT normalizable** — *"the horizons sit at infinite tortoise distance, where the mode tends to a constant."*
   > **"The two are not interchangeable, and CR reads the fermion as a mode of the *existent leaf*, not a propagating spacetime field carrying the tortoise norm — on the leaf it is a bound state."**
   
   *"Direct solution of the exact zero-mode across a full undercritical slicing confirms both limits."* [P14 §52; ← §1e (the layered ontology)]
3. **prop:wall.** *"Each throat wall binds **exactly one** normalizable chiral zero-mode. Its chirality is a definite σ_y eigenvalue, whose sign is the sign of the signed-radius flip; **that operator is the diagram-automorphism parity R = γ⁵**."* **"This is the ℤ₂ (chirality) half of the substrate's discrete structure, realised as a bound state, *not posited*.** Reversing the wall reverses χ_±, flipping the chirality — **the parity *is* the chirality**, exactly as the algebroid paper reads it off the constraint algebra."* [P14 prop:wall; ← §1h]
4. **THREE WALLS — the geometry.** **r=0 is *not* the throat's centre but a point *on* the throat circle** — the back, X₁=−α, **fixed relative to the hinge about which the slicing plane swings.** The substrate carries **three such hinges, 120° apart at transverse distance 2α, of which the throat circle is the incircle.** **The ℤ₃-fixed centre carries no wall**; the walls lie on the circle at **polar 180°, 300°, 60°** — a **ℤ₃-orbit**, each antipodal to its hinge. [P14 §count §60; ← §1f]
5. **prop:forced — the three-plane construction is forced *within CR*.** Two constructions are geometrically available: **one plane on one chosen hinge, or one plane on each.** *"They are not on equal footing."*
   > **"A one-plane construction must select *which* hinge to build on: a free modulus, unfixed by the geometry, an arbitrary choice among three ℤ₃-equivalent options. The ℤ₃-symmetric three-plane construction is the unique configuration carrying no such modulus — the symmetric point, pinned by the symmetry."**
   
   **A construction carrying an unfixed arbitrary modulus is exactly what least-arbitrariness excludes.** *(This is P6's Rule 2 in the ontological register, invoked by name.)*
   - **The two legs are in fact ONE.** The count rests on (i) the maximal-symmetry principle and (ii) that fermion generations are set by the matter construction at all — but *"given the three-hinge leaf, the three throat walls are **distinct loci**, so the wall-bound zero-modes have **disjoint support and span a three-dimensional space — a flavour triplet, three physical states in any basis, not one state redescribed** — so leg (ii) follows from leg (i) rather than standing beside it."*
   - **THE HONEST EDGE, stated plainly by the paper.** *"'Forced within CR' is thus forced by that **vindicated criterion**, not an added axiom; **the honest edge is that a framework declining the criterion reads the natural single-hinge index — one — not three.**"* And earlier: *"It is not a theorem a framework lacking that principle must accept… **and we do not inflate it**."* [P14 prop:forced, §76; ← §1g]
6. **THE COUNT IS A WELL-DEFINED INDEX — and *why* it is, exactly where the bulk index is not.**
   - **The leaf is COMPACT.** *"In the leaf's proper measure dℓ=dr/√|f| the closed slicing has **finite total length** — the horizon turning points at finite proper distance, the r=0 crossing an integrable √-singularity — **so the leaf is compact and the Dirac operator on it carries a well-defined analytical index dim ker, exactly where the bulk index on the non-compact spacetime (tortoise measure) is obstructed**."*
   - **The even-crossing constraint does not apply.** The signed-radius flip is **through the r=0 *branch point*, rather than a single-valued crossing on a loop** — *"so the even-crossing constraint that binds a single-valued sign function on a circle **does not apply**, and the three same-chirality modes **on disjoint support have no cancelling partner**."*
   - **dim ker₊ = 3, dim ker₋ = 0.** *"The count is the **net chirality: a γ⁵-graded index, protected under any deformation that preserves the three-wall structure**."*
   > **"It is a *leaf* (vantage) index, which is why it is well-defined precisely where the spacetime bulk index is not."**
   
   **This is the direct answer to P13's non-compactness escape, from the other side:** *"the count is a **wall-localized zero-mode index, not a bulk dS₅ index**, and localized modes are indifferent to the bulk's non-compactness."* [P14 §78, §scope; ↔ §1p]
7. **The family symmetry: D₆ = S₃ × ℤ₂.** The three walls are permuted by the **hinge triangle's symmetry**: the ℤ₃ rotation cycles them (180→300→60); **each of the three reflections — across the axis through one wall — swaps the other two and fixes it.** The three transpositions and the three-cycle generate **the full S₃**. With prop:wall's chirality ℤ₂: **D₆ = S₃ × ℤ₂ (family × chirality) = Aut(A₂).**
   - **Global vs gauged is "not a choice made here but the *kind* the discrete structure fixes":** the Weyl S₃ is the **deck/monodromy symmetry of the solution space and no substrate isometry**; the orientation parity ℤ₂ **is** an isometry, acting on the cut spinor as γ⁵ — *"so chirality descends **gauged** and flavour **global**, the Standard Model's own arrangement following from **which factor is an isometry**."*
   - **Why they are *generations*:** *"Each plane carries the same three horizon **values**, so the three generations are **identical in content**, distinguished only by their wall; this is why the family symmetry is a symmetry of **identical copies** — the defining property of Standard-Model generations."* [P14 §family; ← §1i, §1h]
8. **THE DEGENERACY IS FORCED — and there is no asymmetric handle in the mass parameter.** *"The three shared horizons are roots of one cubic r³−r+2M=0, and **every root, designated the slicing parameter, returns the *same* 2M=r₀−r₀³**"* (since r³=r−2M for each) — *"so the three carry **one mass parameter**: one object read three ways on the fully D₆-symmetric horizon locus, a diagonal line and a 45° tilted ellipse in the (r₀,r)-plane."*
   - On that locus, **the 2+1 of a designated root against the ellipse pair is the diagonal designation σ**, and **the sign 2+1 (two horizons of one sign against one) is the reflection through the origin** (2M↦−2M, the chirality parity) — **"each a description structure paired with its symmetric twin — so *no asymmetric handle sits in the mass parameter*."** *(This is the partial-symmetry-flatten guard, written into the physics.)*
   - *"The locus **does** carry a flavour-**breaking structure** (the slicing singles one root against the ellipse pair), held **do-not-assert**; but the mass **values** are the ordinary electroweak route."* [P14 §family; → §1p link 15]
9. **THE COSMOGENESIS OF THE GENERATIONS — three features, each read off established structure.**

- **§network — the cooling leg IS a standard BBN.** **Y_p≃0.249 (obs 0.245), D/H≃2.5×10⁻⁵ (obs 2.5×10⁻⁵)** with one inherited datum (the η-analogue, the same that fixes the peaks); **deuterium un-tuned** (the freeze-out T the data demands = the bottleneck the standard rate delivers). **Li-7 the standard 3× over-prediction — the lithium problem, SHARED with ΛCDM.** **Corrects the cosmology paper's earlier "inherited / Li dissolved" reading** — the concrete thermal history **forces synthesis**. ## 1t. The figure programme — the overhead IS the light cone, and what classical geometry can see of it (P3 §hinge-geometry + p0/17 §power; the ledger)

*Placed r1113. **Home:** the load-bearing half is now **in the corpus** (P3 `prop:twoalpha`; p0/17 `§sec:power`, `prop:tangentnull`, `rem:onecircle`); the catalogue, the receipts, and the failures that drew the boundary live in **`FIGURE_THEOREM_LEDGER.md`** with **`storyboard_receipts/`**. This card exists because that ledger was an **orphan** — reachable only from a changelog — and per `CODA_FIELD_NOTE` r1090 a live document nothing points at is silently rebuilt. Read this card before touching the overhead figure, the hinge's placement, or any classical-geometry reading of the substrate.*

**THE ONE FACT, and everything else is it re-read.**
> **The power of a point w.r.t. the throat IS the square of its height in the embedding.**
> `pow(P) = |X|² − α²` (Steiner's 1826 invariant) **and** `|X|² − α² = X₀²` (the hyperboloid). Same combination, twice.

⟹ **`prop:tangentnull` (p0/17):** the tangent from any point of the substrate to the throat is a **null line** of the embedding — *Euclid's tangent–secant relation and the null condition are the same equation.* Two-line proof, both classical; verified at 600 heights, `max|ds²| = 5.7e-14`. **The rulings ARE those tangents**, so p0's *"doubly ruled by straight null lines"* and *"every tangent to the throat is null"* are **one statement**; the classical register adds **reach** (every point of the overhead, not only the ones a ruling passes through).
⟹ **The overhead figure is not a diagram of the geometry. It is the hinge's light cone, drawn.** *"It's all optics" is not a metaphor.*
⚠ **BOUNDED:** the sightlines that **touch** are light rays; the ones that **cut** are not. And that is Euclid's own — the power theorem is about **secants**, the null condition is its degenerate case where the two points merge, **and the tangent is exactly where the light cone is.**

**⊢ THE LAW — `rem:onecircle` (p0/17), and it took three failures to cut it.**
> **`power = X₀²` is NOT a fact about powers. It is a fact about ONE circle: the substrate's waist.**
> **The protocol translates exactly what is built from the power of a point w.r.t. the throat. There is one privileged circle and it is blind to every other.**

The law was *found by running the catalogue*, not posited, and each failure cut it finer: **Ptolemy** (⊢47) → a theorem translates only if its **points** span heights · **La Hire** (⊢55) → not the points, whether the theorem's **quantity** is one the height can enter · **Casey** (⊢58) → not *any* power — **the power w.r.t. the throat.** *A chord, a bilinear relation among points, or a power w.r.t. another circle carries no height.* **The boundary is not a defect: it is the statement that the substrate has exactly one waist, met in the classical language.**

**⚠ TWO MODERN INGREDIENTS, NOT ONE — and the historiography was corrected at source (r1112).** The ledger had said the protocol's theorems *"were proved 2300 years ago."* **Wrong by two centuries.** Of the outputs, only the tangent–secant and pole–polar are ancient — **and the only ancient theorem that FAILED is the chord one (Ptolemy).** The rest is 19th-c: radical axis **Steiner 1826**; inversion 19th-c; nine-point **Brianchon–Poncelet 1821 / Feuerbach 1822**; Casey **1866/1881** — and **in Wasan ~30 years earlier (Chochu Siraisi, 1830)**, which is *the tradition gap Daryl named, located*. **And ⊢9's *power* is Steiner's invariant, not Euclid's relation:** Euclid proves a relation among segments; **Steiner shifted the focus from the chords to the POINT.** `pow = X₀²` needs the invariant. *That second move **is the corpus's own** — the same move as reading `r₀` as the offset that IS the mass.*

**WHAT IS IN THE CORPUS (baked r1113):**
- **P3 `prop:twoalpha`** (§hinge-geometry) — **the distance 2α is not chosen.** The hole determines exactly one circle without further choice (its own edge through its own centre); that circle **ends** at 2α. Five equivalences: `R=2α` ⟺ the 60° subtense ⟺ midpoint-tangency ⟺ **the nine-point circle IS the throat** (Euler's `R/2`) ⟺ tangent length = height = `√3α`. *The point is not any one of them but their number.* Then **`eq:tripleathinge`: the placement FORCES Nariai** by the triple angle alone (`sin w=½ ⟹ sin 3w=1`), and the chain `α → 2α → sin w=½ → sin 3w=1 → w=360/(3×4)=30°` **ends on P3's own twelve designations.** *This answers a question P3 had left standing.*
- **p0/17 `§sec:power`** — the identity, `prop:tangentnull`, `rem:onecircle`, and both modern ingredients. Refs **Steiner 1826**, **Euclid III.36**.

**WHAT IS NOT BAKED, and lives in the ledger** (58 statements, 25 receipts, all running): the protocol *as a protocol*; the construction's other entries (**the polar of a vantage IS its horizon**, by *three* independent routes — chord of contact, radical axis, inversive image; **the Thales circle IS the dial**; **τ is a light-path** while **σ is a monodromy**, so *the rulings realise exactly the part of D₃ that is a substrate isometry*); the rhymes; **R5 — the waves**, do-not-assert, Daryl's, unassessed.

**The guard.**
> **The protocol is a SECOND ROUTE into physics already there, not new physics.** That is why it kept landing on things the corpus knew — Nariai, the horizon, the perspectival thesis — and it is what a correct dictionary does. Do not read a re-derivation as a discovery.
> **The failures are half the value and are kept, not tidied.** Ptolemy, La Hire and Casey each *hold* on the figure and return nothing; each death cut the law finer. **A theorem that holds and says nothing is the boundary reporting itself.**
> **A count is not a map.** At Nariai there are two rulings and two colliding roots; the match means nothing (different `2M`, different spaces, one a motion and one a monodromy).
> **`√15 α` is furniture.** The excentres are real substrate points far above the hinges. A new surd on a real point is the exact shape of a flavour-match: it is `|X|=4α=2R` through the hyperboloid, and `R=2α` is the hinge. **A surd on a real point is furniture until something reads it.**
> **Sampling cannot prove an absence** (`CODA_FIELD_NOTE` face 37, earned here): a sweep found "no crossing" at 0.004 when the discretisation was 0.008. If the question is algebraic, answer it algebraically.

**What it roots / joints (both ways):** rests on **P3** (the hinge, the dial, `prop:triple`, the gnomonic projection) and **p0/17** (the substrate's null structure — P3's abstract already defers it there: *"the substrate-level ontology and the c–Λ null-ruling gauge are developed in the companion geometric-core paper; here they ground the construction"* — **which is the line the material split along, and it is the corpus's own, not one invented for it**). Joints to **P5** (τ and σ; `prop:monodromy` is what makes "is σ a light-path" **ill-posed**), **P7** (`lem:twoturnings` — untouched by `A=∛2·ρ`, which is a ratio between two *radii*, not an identification of the two ℤ₃ *symmetries*), and **`THE_PLAN` Lane 7**. [↔ §1f, §1i, §1j]

**★ DOWNSTREAM, BAKED r1115 — where the corpus now carries it.** The survey was run at source, paper by paper, and only where a real locus existed:
- **P5 `rem:global`** (the family symmetry is global, not gauged) — **the same partition reached from the null side, mentioning neither spinors nor the cover:** `τ` is two steps along a null ruling (a hinge-transpose *is* a dial turn), so **τ is a closed path of light and `τ³=id` is its closure**; `σ` admits no such path, being by `prop:monodromy` a **loop in the complex `2M`-plane** — and `2M` labels the family rather than pointing along the manifold. *A ruling joins two points of one manifold; σ joins two **descriptions** of one geometry.* ⟹ **the rulings realise exactly that part of D₃ which is a substrate isometry, and nothing else.**
- **P12 §discrete** (the two factors descend by *different kind*) — the same, by a route mentioning neither spinors nor `su(3)`.
- **P13** (the two `D₆` factors, only the `ℤ₂` an isometry) — **the gauged/global split is legible in the light cone before any Dirac field is put on the cut.**
- **P14 §gen** (the three hinges at 2α) — **that distance is not a stipulation the fermion construction inherits**; the three walls stand on a triangle **the hole placed**, and the `ℤ₃` permuting them is **the hole's own three-foldness**, not a choice made there.
- **P8 §dictionary** (*"the horizon locus comes out a conic: it is the shadow of a planar cut of a quadric"*) — **the conic the horizon locus traces and the cone the cuts are read against are the same quadric seen twice.**
- **Not touched, and the restraint is the result:** P1, P2, P4, P6, P7, P9, P10, P11, P15, P16 — no locus the material bears on. *Adding to them would be decoration.*
- **Full corpus recompiled: 17/17, 0 undefined.**


## 1u. The combinatorics programme — the corpus's numbers are TWO families, and the boundary between them is the Standard Model's arrangement (Lane 8; the ledger)

*Placed r1167. **Home:** the load-bearing half is **in the corpus** — p0/17 `§unification`'s **seventh face** (the join, r1157) and its abstract; **P14 `§sec:twofactors`** (r1161, the citable home — where both factors become matter); **P12** (the direct product, and the kind-sorting read off the light cone); **P3 `§hinge-geometry`** (both geometric legs, r1164); **P13 `§sec:a2`** (r1167 — the closed route and the opening are the two factors). The audit itself, its receipts, and the splits that cut the law live in **`COMBINATORICS_LEDGER.md`** with **`storyboard_receipts/L8_*.py`**. This card is the index; the ledger is the record.*

**⌗ WHAT IT IS.** Daryl asked (r1138) for *"a perfect merging of geometry and combinatorics, the entire structure falling from one gauge that breaks symmetries in a cascading way."* **The lane returned: the merging is real and is ONE CIRCLE; the falling-from-one-gauge is real and is THE CHAIN; the cascade is NOT — it is a SORTING, and the sorting is the Standard Model's own arrangement.**

**⊢ L8.0 · THE DISCRIMINATOR (r1145), and it is why the rest can be trusted.** *A structural identity requires a **DERIVATION** — one object from whose own equation both quantities follow with no new input. A shared abstract type requires a **PROOF OF INEQUIVALENCE**. Neither ⟹ **UNDECIDED**, and undecided is honest.* **The asymmetry that makes it a law: a NO must be EARNED** — *"no link found"* is UNDECIDED, and reading it as *"two numbers"* rigs the question sceptically exactly as *"it's all one"* rigs it credulously. **★ The indexing rule: the verdict belongs to the CLAIM, not the pair** — the same two turnings return **NO** to *"the two ℤ₃'s are one symmetry"* (proved) and **OPEN** to *"there is a metric ratio between the radii"* (`A = ∛2·ρ`), simultaneously, both honest.

**⊢ L8.1 · THE NUMBER AUDIT — CLOSED (r1155). 6 welds · 9 proved splits · 2 dependent · 1 undecided.** **It did not return "it's all one."** Welds: **the 3** (roots = hinges = walls = generations; the object is **the dial**, and the designation is the **sine** — `sin(3(w+120°k)) = sin 3w`, so three dial positions solve one cubic) · **the 12** (`3×4`, both factors forced by one route) · **the 30** · **the √3** · **the ∛2** · **the 2**. Splits: **the 120** (*an arc split is a 2 wearing a 3's clothes*) · **the 2:1 lift** (*sin² peaks at half its own period; no cubic in it*) · **`2α` vs `−2ρ`** (a doubling vs a multiplicity compensation; `2α` exists for every M, `−2ρ` only at Nariai) · six more.

**★★ AND THE TALLY RE-DERIVED THE RESIDUE FROM THE NUMERALS.** Every weld ran through one of exactly **two** objects: **the triple-angle map / the sine** (2M-**fixing**, root-permuting — the 3, the 12, the 30, the √3, the ∛2) or **the matrix `R`** (2M-**flipping**, ruling-swapping — the 2). **Those are the two factors of `Aut(A₂) = S₃×ℤ₂`** — **P12's theorem, reached from the other side, by numbers never told to look for it. And the nine NOs are exactly the cross-factor pairs.** ⟹ ***numbers weld WITHIN a factor and split ACROSS it***, which is **why the audit could not return "it's all one": a direct product is precisely a structure that is NOT one thing.**

**⊢ L8.2 · THE SORTING, not a cascade (r1156).** **A chain cannot break what it never had:** of `D₆ = S₃×ℤ₂`, **only the `ℤ₂` is a substrate isometry** (the parity `R`, acting on the cut spinor as `γ⁵`), so **chirality descends gauged**; the **`S₃` is the deck symmetry of the solution space and NO isometry** (`su(3)⊄so(5,1)`, P13), so **flavour is global**. *The `S₃` was never a substrate symmetry, so nothing broke it* — a **derivation chain**, not a symmetry-breaking cascade. *(The corpus's one actual cascade is P13 §cascade's **dimensional descent** — same word, a different object: the audit's own recurring NO, arriving at the level of the lane's own name.)* **ONE GAUGE (α) → ONE GROUP (`D₆`, via the sine and via `R`) → ONE QUESTION asked of each factor (*is it a substrate isometry?*) → the SM's gauged/global arrangement.** **The sorting is the stronger claim:** a cascade needs a sequence and each stage's breaking argued; the sorting needs **one group and one question**.

**★★★ ⊢ L8.3 · THE MARRIAGE (r1157) — THE PROGRAMME'S MAIN RESULT.**
> **The Standard Model's arrangement of a gauged chirality against a global flavour is the difference between being ON the one circle and being TANGENT to it.**

**The three roots are the points ON the throat circle** (P3: *"these three special points are the geometric counterparts of the three roots"*); **the two rulings are the lines TANGENT to it** (p0: *"the rulings ARE those tangents"*); **`S₃` permutes what is ON it, `ℤ₂` swaps what is TANGENT to it** (P12: *"the two factors act on independent structures"*). **The residue factorises because *on* and *tangent* are independent relations.** **And the kind-sorting falls out of the same distinction:** a **ruling is a line OF the substrate**, so exchanging the pair is a **motion** of it — an isometry, acting as `γ⁵`: **chirality gauged**. A **root labels a different CUT**, so permuting the triple moves through the **solution space** — no motion, the deck symmetry: **flavour global**.

**⛔ THE GUARD, and it is the lane's real verdict — this is NOT "it's all one."** **Its entire content is a DIRECT PRODUCT**: two independent relations to one circle, two factors, **two kinds**. L8.1 found the numbers **weld within a factor and split across**; L8.2 found the factors **differ in kind**; L8.3 found the factors **are the circle's two relations**. ***Each step made the structure LESS one thing and MORE explained.*** **A ledger that had merged on family resemblance would have had none of it.**

**⚠ AND THE CAUTION THE CORPUS SUPPLIES AGAINST ITS OWN STATEMENT (P13 §wall, reached independently; = L8.1-d's *check the group, not the generators*).** *"Every orientation-reversing isometry swaps the two rulings, so the exchange distinguishes nothing."* ⟹ **L8.3 is a statement about the GROUP acting on the two structures — never a characterisation of `R` by the ruling-swap.** The distinction is not *which factor swaps* but **what the swapped structure IS**: a line of the substrate, or a label on a cut.

### Tier C — Conceptual geometric-boundary constraints (the essays)  [deep-read complete: Arc 1, c18; verified at source, c19]
*These constrain the geometric boundaries conceptually — what the geometry is allowed to mean. All now
`[in-bundle]` at `resources/conceptual-boundary-essays/`; deep reads recorded in `ONTOLOGY_DEEP_READ_NOTES.md` (Arc 1).*
- **5D Trope** — `conceptual-boundary-essays/five-dimensional-trope.md` **[in-bundle]**
  - *Establishes:* existence ≠ occurrence (things *exist*/endure; events *happen*/are instantaneous); existence is
    what the clock measures; reifying the 4-manifold smuggles a fifth dimension — a Newtonian meta-time / meta-now —
    needed only to make the block "exist." The conceptual root of the **meta-lapse** the lapse/shift proposition
    formalizes (P7 §Ontological Simultaneity).
- **Space-Time Doesn't Exist** — `conceptual-boundary-essays/space-time-doesnt-exist.md` **[in-bundle]**
  - *Establishes:* space-time is the map of happenings, never the existent — the conceptual root of P7's "the
    evolving layer is what exists, the manifold its record." Physics has no existence-variable for space-time; the
    philosophy-of-time debate smuggles event-existence at the outset.
- **Wild Goose Chase** — `conceptual-boundary-essays/wild-goose-chase.md` **[in-bundle]**
  - *Establishes:* the relativity of synchrony is **general** (geometry + kinematics of relative motion, not the
    light postulate — which only fixes the numbers) — grounds **shift = relativity of synchrony** in the lapse/shift
    proposition. Synchrony relative, simultaneity absolute; the modal-fallacy argument (no-local-detection ≠ no-fact);
    CMB isotropy + dipole as the empirical cosmic rest frame.
- **Did Einstein Misconstrue Relativity?** — `conceptual-boundary-essays/did-einstein-misconstrue-relativity.md` **[in-bundle]**
  - *Establishes:* the interpretive fork (all-relative vs. relativity-consistent-with-a-true-rest); relativity "only
    says a valid description *can* be given from any frame" — the representational-freedom reading. Uniform expansion
    (CMB, 1 part in 10⁵) → absolute rest + simultaneity; Einstein's equations flawless, his interpretation must shift.
    Co-grounds **shift = relativity** in the lapse/shift proposition.
- **Physicists and Philosophers Have Long Struggled** — `conceptual-boundary-essays/physicists-and-philosophers-long-struggled.md` **[in-bundle]**
  - *Establishes:* the 2500-year category error (Heraclitus → Parmenides → Zeno → eternalism) is the exist/happen
    conflation; cosmology resolves it (a 3D universe of existing things, events tracing worldlines = a model, not an
    existent). The map mistaken for the territory (Wells's Time Machine).
- **Is it Ethical to Ignore?** — `conceptual-boundary-essays/is-it-ethical-to-ignore.md` **[in-bundle]** *(the Tier-C synthesis AND the ethics hinge into Tier D)*
  - *Establishes:* maps the four-essay arc as one interlocking argument (5D-trope → space-time-doesn't-exist →
    physicists-and-philosophers → did-einstein-misconstrue); the two corrections as rules (events *happen* while
    things exist; "appear simultaneous" is local, no bearing on global simultaneity). The ethics argument — authority's
    obligation to engage a demonstrated correction — is the Tier-C→Tier-D hinge; pairs with ECC/PST.

## 3. The flag registry

Each flag marks a place a note deferred ontology to Daryl. The flag carries the **corrected wording**
plus a footnote marker **[†ONT‑N]** pointing here, where the **justification** is gathered paper-by-paper.

### FLAG ONT‑1 — RESOLVED / expunged (r269): the question was never open — P7 settles it by a named axiom.
- **Was (the bug):** the plan held open *where the individuation / hole-argument keystone sits — its own
  dynamic-half region vs threaded through the cosmology* — as if undecided, to be read off the backbone
  paper-by-paper. That treated as open a matter the corpus closes explicitly, which bred the dissonance
  (the reading notes kept saying the placement "waits on P1/P7" when P7 had already fixed it).
- **Settlement (at source):** P7 (`CR_framework.tex`), §"The Hole Argument and Event Identity"
  (`sec:CR-hole`), states the **Ontological Event Individuation** axiom — *events are individuated by
  their occurrence within the ontological spatial layers 𝒮_t, not by their coordinate representation in
  the spacetime manifold* — resolving the hole argument as representational redundancy, not ontological
  ambiguity (P3's rigidity supplies the no-moduli individuation the axiom rests on). So the individuation
  is **threaded through the cosmology**, established in P7's axiomatic foundation — **not** its own region.
- **Action:** the `[†ONT‑1]` marker is removed from `THE_PLAN.md:236`, which now states the settlement.
  **No open flags remain in this registry.**

*(Further flags appended here as found. The historical instances — past `gate_session_notes*` scope
questions and greenlights, and the `CORPUS_MAP` changelog — are **frozen records**, left as they are;
they record what happened, including the now-corrected framing.)*

---

## 4. The workflow

1. **Gather resources** (§2 status tags). Daryl supplies the `.tex` for what isn't public; the gate
   grabs the public cosmiCave / Conversation pieces and stuffs them in `resources/`.
2. **Read paper by paper.** Read one foundational piece fully, then **work through every flag**, adding
   how *that* piece justifies (or constrains) each — then move to the next piece. (Paper-major, not
   flag-major, so each piece is read once and applied across all flags while it is fresh.)
3. **Interrogate before adding.** Each justification entry is challenged on the merits — is it valid, is
   it load-bearing, does the piece actually establish it — before it goes in. Conservative and at weight:
   a thin or strained justification is marked thin, not dressed up.
4. **Reduce.** When the flags are filled, the foundation is the union of interrogated, load-bearing
   justifications — the formally reduced ontological core, requiring its pieces, with Daryl removed from
   the mechanism.

---

- **Flag-marker convention:** `[†ONT‑N]` at each site → this index's FLAG ONT‑N entry. Editable footnote:
  the wording and the justification list both grow in place.
- **Align `THE_PLAN.md`:36 to `CODA_FIELD_NOTE.md`** ("Whose call it is"): the field note already states
  the corrected framing; the plan should not contradict it. (FLAG ONT‑1 does this.)
- **`resources/` additions:** new sub-folders for Tier C (conceptual-boundary essays) and Tier D
  (epistemic-engine pieces), alongside `PhD_thesis/` and `Beyond-Space-Time/`; update `RESOURCES_README.md`
  to register the ontology-backbone arc.
- **Gate-report discipline:** the gate's own reports stop saying "the ontology is yours to set"; where an
  ontological placement is open, it is *read from the backbone or flagged for backbone-gathering*, not
  deferred to the orchestrator.
- **End state:** the ontological foundation is a named, interrogated set of load-bearing pieces — the
  formally reduced core — not an authority claim.
